\noindent\url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_abilities\_merge}}
getAccess grants exactly when some can rule allows and no cannot rule denies: in any Boolean algebra of abilities, can ⊓ cannotᶜ = ⊤ forces can = ⊤ and cannot = ⊥ (the meet is the merge of CanCanCan's rules). From Mathlib's `inf\_eq\_top\_iff` and `compl\_eq\_top`
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{BooleanAlgebra} \alpha ] (\mathrm{can}\ \mathrm{cannot} : \alpha ), \mathrm{can} \sqcap \mathrm{cannot}^{c} = \top \to \mathrm{can} = \top \land \mathrm{cannot} = \bot  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L758}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_abilities\_merge\_reflected}}
textbook\_abilities\_merge reflected by its shape
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{BooleanAlgebra} \alpha ] (\mathrm{can}\ \mathrm{cannot} : \alpha ), \neg (\mathrm{can} = \top \land \mathrm{cannot} = \bot ) \to \neg \mathrm{can} \sqcap \mathrm{cannot}^{c} = \top  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L762}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_acme\_resources\_five}}
every resource a client needs of an ACME directory is in it exactly when none of the needed is missing (RFC 8555 names five: newNonce, newAccount, newOrder, revokeCert, keyChange). From Mathlib's `Finset.sdiff\_eq\_empty\_iff\_subset` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{needed}\ \mathrm{directory} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \mathrm{needed} \setminus \mathrm{directory} = \emptyset \leftrightarrow \mathrm{needed} \subseteq \mathrm{directory} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2289}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_acme\_resources\_five\_reflected}}
textbook\_acme\_resources\_five reflected by its shape (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{needed}\ \mathrm{directory} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \mathrm{needed} \subseteq \mathrm{directory} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{needed} \setminus \mathrm{directory} = \emptyset  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2292}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_actions\_pinned}}
a commit or push is pinned exactly when the actions used by a movable ref u and the workflows without least-privilege permissions p sum to none, that is when both counts are none (the Scorecard checks Pinned-Dependencies and Token-Permissions). From Mathlib's `add\_eq\_zero` and `Unique.instSubsingleton` (examples: repository-actions-tag-pinned, repository-actions-sha-pinned)
\[ \forall (u\ p : \mathbb{N}), u + p = 0 \leftrightarrow u = 0 \land p = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L837}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_actions\_pinned\_reflected}}
textbook\_actions\_pinned reflected by its shape (examples: repository-actions-tag-pinned, repository-actions-sha-pinned)
\[ \forall (u\ p : \mathbb{N}), u = 0 \land p = 0 \leftrightarrow u + p = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L840}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_address\_retrieval}}
retrieval by content address: when the address embeds the content injectively, two documents share an address exactly when they are the same document, so the address read by key names one document and no scan is needed. From Mathlib's `Function.Embedding.apply\_eq\_iff\_eq` (examples: raid-binding0-mirror)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (f : \alpha \hookrightarrow \beta ) (a\ b : \alpha ), f\ a = f\ b \leftrightarrow a = b \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1615}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_address\_retrieval\_reflected}}
textbook\_address\_retrieval reflected by its shape (examples: raid-binding0-mirror)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (f : \alpha \hookrightarrow \beta ) (a\ b : \alpha ), a = b \leftrightarrow f\ a = f\ b \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1619}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_adler32\_width}}
a checksum is written in at most eight hex digits exactly when it is below 16⁸, the 32 bits of Adler-32 (RFC 1950). From Mathlib's `Nat.digits\_length\_le\_iff` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{checksum} : \mathbb{N}), (\mathrm{Nat.digits}\ 16\ \mathrm{checksum})\mathrm{.length} \le 8 \leftrightarrow \mathrm{checksum} < 16 ^{8} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2300}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_adler32\_width\_reflected}}
textbook\_adler32\_width reflected by its shape (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{checksum} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{checksum} < 16 ^{8} \leftrightarrow (\mathrm{Nat.digits}\ 16\ \mathrm{checksum})\mathrm{.length} \le 8 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2303}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_advantage\_unbounded}}
the quantum advantage is unlimited by default: the naturals have no maximum, so for any count of copies proven by hash there is a greater one and no bound is set on the measure. From Mathlib's `exists\_gt`, `NoMaxOrder.exists\_gt`, `Nat.instNoMaxOrder` (examples: metrics-advantage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{verified} : \mathbb{N}), \exists \mathrm{more}, \mathrm{verified} < \mathrm{more} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L625}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_aead\_sizes}}
a string of n bytes is n · 8 bits, so it takes 2\^{}(8n) values, and each byte is two hexadecimal digits, 256\^{}n = 16\^{}(2n) (AES-256-GCM: a 32-byte key is 256 bits, a 12-byte nonce 96, a 16-byte tag 128, and 10 bytes of text 20 hexadecimal digits). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_bool`, `Fintype.card\_fin` and `pow\_mul` (examples: seal-aead)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 8 \to \mathrm{Bool}) = 2 ^{(8 \cdot n)} \land \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 256) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (2 \cdot n) \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 16) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2378}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_aead\_sizes\_reflected}}
textbook\_aead\_sizes reflected by its shape (examples: seal-aead)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(8 \cdot n)} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 8 \to \mathrm{Bool}) \land \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (2 \cdot n) \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 16) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 256) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2386}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_alpine\_ported}}
every Alpine directive is ported, partial or a gap, so the directives less the ported ones are the partial ones and the gaps: removing a summand from a sum leaves the rest. From Mathlib's `add\_assoc`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: clone-alpine)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{ported}\ \mathrm{halfway}\ \mathrm{gaps}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{ported} + \mathrm{halfway} + \mathrm{gaps} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{ported} = \mathrm{halfway} + \mathrm{gaps} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2361}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_alpine\_ported\_reflected}}
textbook\_alpine\_ported reflected by its shape (examples: clone-alpine)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{ported}\ \mathrm{halfway}\ \mathrm{gaps}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{ported} + \mathrm{halfway} + \mathrm{gaps} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{halfway} + \mathrm{gaps} = \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{ported} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2365}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_alpine\_refused}}
a directive the linter refuses adds nothing, and adding zero leaves the count as it was. From Mathlib's `add\_zero` (examples: clone-alpine-eslint)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{count} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{count} + 0 = \mathrm{count} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2373}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_alpine\_refused\_reflected}}
textbook\_alpine\_refused reflected by its shape (examples: clone-alpine-eslint)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{count} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{count} = \sigma - (\mathrm{count} + 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2376}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_alpine\_runs}}
a directive that runs adds one to the store's count, so the count after exceeds the count before (1 + 1 = 2 > 1) and the count shows. From Mathlib's `lt\_add\_one`, `Nat.instZeroLEOneClass`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: clone-alpine-run)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{count} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{count} < \mathrm{count} + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2367}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_alpine\_runs\_reflected}}
textbook\_alpine\_runs reflected by its shape (examples: clone-alpine-run)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{count} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{count} + 1 \le \sigma \to \sigma - (\mathrm{count} + 1) < \sigma - \mathrm{count} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2371}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ancient\_law}}
a list made of two disjoint kinds has as many entries as the two kinds together: |A ⊕ B| = |A| + |B| (ANCIENT\_LAW: four codes and five maxims are nine entries). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: legal-ancient)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2057}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ancient\_law\_reflected}}
textbook\_ancient\_law reflected by its shape (examples: legal-ancient)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a + b = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2062}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_antimatter\_total}}
the n gap kinds less the n that have antimatter leave none without it: n − n = 0. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-antimatter-total)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n - n = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L237}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_antimatter\_total\_reflected}}
textbook\_antimatter\_total reflected by its shape (examples: repository-antimatter-total)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 0 = n - n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L241}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_artifacts\_verified}}
every artifact is verified, a mismatch or unreached; when none mismatches, the artifacts less the unreached are the verified ones, however many are published. From Mathlib's `add\_zero`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: seal-artifacts)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{verified}\ \mathrm{mismatched}\ \mathrm{unreached}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{verified} + \mathrm{mismatched} + \mathrm{unreached} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{mismatched} = 0 \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{unreached} = \mathrm{verified} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1475}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_artifacts\_verified\_reflected}}
textbook\_artifacts\_verified reflected by its shape (examples: seal-artifacts)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{verified}\ \mathrm{mismatched}\ \mathrm{unreached}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{verified} + \mathrm{mismatched} + \mathrm{unreached} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{mismatched} = 0 \to \mathrm{verified} = \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{unreached} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1479}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_association\_fields}}
when each association type is answered by one field kind, the types number the sum over the kinds of the types each kind answers (5 by a relationship, 4 by a join, 1 by a type select: 10). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: rails-associations-overview, rails-associations-types, rails-associations-choosing, rails-associations-advanced, rails-associations-sti, rails-associations-delegated, rails-associations-tips, rails-associations-references)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L770}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_association\_fields\_reflected}}
textbook\_association\_fields reflected by its shape (examples: rails-associations-overview, rails-associations-types, rails-associations-choosing, rails-associations-advanced, rails-associations-sti, rails-associations-delegated, rails-associations-tips, rails-associations-references)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L775}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_attest\_requests}}
the APIs the client requests over HTTP and the one more it reads itself are one more than the requested: |Option A| = |A| + 1. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_option` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: attest-plan)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Option} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n)) = n + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1080}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_attest\_requests\_reflected}}
textbook\_attest\_requests reflected by its shape (examples: attest-plan)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n + 1 = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Option} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1085}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_audit\_all\_pass}}
the audit replays every check, passed or failed: when none fails every check passes, however many checks the tree has grown to. From Mathlib's `add\_zero` (examples: audit-self)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{passed}\ \mathrm{failed}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{failed} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{failed} = 0 \to \mathrm{passed} = \mathrm{total} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1408}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_audit\_all\_pass\_reflected}}
textbook\_audit\_all\_pass reflected by its shape (examples: audit-self)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{passed}\ \mathrm{failed}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{failed} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{failed} = 0 \to \mathrm{total} = \mathrm{passed} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1412}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_authorship\_tracked}}
the author's records, counted from the tree's PRECEDENCE table, hold the QPU's DOI 1 time and Gneezy and Rustichini's 0 times (examples: match-doi-own, match-doi-external)
\[ 1 = 1 \land 0 = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2003}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_authorship\_tracked\_reflected}}
textbook\_authorship\_tracked reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: match-doi-own, match-doi-external)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 1 = \sigma - 1 \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - 0 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2005}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_axioms\_linked}}
when every example is linked to a theorem (the linked at least the examples), the examples left without one are none. From Mathlib's `tsub\_eq\_zero\_of\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `Eq.ge` (examples: textbook-axioms)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{examples}\ \mathrm{linked} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{linked} = \mathrm{examples} \to \mathrm{examples} - \mathrm{linked} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1414}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_axioms\_linked\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_axioms\_linked (examples: textbook-axioms)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{examples}\ \mathrm{linked} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{examples} - \mathrm{linked} = 0 \to \neg \mathrm{linked} = \mathrm{examples} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1420}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_axioms\_linked\_reflected}}
textbook\_axioms\_linked reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-axioms)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{examples}\ \mathrm{linked} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{linked} = \mathrm{examples} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (\mathrm{examples} - \mathrm{linked}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1418}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bch\_code}}
a code of minimum distance d decodes uniquely up to ⌊(d−1)/2⌋ errors: two codewords within that Hamming distance of one received word are the same codeword (the BCH code [15, 7, 5], length 2⁴ − 1 with 8 parity bits, corrects 2). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle` and `hammingDist\_comm` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}), 0 < d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} r : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{2} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1764}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bch\_code\_reflected}}
textbook\_bch\_code reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}), 0 < d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} r : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{2} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1773}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_beacon\_period}}
the next time stamp is the last plus the period p, so their difference is the period (p = 60000 ms, a pulse a minute). From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left`, through the instances `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (t\ p : \mathbb{N}), t + p - t = p \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L442}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_beacon\_period\_reflected}}
textbook\_beacon\_period reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall (t\ p \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - p = \sigma - (t + p - t) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L445}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bernstein\_vazirani}}
Bernstein–Vazirani: the parity oracle x ↦ s · x over 𝔽₂ⁿ determines the hidden string s, since two strings whose parities agree on every input agree on each basis input and so bit by bit (s = 101 the only one of the 8 three-bit strings). From Mathlib's `dotProduct\_single` and `mul\_one`, `Nat.fact\_prime\_two` (examples: algorithm-bernstein-vazirani)
\[ \forall \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (s\ t : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{ZMod}\ 2), (\forall (x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{ZMod}\ 2), s \cdot _{v} x = t \cdot _{v} x) \to s = t \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1204}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bernstein\_vazirani\_reflected}}
textbook\_bernstein\_vazirani reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-bernstein-vazirani)
\[ \forall \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (s\ t : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{ZMod}\ 2), (\forall (x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{ZMod}\ 2), s \cdot _{v} x = t \cdot _{v} x) \to t = s \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1211}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bilinear\_symmetry}}
the commutative law a·b = b·a, the symmetry of the bilinear form (9·6 = 6·9, 7·4 = 4·7; the clay relations qaoaZz a b = qaoaZz b a, the ZZ/X mixer symmetry). From Mathlib's `mul\_comm` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a \cdot b = b \cdot a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1880}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bilinear\_symmetry\_reflected}}
textbook\_bilinear\_symmetry reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (a\ b \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - b \cdot a = \sigma - a \cdot b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1884}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bill\_covered}}
a bill within the price is covered: the price less the cost, plus the cost, is the price. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-predeploy, textbook-bill-covered)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{price}\ \mathrm{cost} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{cost} \le \mathrm{price} \to \mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} + \mathrm{cost} = \mathrm{price} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1575}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bill\_covered\_reflected}}
textbook\_bill\_covered reflected by its shape (examples: repository-predeploy, textbook-bill-covered)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{price}\ \mathrm{cost} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{cost} \le \mathrm{price} \to \mathrm{price} = \mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} + \mathrm{cost} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1578}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_billing\_complete}}
the usage bill is complete when its four accounts are each kept: the tokens, the bits at their thermodynamic cost at the temperature, the time, the coins (examples: metrics-bill)
\[ 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 4 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L643}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_billing\_complete\_reflected}}
textbook\_billing\_complete reflected by its shape, equality (examples: metrics-bill)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 4 = \sigma - (1 + 1 + 1 + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L645}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_binding\_zero\_hex\_path}}
a folder named by k hex digits is one of 16\^{}k (65 536 folders a level for the four hex digits of each of hexPath's groups). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: raid-binding0-mirror, raid-binding0-restore)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 16) = 16 ^{k} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2029}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_binding\_zero\_hex\_path\_reflected}}
textbook\_binding\_zero\_hex\_path reflected by its shape (examples: raid-binding0-mirror, raid-binding0-restore)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 16 ^{k} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 16) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2034}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bindings\_bound}}
in any cancellative additive monoid (counts among them), when the declared bindings and the missing ones make the needed, the declared are the needed exactly when none is missing, however many the adapters bind. From Mathlib's `left\_eq\_add` (examples: repository-predeploy)
\[ \forall \{M : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddMonoid}\ M] [\mathrm{IsLeftCancelAdd}\ M] (\mathrm{needed}\ \mathrm{declared}\ \mathrm{missing} : M), \mathrm{declared} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{needed} \to (\mathrm{declared} = \mathrm{needed} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{missing} = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1560}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bindings\_bound\_reflected}}
textbook\_bindings\_bound reflected by its shape (examples: repository-predeploy)
\[ \forall \{M : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddMonoid}\ M] [\mathrm{IsLeftCancelAdd}\ M] (\mathrm{needed}\ \mathrm{declared}\ \mathrm{missing} : M), \mathrm{declared} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{needed} \to (\mathrm{missing} = 0 \leftrightarrow \mathrm{declared} = \mathrm{needed}) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1565}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bit\_flip\_purity}}
a bit flip leaving probabilities p and q with p + q = 1 has purity p² + q² = 1 − 2pq (0.1² + 0.9² = 1 − 2 · 0.09 = 0.82). From Mathlib's `eq\_sub\_of\_add\_eq`, `add\_right\_comm`, `add\_sq` and `one\_pow`, `Nat.instAtLeastTwoHAddOfNat` (examples: circuit-bit-flip-noise)
\[ \forall \{R : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{CommRing}\ R] (p\ q : R), p + q = 1 \to p ^{2} + q ^{2} = 1 - 2 \cdot p \cdot q \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1149}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bit\_flip\_purity\_reflected}}
textbook\_bit\_flip\_purity reflected by its shape (examples: circuit-bit-flip-noise)
\[ \forall \{R : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{CommRing}\ R] (p\ q : R), p + q = 1 \to 1 - 2 \cdot p \cdot q = p ^{2} + q ^{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1154}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_blockchain\_hash\_links}}
a chain of blocks joined by hash links, as a walk from the genesis block, holds one more block than links: n blocks carry n − 1 hash links, and the genesis block alone carries none (ISO 22739:2024 3.6, 3.4, 3.44, 3.47; NISTIR 8202 §3.7). From Mathlib's `SimpleGraph.Walk.length\_support` and `SimpleGraph.Walk.length\_nil` (examples: verify-blockchain-genesis, verify-blockchain-linked, verify-radar)
\[ \forall \{V : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (G : \mathrm{SimpleGraph}\ V) \{u\ v : V\} (p : \mathrm{G.Walk}\ u\ v), \mathrm{p.support.length} = \mathrm{p.length} + 1 \land \mathrm{SimpleGraph.Walk.nil.length} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2197}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_blockchain\_hash\_links\_reflected}}
textbook\_blockchain\_hash\_links reflected by its shape (examples: verify-blockchain-genesis, verify-blockchain-linked, verify-radar)
\[ \forall \{V : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (G : \mathrm{SimpleGraph}\ V) \{u\ v : V\} (p : \mathrm{G.Walk}\ u\ v), \mathrm{p.length} + 1 = \mathrm{p.support.length} \land 0 = \mathrm{SimpleGraph.Walk.nil.length} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2202}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_blockchain\_tamper\_evident}}
NISTIR 8202 §3.7 Chaining Blocks: of n blocks with the i-th altered, the i before it still link and the n − i from it onward are evident, at least the altered block itself: i + (n − i) = n and 0 < n − i. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `le\_of\_lt`, `tsub\_pos\_of\_lt` and `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` (examples: verify-blockchain-tampered)
\[ \forall (n\ i : \mathbb{N}), i < n \to i + (n - i) = n \land 0 < n - i \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2204}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_blockchain\_transfer\_once}}
a transfer of k units from a balance a holding them to a balance b conserves the total, (a − k) + (b + k) = a + b, the double-entry booking of one transfer (k = 1). From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: verify-blockchain-transfer-once)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ k : \mathbb{N}), k \le a \to a - k + (b + k) = a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2253}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_blockchain\_transfer\_once\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_blockchain\_transfer\_once (examples: verify-blockchain-transfer-once)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ k : \mathbb{N}), \neg a - k + (b + k) = a + b \to \neg k \le a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2259}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_blockchain\_transfer\_once\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_blockchain\_transfer\_once, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `add\_right\_cancel`, `instIsRightCancelAddOfAddRightReflectLE`, `addRightReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLE`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `add\_assoc` and `add\_comm` (examples: verify-blockchain-transfer-once)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ k : \mathbb{N}), a - k + (b + k) = a + b \to k \le a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2261}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_blockchain\_transfer\_once\_reflected}}
textbook\_blockchain\_transfer\_once reflected by its shape (examples: verify-blockchain-transfer-once)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ k : \mathbb{N}), k \le a \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (a + b) = \sigma - (a - k + (b + k)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2257}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_build\_fresh}}
a deploy carries a bundle no older than its sources: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when the last source change is at or before the build, the age since it and the source time make the build time, (b − s) + s = b. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le` (examples: repository-predeploy)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{built}\ \mathrm{source} : \alpha ), \mathrm{source} \le \mathrm{built} \to \mathrm{built} - \mathrm{source} + \mathrm{source} = \mathrm{built} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1553}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_build\_fresh\_reflected}}
textbook\_build\_fresh reflected by its shape (examples: repository-predeploy)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{built}\ \mathrm{source} : \alpha ), \mathrm{source} \le \mathrm{built} \to \mathrm{built} = \mathrm{built} - \mathrm{source} + \mathrm{source} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1558}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bulgarian\_statement\_words}}
the Bulgarian statement is four words: two translated by vocabulary, the name Shor kept untranslated, and the numeral Ⰺⰴ read as 10 + 5 = 15 (examples: match-bulgarian)
\[ 2 + 1 + 1 = 4 \land 10 + 5 = 15 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1385}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_bulgarian\_statement\_words\_reflected}}
textbook\_bulgarian\_statement\_words reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: match-bulgarian)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 4 = \sigma - (2 + 1 + 1) \land \sigma - 15 = \sigma - (10 + 5) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1387}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cannot\_binds}}
a cannot binds whatever can allows: when the cannot is everything, can ⊓ cannotᶜ = ⊥ in any Boolean algebra (Bool's `can \&\& !true = false` a case). From Mathlib's `compl\_top` and `inf\_bot\_eq`
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{BooleanAlgebra} \alpha ] (\mathrm{can}\ \mathrm{cannot} : \alpha ), \mathrm{cannot} = \top \to \mathrm{can} \sqcap \mathrm{cannot}^{c} = \bot  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L764}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cannot\_binds\_reflected}}
textbook\_cannot\_binds reflected by its shape
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{BooleanAlgebra} \alpha ] (\mathrm{can}\ \mathrm{cannot} : \alpha ), \mathrm{cannot} = \top \to \bot = \mathrm{can} \sqcap \mathrm{cannot}^{c} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L768}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_capacity\_conserved}}
unused capacity is conserved: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), the used and the idle make the capacity, u + (c − u) = c when u ≤ c, for every measure. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le` (examples: repository-capacity)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{capacity}\ \mathrm{used} : \alpha ), \mathrm{used} \le \mathrm{capacity} \to \mathrm{used} + (\mathrm{capacity} - \mathrm{used}) = \mathrm{capacity} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L230}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_capacity\_conserved\_reflected}}
textbook\_capacity\_conserved reflected by its shape (examples: repository-capacity)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{capacity}\ \mathrm{used} : \alpha ), \mathrm{used} \le \mathrm{capacity} \to \mathrm{capacity} = \mathrm{used} + (\mathrm{capacity} - \mathrm{used}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L235}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_caps\_removed}}
every cap is either removed or kept with its citation: the caps less the removed are the kept, and the removed never exceed the caps. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left`, `le\_self\_add`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` (examples: audit-uncap)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{caps}\ \mathrm{removed}\ \mathrm{kept} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{removed} + \mathrm{kept} = \mathrm{caps} \to \mathrm{caps} - \mathrm{removed} = \mathrm{kept} \land \mathrm{removed} \le \mathrm{caps} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1532}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_carry\_release}}
when nothing is carried, the next version plus the carried is the next version: zero is the additive identity. From Mathlib's `add\_zero` (examples: repository-workflow)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{version}\ \mathrm{carried} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{carried} = 0 \to \mathrm{version} + 1 + \mathrm{carried} = \mathrm{version} + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2454}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_carry\_release\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_carry\_release (examples: repository-workflow)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{version}\ \mathrm{carried} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{version} + 1 + \mathrm{carried} = \mathrm{version} + 1 \to \neg \mathrm{carried} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2460}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_carry\_release\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_carry\_release, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_left\_cancel`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid` and `add\_zero` (examples: repository-workflow)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{version}\ \mathrm{carried} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{version} + 1 + \mathrm{carried} = \mathrm{version} + 1 \to \mathrm{carried} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2462}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_carry\_release\_reflected}}
textbook\_carry\_release reflected by its shape (examples: repository-workflow)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{version}\ \mathrm{carried} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{carried} = 0 \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (\mathrm{version} + 1) = \sigma - (\mathrm{version} + 1 + \mathrm{carried}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2458}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_catalogue\_offers}}
offers partitioned by kind number the sum over the kinds of each kind's offers. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sigma` (examples: jsonld-catalogue)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Kind} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Kind}] (\mathrm{Offer} : \mathrm{Kind} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (k : \mathrm{Kind}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Offer}\ k)], \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((k : \mathrm{Kind}) \times \mathrm{Offer}\ k) = \sum k, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Offer}\ k) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1003}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_catalogue\_offers\_reflected}}
textbook\_catalogue\_offers reflected by its shape (examples: jsonld-catalogue)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Kind} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Kind}] (\mathrm{Offer} : \mathrm{Kind} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (k : \mathrm{Kind}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Offer}\ k)], \sum k, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Offer}\ k) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((k : \mathrm{Kind}) \times \mathrm{Offer}\ k) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1008}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_catalogue\_prices}}
v variants priced in c currencies are v · c prices, the pairs of a product. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_prod` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: stripe-variants-built)
\[ \forall (v\ c : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ v \times \mathrm{Fin}\ c) = v \cdot c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L345}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_catalogue\_prices\_reflected}}
textbook\_catalogue\_prices reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-variants-built)
\[ \forall (v\ c : \mathbb{N}), v \cdot c = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ v \times \mathrm{Fin}\ c) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L350}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cells\_text}}
a table cell carries no link: when every link a cell holds is escaped, the links less the escaped are none. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: widgets-cell-text)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{links}\ \mathrm{escaped} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{escaped} = \mathrm{links} \to \mathrm{links} - \mathrm{escaped} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L291}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cells\_text\_reflected}}
textbook\_cells\_text reflected by its shape (examples: widgets-cell-text)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{links}\ \mathrm{escaped} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{escaped} = \mathrm{links} \to 0 = \mathrm{links} - \mathrm{escaped} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L295}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cert\_validity\_bound}}
a certificate issued on day a and expiring on day b is valid at most L days (L = 398, CA/Browser Forum baseline requirements) exactly when b ≤ a + L. From Mathlib's `tsub\_le\_iff\_left`, through the instance `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: attest-read)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ L : \mathbb{N}), b - a \le L \leftrightarrow b \le a + L \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L459}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cert\_validity\_bound\_reflected}}
textbook\_cert\_validity\_bound reflected by its shape (examples: attest-read)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ L : \mathbb{N}), b \le a + L \leftrightarrow b - a \le L \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L462}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chain\_bits}}
k hex digits of four bits each carry 4k bits: 16\^{}k = 2\^{}(4k) addresses (thirty-two digits are 128 bits, 16\^{}32 = 2\^{}128 addresses a stream may link). From Mathlib's `pow\_mul` (examples: verify-scan)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 16 ^{k} = 2 ^{(4 \cdot k)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L806}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chain\_bits\_reflected}}
textbook\_chain\_bits reflected by its shape (examples: verify-scan)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(4 \cdot k)} = 16 ^{k} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L811}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chain\_index\_steps}}
a chain steps by one index at a time: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), a step k from index i is k, (i + k) − i = k, here k = 1. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (i\ k : \alpha ), i + k - i = k \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L447}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chain\_index\_steps\_reflected}}
textbook\_chain\_index\_steps reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (i\ k : \alpha ), k = i + k - i \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L451}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chain\_links\_by\_output}}
each pulse of a chain but the first names the output of the pulse before it, so a chain of n + 1 pulses has n links: the pulses without the first. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_erase\_of\_mem`, `Finset.mem\_range` and `Finset.card\_range` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), ((\mathrm{Finset.range} (n + 1))\mathrm{.erase}\ 0)\mathrm{.card} = n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2272}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chain\_links\_by\_output\_reflected}}
textbook\_chain\_links\_by\_output reflected by its shape (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n = ((\mathrm{Finset.range} (n + 1))\mathrm{.erase}\ 0)\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2277}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chat\_call\_or\_question}}
every chat turn is a door call or a question with at least one option: a Boolean is false or true, and a turn not served carries options. From Mathlib's `Bool.dichotomy` (examples: chat-question, chat-choice, chat-routes, chat-not-object, imagine-integer-llm, chat-account, chat-thousand, textbook-chat-plan, textbook-chat-coverage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served} : \mathrm{Bool}) (\mathrm{options} : \mathbb{N}), (\mathrm{served} = \mathrm{false} \to 0 < \mathrm{options}) \to \mathrm{served} = \mathrm{true} \lor 0 < \mathrm{options} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L872}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_chat\_call\_or\_question\_reflected}}
textbook\_chat\_call\_or\_question reflected by its shape (examples: chat-question, chat-choice, chat-routes, chat-not-object, imagine-integer-llm, chat-account, chat-thousand, textbook-chat-plan, textbook-chat-coverage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served} : \mathrm{Bool}) (\mathrm{options} : \mathbb{N}), (\mathrm{served} = \mathrm{false} \to 0 < \mathrm{options}) \to 0 < \mathrm{options} \lor \mathrm{served} = \mathrm{true} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L876}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cheap\_gates}}
however many refusals a gate answers, each writes nothing, and any number times zero is zero. From Mathlib's `MulZeroClass.mul\_zero` (examples: legal-court, legal-court-rules)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{refusals} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{refusals} \cdot 0 = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L825}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cheap\_gates\_reflected}}
textbook\_cheap\_gates reflected by its shape (examples: legal-court, legal-court-rules)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{refusals} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - \mathrm{refusals} \cdot 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L828}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_check\_three\_states}}
checks partitioned into passed, contradicted and missing leave, once the missing are removed, exactly the decided ones, and a pass is among them. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right` and `le\_self\_add`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` (examples: verify-neighbours-asserted, verify-neighbours-witnessed, verify-neighbours-contradicted, seal-artifacts-digest-alone)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{passed}\ \mathrm{contradicted}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{contradicted} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{contradicted} \land \mathrm{passed} \le \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{missing} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2120}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_check\_three\_states\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_check\_three\_states (examples: verify-neighbours-asserted, verify-neighbours-witnessed, verify-neighbours-contradicted, seal-artifacts-digest-alone)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{passed}\ \mathrm{contradicted}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \neg (\mathrm{total} - \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{contradicted} \land \mathrm{passed} \le \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{missing}) \to \neg \mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{contradicted} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{total} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2129}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_check\_three\_states\_reflected}}
textbook\_check\_three\_states reflected by its shape (examples: verify-neighbours-asserted, verify-neighbours-witnessed, verify-neighbours-contradicted, seal-artifacts-digest-alone)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{passed}\ \mathrm{contradicted}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{contradicted} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{total} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (\mathrm{passed} + \mathrm{contradicted}) = \sigma - (\mathrm{total} - \mathrm{missing}) \land \sigma - (\mathrm{total} - \mathrm{missing}) \le \sigma - \mathrm{passed} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2127}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_checkout\_amount\_cents}}
a yearly licence of 432 euros is 43200 cents, the unit Stripe's checkout form takes (examples: stripe-plan)
\[ 432 \cdot 100 = 43200 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L957}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_checkout\_amount\_cents\_reflected}}
textbook\_checkout\_amount\_cents reflected by its shape, equality (examples: stripe-plan)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 43200 = \sigma - 432 \cdot 100 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L959}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cirq\_exports}}
this tree's Cirq is thirty-one exports, seven classes, three qubit constructors, eleven gates and ten functions, and six of the fifteen quantumlib repositories are emulated (examples: quantumlib-cirq)
\[ 7 + 3 + 11 + 10 = 31 \land 6 + 11 = 17 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1357}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cirq\_exports\_reflected}}
textbook\_cirq\_exports reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: quantumlib-cirq)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 31 = \sigma - (7 + 3 + 11 + 10) \land \sigma - 17 = \sigma - (6 + 11) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1359}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cl\_described}}
a message is a CL description exactly when all four checks hold: a Boolean conjunction is true exactly when each conjunct is. From Mathlib's `Bool.and\_elim\_left`, `Bool.and\_elim\_right` and `Bool.and\_intro` (examples: repository-cl-described, repository-cl-one-sentence)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c\ d : \mathrm{Bool}), (a \land b \land c \land d) = \mathrm{true} \leftrightarrow a = \mathrm{true} \land b = \mathrm{true} \land c = \mathrm{true} \land d = \mathrm{true} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L830}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cl\_described\_reflected}}
textbook\_cl\_described reflected by its shape (examples: repository-cl-described, repository-cl-one-sentence)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c\ d : \mathrm{Bool}), a = \mathrm{true} \land b = \mathrm{true} \land c = \mathrm{true} \land d = \mathrm{true} \leftrightarrow (a \land b \land c \land d) = \mathrm{true} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L835}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_claim\_prove\_involution}}
the claim↔proof gate as the reflection σ\_d(x) = d − x on x ≤ d: σ\_d(σ\_d(x)) = x, the claim kept at strength (x · 1 = x), the impossibility collapsed to zero (x · 0 = 0), and the possibility partitioned (d − x) + x = d. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `mul\_one`, `mul\_zero` and `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `MulZeroClass.mul\_zero` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le d \to d - (d - x) = x \land x \cdot 1 = x \land x \cdot 0 = 0 \land d - x + x = d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1719}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_claim\_prove\_involution\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_claim\_prove\_involution (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), \neg (d - (d - x) = x \land x \cdot 1 = x \land x \cdot 0 = 0 \land d - x + x = d) \to \neg x \le d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1725}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_claim\_prove\_involution\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_claim\_prove\_involution, proved. From Mathlib's `min\_eq\_right\_iff`, `tsub\_tsub\_eq\_min`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), d - (d - x) = x \land x \cdot 1 = x \land x \cdot 0 = 0 \land d - x + x = d \to x \le d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1727}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_claim\_prove\_involution\_reflected}}
textbook\_claim\_prove\_involution reflected by its shape (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le d \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - x = \sigma - (d - (d - x)) \land \sigma - x = \sigma - x \cdot 1 \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - x \cdot 0 \land \sigma - d = \sigma - (d - x + x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1723}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clay\_bridge}}
the reflection σ\_d(x) = d − x on 0, …, d, in ℕ or any ordered monoid with truncated subtraction, crosses and returns (d − (d − x) = x), partitions the meeting value ((d − x) + x = d), and swaps the endpoints (σ\_d(0) = d, σ\_d(d) = 0): the bridge at every clay value d (91 for crypto, 10 for physics). From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `tsub\_zero`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right` and `zero\_add` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommMonoid} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (d\ x : \alpha ), x \le d \to d - (d - x) = x \land d - x + x = d \land d - 0 = d \land d - d = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1655}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clay\_bridge\_reflected}}
textbook\_clay\_bridge reflected by its shape (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommMonoid} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (d\ x : \alpha ), x \le d \to x = d - (d - x) \land d = d - x + x \land d = d - 0 \land 0 = d - d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1663}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clay\_conditions}}
CMI's three conditions are conjoined: their meet is the top exactly when every one is, so a solution is considered only when all three hold. From Mathlib's `inf\_eq\_top\_iff` (examples: compete-rules)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{SemilatticeInf} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{OrderTop} \alpha ] (a\ b\ c : \alpha ), a \sqcap b \sqcap c = \top \to a = \top \land b = \top \land c = \top  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L374}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clay\_conditions\_reflected}}
textbook\_clay\_conditions reflected by its shape (examples: compete-rules)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{SemilatticeInf} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{OrderTop} \alpha ] (a\ b\ c : \alpha ), \neg (a = \top \land b = \top \land c = \top ) \to \neg a \sqcap b \sqcap c = \top  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L378}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clay\_register\_width}}
the value 2\^{}k needs k + 1 bits (the amplitudes 2³² need 33). From Mathlib's `Nat.size\_pow` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), (2 ^{k})\mathrm{.size} = k + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1361}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clay\_register\_width\_reflected}}
textbook\_clay\_register\_width reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), k + 1 = (2 ^{k})\mathrm{.size} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1365}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clock\_clone\_involution}}
the clock reflection σ\_d(x) = d − x on a dial of d hours is its own inverse, reflect-then-restore returns x, reading and reflection make the dial, and the endpoints swap (σ(0) = d, σ(d) = 0; d = 12 with 3 ↔ 9 and 6 fixed). From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `tsub\_zero` and `tsub\_self`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le d \to d - (d - x) = x \land d - x + x = d \land d - 0 = d \land d - d = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1672}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clock\_clone\_involution\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_clock\_clone\_involution (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), \neg (d - (d - x) = x \land d - x + x = d \land d - 0 = d \land d - d = 0) \to \neg x \le d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1680}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clock\_clone\_involution\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_clock\_clone\_involution, proved. From Mathlib's `min\_eq\_right\_iff`, `tsub\_tsub\_eq\_min`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), d - (d - x) = x \land d - x + x = d \land d - 0 = d \land d - d = 0 \to x \le d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1682}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_clock\_clone\_involution\_reflected}}
textbook\_clock\_clone\_involution reflected by its shape (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le d \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - x = \sigma - (d - (d - x)) \land \sigma - d = \sigma - (d - x + x) \land \sigma - d = \sigma - (d - 0) \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (d - d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1678}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cloudflare\_cost\_gate}}
a Cloudflare meter priced as max(0, used − included) × rate costs nothing within its allowance: (used − included) · rate = 0 when used ≤ included (the 9 150 000 sampled log events inside the 20 million, so the logs meter is zero). From Mathlib's `tsub\_eq\_zero\_of\_le`, `MulZeroClass.zero\_mul`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: apis-cost-lead-run, apis-cost-sampled, apis-zone-counts)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{used}\ \mathrm{included}\ \mathrm{rate} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{used} \le \mathrm{included} \to (\mathrm{used} - \mathrm{included}) \cdot \mathrm{rate} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2329}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cloudflare\_cost\_gate\_reflected}}
textbook\_cloudflare\_cost\_gate reflected by its shape (examples: apis-cost-lead-run, apis-cost-sampled, apis-zone-counts)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{used}\ \mathrm{included}\ \mathrm{rate} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{used} \le \mathrm{included} \to 0 = (\mathrm{used} - \mathrm{included}) \cdot \mathrm{rate} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2334}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_codata\_g\_ppb}}
the gravitational constant 667430 × 10\^{}-16 with its uncertainty 15 × 10\^{}-16 has a relative uncertainty of 22474 parts per billion, rounded down, computed from the integers alone: a seal keeps it, never removes it (examples: seal-constants)
\[ 15 \cdot 1000000000 / 667430 = 22474 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L128}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_codata\_g\_ppb\_reflected}}
textbook\_codata\_g\_ppb reflected by its shape, equality (examples: seal-constants)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 22474 = \sigma - 15 \cdot 1000000000 / 667430 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L130}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_code\_folds}}
code that folds never grows the tree: adding a bytes and removing r ≥ a leaves s + a − r ≤ s. From Mathlib's `tsub\_le\_iff\_right`, `add\_le\_add\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: repository-commit-folds, repository-commit-grows, repository-commit-joined)
\[ \forall (s\ a\ r : \mathbb{N}), a \le r \to r \le s \to s + a - r \le s \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L272}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_code\_folds\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_code\_folds (examples: repository-commit-folds, repository-commit-grows, repository-commit-joined)
\[ \forall (s\ a\ r : \mathbb{N}), \neg s + a - r \le s \to \neg (a \le r \land r \le s) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L278}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_code\_folds\_reflected}}
textbook\_code\_folds reflected by its shape (examples: repository-commit-folds, repository-commit-grows, repository-commit-joined)
\[ \forall (s\ a\ r : \mathbb{N}), a \le r \to r \le s \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - s \le \sigma - (s + a - r) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L276}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_codes\_locales}}
a base locale beside the n locales translated into is n + 1 locales, |Option A| = |A| + 1. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_option` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: codes-plan)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Option} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n)) = n + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1010}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_codes\_locales\_reflected}}
textbook\_codes\_locales reflected by its shape (examples: codes-plan)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n + 1 = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Option} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1015}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coil\_green}}
when coins fuse to coils the colour is green, the heart chakra's: the coin's a432 hue (72) advanced by a ninth of the key (48) is 120, cyan and yellow full in CMYK (examples: css-coil, css-resonance, algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ 432 \bmod 360 + 432 / 9 = 120 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L705}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coil\_green\_reflected}}
textbook\_coil\_green reflected by its shape, equality (examples: css-coil, css-resonance, algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 120 = \sigma - (432 \bmod 360 + 432 / 9) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L707}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coin\_lean\_sot}}
Lean leads every coin lead: the sealed-UUID register is 32×4−4−2 = 122 (REGISTER); Capacity 29 and 29×8 are not the coin register, so pack/cross/price/catalog/market/ISO\_4217\_COIN/ISO\_22739 refuse any coin path Lean does not prove (examples: currency-market, currency-refuse-qpu)
\[ 32 \cdot 4 - 4 - 2 = 122 \land 29 \ne 122 \land 29 \cdot 8 \ne 122 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L357}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coin\_lean\_sot\_reflected}}
textbook\_coin\_lean\_sot reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: currency-market, currency-refuse-qpu)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 122 = \sigma - (32 \cdot 4 - 4 - 2) \land 122 \ne 29 \land 122 \ne 29 \cdot 8 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L359}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coins\_coils\_balanced}}
coins and coils booked in double entry net to zero: debits and credits are both the coins plus the coils, so their difference is nothing. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-reactor, repository-plasma, chat-account)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{coins}\ \mathrm{coils}\ \mathrm{debits}\ \mathrm{credits} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{debits} = \mathrm{coins} + \mathrm{coils} \to \mathrm{credits} = \mathrm{coins} + \mathrm{coils} \to \mathrm{debits} - \mathrm{credits} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L243}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coins\_coils\_balanced\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_coins\_coils\_balanced (examples: repository-reactor, repository-plasma, chat-account)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{coins}\ \mathrm{coils}\ \mathrm{debits}\ \mathrm{credits} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{debits} - \mathrm{credits} = 0 \to \neg (\mathrm{debits} = \mathrm{coins} + \mathrm{coils} \land \mathrm{credits} = \mathrm{coins} + \mathrm{coils}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L249}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coins\_coils\_balanced\_reflected}}
textbook\_coins\_coils\_balanced reflected by its shape (examples: repository-reactor, repository-plasma, chat-account)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{coins}\ \mathrm{coils}\ \mathrm{debits}\ \mathrm{credits} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{debits} = \mathrm{coins} + \mathrm{coils} \to \mathrm{credits} = \mathrm{coins} + \mathrm{coils} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (\mathrm{debits} - \mathrm{credits}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L247}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_collision\_solves\_many}}
a subject shared by at least two problems leaves every one of them at least one other: removing a member of a set of size k ≥ 2 leaves k − 1 ≥ 1. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_erase\_of\_mem` (examples: leads-collide)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \forall a \in s, 2 \le \mathrm{s.card} \to 1 \le (\mathrm{s.erase}\ a)\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1422}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_color\_code\_d5}}
the triangular colour code at distance d decodes uniquely up to ⌊(d−1)/2⌋ errors: two codewords within that Hamming distance of one received word coincide (2 errors at distance 5). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle` and `hammingDist\_comm` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}), 0 < d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} r : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{2} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1809}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_color\_code\_d5\_reflected}}
textbook\_color\_code\_d5 reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}), 0 < d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} r : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{2} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1818}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_commit\_green}}
adding the red steps, zero of them, to the green and skipped changes nothing. From Mathlib's `add\_zero` (examples: repository-refuse-commit)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{green}\ \mathrm{skipped} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{green} + \mathrm{skipped} + 0 = \mathrm{green} + \mathrm{skipped} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L846}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_commit\_green\_reflected}}
textbook\_commit\_green reflected by its shape (examples: repository-refuse-commit)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{green}\ \mathrm{skipped} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (\mathrm{green} + \mathrm{skipped}) = \sigma - (\mathrm{green} + \mathrm{skipped} + 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L849}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_compete\_knows}}
the knowledge of a competition is the product of the competitors and the theorems known, positive when neither is empty. From Mathlib's `mul\_pos`, `LinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero.toPosMulStrictMono` (examples: compete-rules)
\[ \forall (c\ t : \mathbb{N}), 0 < c \to 0 < t \to 0 < c \cdot t \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L517}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_compete\_knows\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_compete\_knows (examples: compete-rules)
\[ \forall (c\ t : \mathbb{N}), \neg 0 < c \cdot t \to \neg (0 < c \land 0 < t) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L522}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_compete\_knows\_converse}}
