the converse of textbook\_compete\_knows, proved. From Mathlib's `CanonicallyOrderedAdd.mul\_pos`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `IsStrictOrderedRing.noZeroDivisors`, `Nat.instIsStrictOrderedRing`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE` and `Nat.instStarOrderedRing` (examples: compete-rules)
\[ \forall (c\ t : \mathbb{N}), 0 < c \cdot t \to 0 < c \land 0 < t \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L524}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_compete\_knows\_reflected}}
textbook\_compete\_knows reflected by its shape (examples: compete-rules)
\[ \forall (c\ t : \mathbb{N}), 0 < c \to 0 < t \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), c \cdot t \le \sigma \to \sigma - c \cdot t < \sigma - 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L520}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_concatenated\_code}}
concatenating a code of length n₁ with one of length n₂ encodes each of the n₁ outer positions into n₂, a length of n₁ · n₂ (the Steane code with itself: 7 · 7 = 49). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_prod` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall (n_{1} n_{2} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n_{1} \times \mathrm{Fin}\ n_{2}) = n_{1} \cdot n_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1833}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_concatenated\_code\_reflected}}
textbook\_concatenated\_code reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall (n_{1} n_{2} : \mathbb{N}), n_{1} \cdot n_{2} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n_{1} \times \mathrm{Fin}\ n_{2}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1838}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_context\_balanced}}
the tokens saved and the tokens kept make the whole list: (full − kept) + kept = full whenever kept ≤ full. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: extend-out-balanced)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{full}\ \mathrm{kept} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{kept} \le \mathrm{full} \to \mathrm{full} - \mathrm{kept} + \mathrm{kept} = \mathrm{full} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L633}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_context\_balanced\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_context\_balanced (examples: extend-out-balanced)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{full}\ \mathrm{kept} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{full} - \mathrm{kept} + \mathrm{kept} = \mathrm{full} \to \neg \mathrm{kept} \le \mathrm{full} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L638}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_context\_balanced\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_context\_balanced, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: extend-out-balanced)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{full}\ \mathrm{kept} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{full} - \mathrm{kept} + \mathrm{kept} = \mathrm{full} \to \mathrm{kept} \le \mathrm{full} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L640}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_context\_balanced\_reflected}}
textbook\_context\_balanced reflected by its shape (examples: extend-out-balanced)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{full}\ \mathrm{kept} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{kept} \le \mathrm{full} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{full} = \sigma - (\mathrm{full} - \mathrm{kept} + \mathrm{kept}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L636}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_correction\_by\_majority}}
with a real majority, more than half of the copies agreeing, the copies that disagree are fewer than the agreeing ones: A ⊆ s and |s| < 2|A| give |s \textbackslash{} A| < |A|. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` (examples: e2e-corrected-by-majority)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s\ A : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), A \subseteq s \to \mathrm{s.card} < 2 \cdot \mathrm{A.card} \to (s \setminus A)\mathrm{.card} < \mathrm{A.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2143}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_court\_audits\_repo}}
verifying the repo by its content address is one check while recomputing re-runs all n facts, so for any tree of at least two facts, 1 + 1 ≤ n means 1 < n. From Mathlib's `Order.add\_one\_le\_iff` and `Nat.instNoMaxOrder` (examples: legal-court-repo-audit)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 \le n \to 1 < n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L596}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coverage\_bounded}}
when the doors counted are among the servers counted, multiplying doors ≤ total by 100 bounds the whole-percent share by 100. From Mathlib's `mul\_le\_mul\_of\_nonneg\_right`, through the instances `IsOrderedRing.toMulPosMono`, `IsStrictOrderedRing.toIsOrderedRing` and `Nat.instIsStrictOrderedRing` (examples: extend-coverage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{doors}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{doors} \le \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{doors} \cdot 100 / \mathrm{total} \le 100 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L309}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coverage\_bounded\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_coverage\_bounded (examples: extend-coverage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{doors}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{doors} \cdot 100 / \mathrm{total} \le 100 \to \neg \mathrm{doors} \le \mathrm{total} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L315}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_coverage\_bounded\_reflected}}
textbook\_coverage\_bounded reflected by its shape (examples: extend-coverage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{doors}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{doors} \le \mathrm{total} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 100 \le \sigma - \mathrm{doors} \cdot 100 / \mathrm{total} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L313}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_create\_hue}}
a hue is an angle modulo the full turn: adding 360 degrees gives the same hue (432 = 72 + 360 is the hue 72). From Mathlib's `Nat.add\_modEq\_right\_iff` and `dvd\_refl` (examples: css-create)
\[ \forall (h : \mathbb{N}), 360 + h \equiv h [\mathrm{MOD}\ 360] \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L701}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_css\_one\_source}}
the hue of an address is its handle read in ZMod 360, whose value is the handle mod 360, one value per handle. From Mathlib's `ZMod.val\_natCast` (examples: css-one-source, fold-hex-one-source)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (\uparrow n)\mathrm{.val} = n \bmod 360 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2092}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_css\_one\_source\_reflected}}
textbook\_css\_one\_source reflected by its shape (examples: css-one-source, fold-hex-one-source)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \bmod 360 = (\uparrow n)\mathrm{.val} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2096}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cuboctahedron\_faces}}
the faces of the vector equilibrium are the triangles and the rest, the squares: a predicate splits a finite set into those that satisfy it and those that do not (8 + 6 = 14). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_filter\_add\_card\_filter\_not` (examples: verify-witnesses, fold-next)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{faces} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{triangle} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{triangle}], (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{triangle}\ \mathrm{faces})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{faces} | \neg \mathrm{triangle}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{faces.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L317}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cuboctahedron\_faces\_reflected}}
textbook\_cuboctahedron\_faces reflected by its shape (examples: verify-witnesses, fold-next)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{faces} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{triangle} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{triangle}], \mathrm{faces.card} = (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{triangle}\ \mathrm{faces})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{faces} | \neg \mathrm{triangle}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L322}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cve\_id\_well\_formed}}
a CVE id is nine fixed characters (CVE-, a year of four digits, a dash) and a sequence of four digits or more, so it has thirteen characters at least. From Mathlib's `add\_le\_add\_right`, through the instances `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{digits} : \mathbb{N}), 4 \le \mathrm{digits} \to 9 + 4 \le 9 + \mathrm{digits} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2294}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cvss\_bounded}}
a CVSS base score in tenths lies among the 101 values 0 to 100 exactly when it is at most 100. From Mathlib's `Finset.mem\_range` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (s : \mathbb{N}), s \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ 101 \leftrightarrow s \le 100 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L453}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_cvss\_bounded\_reflected}}
textbook\_cvss\_bounded reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall (s : \mathbb{N}), s \le 100 \leftrightarrow s \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ 101 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L457}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dashboard\_algorithm\_combinations}}
a tool's combinations are the product of its enumerable facets' spaces (7 · 19 · 1 · 2 · 6 · 1 · 1 · 8 · 2 · 11 = 280896 for the algorithm tool). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_pi` (examples: match-pattern-dashboard)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Facet} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Facet}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Facet}] (\mathrm{space} : \mathrm{Facet} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_2} : (f : \mathrm{Facet}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{space}\ f)], \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((f : \mathrm{Facet}) \to \mathrm{space}\ f) = \prod f, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{space}\ f) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1396}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dashboard\_algorithm\_combinations\_reflected}}
textbook\_dashboard\_algorithm\_combinations reflected by its shape (examples: match-pattern-dashboard)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Facet} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Facet}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Facet}] (\mathrm{space} : \mathrm{Facet} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_2} : (f : \mathrm{Facet}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{space}\ f)], \prod f, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{space}\ f) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((f : \mathrm{Facet}) \to \mathrm{space}\ f) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1401}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dashboard\_spaces}}
the input space of a tool is the product of its enumerable facets: |Π i, Fin (f i)| = ∏ f i (leads 2 · 2 · 2 · 2 · 5 = 80, algorithm 7 · 19 · 1 · 2 · 6 · 1 · 1 · 8 · 2 · 11 = 280,896). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_pi`, `Finset.prod\_congr` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: dashboard-index)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}) (f : \mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((i : \mathrm{Fin}\ k) \to \mathrm{Fin} (f\ i)) = \prod i, f\ i \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1108}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dashboard\_spaces\_reflected}}
textbook\_dashboard\_spaces reflected by its shape (examples: dashboard-index)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}) (f : \mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathbb{N}), \prod i, f\ i = \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((i : \mathrm{Fin}\ k) \to \mathrm{Fin} (f\ i)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1113}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dates\_ordered}}
what is published precedes what is modified; what begins precedes what ends (examples: textbook-dates-ordered)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a \le b \to a \le b \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L464}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dates\_ordered\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_dates\_ordered (examples: textbook-dates-ordered)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \neg a \le b \to \neg a \le b \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L469}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dates\_ordered\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_dates\_ordered, proved (examples: textbook-dates-ordered)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a \le b \to a \le b \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L471}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dates\_ordered\_reflected}}
textbook\_dates\_ordered reflected by its shape, order (examples: textbook-dates-ordered)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a \le b \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - b \le \sigma - a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L467}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dead\_removed}}
the declarations fixed and those left as leads make the findings: f + (n − f) = n when f ≤ n. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-dead)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{fixed}\ \mathrm{found} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{fixed} \le \mathrm{found} \to \mathrm{fixed} + (\mathrm{found} - \mathrm{fixed}) = \mathrm{found} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1028}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dead\_removed\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_dead\_removed (examples: audit-dead)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{fixed}\ \mathrm{found} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{fixed} + (\mathrm{found} - \mathrm{fixed}) = \mathrm{found} \to \neg \mathrm{fixed} \le \mathrm{found} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1033}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dead\_removed\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_dead\_removed, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-dead)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{fixed}\ \mathrm{found} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{fixed} + (\mathrm{found} - \mathrm{fixed}) = \mathrm{found} \to \mathrm{fixed} \le \mathrm{found} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1035}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dead\_removed\_reflected}}
textbook\_dead\_removed reflected by its shape (examples: audit-dead)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{fixed}\ \mathrm{found} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{fixed} \le \mathrm{found} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{found} = \sigma - (\mathrm{fixed} + (\mathrm{found} - \mathrm{fixed})) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1031}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_decode\_not\_search}}
decoding is algebra, not search: a composition of n positions of b choices is one of b\^{}n candidates, and decoding takes n steps, one per position, with n < b\^{}n for every b > 1. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: fold-decode, fold-decode-one-way)
\[ \forall (n\ b : \mathbb{N}), 1 < b \to \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = b ^{n} \land n < b ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1464}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_decode\_not\_search\_reflected}}
textbook\_decode\_not\_search reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: fold-decode, fold-decode-one-way)
\[ \forall (n\ b : \mathbb{N}), 1 < b \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), b ^{n} \le \sigma \to b ^{n} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \land \sigma - b ^{n} < \sigma - n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1469}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_decode\_rows\_tested}}
a decode row says prime only after testing: a factor d ≥ 2 that is not prime has a prime factor m < d, so a divisor the reduction returns, such as 21 of 105, is split further. From Mathlib's `Nat.exists\_prime\_and\_dvd` and `lt\_of\_le\_of\_ne` (examples: algorithm-factor-order-composite)
\[ \forall (d : \mathbb{N}), 2 \le d \to \neg \mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ d \to \exists m, \mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ m \land m \mid d \land m < d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2111}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_denier\_identified}}
a denial's HTTP code at least 400 is recovered from its offset above 400: (code − 400) + 400 = code. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: legal-court-denier)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{code} : \mathbb{N}), 400 \le \mathrm{code} \to \mathrm{code} - 400 + 400 = \mathrm{code} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L586}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_denier\_identified\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_denier\_identified (examples: legal-court-denier)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{code} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{code} - 400 + 400 = \mathrm{code} \to \neg 400 \le \mathrm{code} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L591}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_denier\_identified\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_denier\_identified, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: legal-court-denier)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{code} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{code} - 400 + 400 = \mathrm{code} \to 400 \le \mathrm{code} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L593}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_denier\_identified\_reflected}}
textbook\_denier\_identified reflected by its shape (examples: legal-court-denier)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{code} : \mathbb{N}), 400 \le \mathrm{code} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{code} = \sigma - (\mathrm{code} - 400 + 400) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L589}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_deutsch\_jozsa}}
on a balanced Boolean function, true and false equally often, the signs (−1)\^{}f(x) cancel, so after one query the all-zero outcome has amplitude zero, which a constant function never gives. From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_ite`, `Finset.sum\_const`, `smul\_neg` and `neg\_add\_cancel` (examples: algorithm-deutsch-jozsa)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \alpha ] (f : \alpha \to \mathrm{Bool}), \{x | f\ x = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} = \{x | \neg f\ x = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} \to (\sum x, \mathrm{if}\ f\ x = \mathrm{true}\ \mathrm{then} -1\ \mathrm{else}\ 1) = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1198}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_deutsch\_jozsa\_reflected}}
textbook\_deutsch\_jozsa reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-deutsch-jozsa)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \alpha ] (f : \alpha \to \mathrm{Bool}), \{x | f\ x = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} = \{x | \neg f\ x = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} \to 0 = \sum x, \mathrm{if}\ f\ x = \mathrm{true}\ \mathrm{then} -1\ \mathrm{else}\ 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1202}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_difference\_leads}}
the leads a difference opens are the members on one side only: with a and b members sharing c, (a − c) + (b − c) = a + b − 2c. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_tsub\_comm` and `two\_mul`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `Nat.instAtLeastTwoHAddOfNat` (examples: audit-difference)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c : \mathbb{N}), c \le a \to c \le b \to a - c + (b - c) = a + b - 2 \cdot c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L532}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_difference\_leads\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_difference\_leads (examples: audit-difference)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c : \mathbb{N}), \neg a - c + (b - c) = a + b - 2 \cdot c \to \neg (c \le a \land c \le b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L538}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_difference\_leads\_reflected}}
textbook\_difference\_leads reflected by its shape (examples: audit-difference)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c : \mathbb{N}), c \le a \to c \le b \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (a + b - 2 \cdot c) = \sigma - (a - c + (b - c)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L536}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_digest\_on\_round\_trip}}
the digests are the round trips, which are among the checked calls: so the digests are at most the checked and the calls without one are the checked less the round trips, |C \textbackslash{} D| = |C| − |R|. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_le\_card` and `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` (examples: verify-call, verify-call-no-round-trip)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (D\ R\ C : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), D = R \to R \subseteq C \to \mathrm{D.card} \le \mathrm{C.card} \land (C \setminus D)\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{C.card} - \mathrm{R.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2131}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_digit\_reflection}}
each digit is linked to its reflection: for every d ≤ n, d + (n − d) = n and reflecting twice returns d (n = 10: 1↔9, 2↔8, 3↔7, 4↔6, 5 fixed). From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd` and `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ d : \mathbb{N}), d \le n \to d + (n - d) = n \land n - (n - d) = d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1665}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_digit\_reflection\_reflected}}
textbook\_digit\_reflection reflected by its shape (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ d : \mathbb{N}), d \le n \to n = d + (n - d) \land d = n - (n - d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1670}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_discovery\_requests}}
a requests of the unit's own and b verifier contracts make a + b discovery requests, the cardinality of a disjoint union (3 + 52 = 55). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: discover-requests)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L898}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_discovery\_requests\_reflected}}
textbook\_discovery\_requests reflected by its shape (examples: discover-requests)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a + b = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L903}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_div\_zero}}
division by zero is total: x / 0 = 0 and x \% 0 = x for every natural, z / 0 = 0 for every integer, and q / 0 = 0 in every group with zero (ℚ here); 0 has no inverse. From Lean core's `Nat.div\_zero`, `Nat.mod\_zero` and `Int.ediv\_zero`, and Mathlib's `div\_zero` (examples: match-div-zero)
\[ \forall (x : \mathbb{N}) (z : \mathbb{Z}) (q : \mathbb{Q}), x / 0 = 0 \land x \bmod 0 = x \land z / 0 = 0 \land q / 0 = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2098}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_div\_zero\_reflected}}
textbook\_div\_zero reflected by its shape (examples: match-div-zero)
\[ \forall (x : \mathbb{N}) (z : \mathbb{Z}) (q : \mathbb{Q}) (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - x / 0 \land \sigma - x = \sigma - x \bmod 0 \land 0 = z / 0 \land 0 = q / 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2102}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dna\_base4}}
a pair of digits (hi, lo) in bases m and n is one digit lo + n · hi in base m · n, read back one for one: DNA's A, C, G, T are base 4, so two bases are one hexadecimal nibble (AC = 1, GT = 11) and a UUID of 32 hex holds 64 bases. From Mathlib's `finProdFinEquiv`, `Equiv.symm\_apply\_apply` and `finProdFinEquiv\_apply\_val` (examples: fold-dna, fold-dna-decode)
\[ \forall (m\ n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{Fin}\ m \times \mathrm{Fin}\ n), \mathrm{finProdFinEquiv.symm} (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) = x \land \uparrow (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) = \uparrow \mathrm{x.2} + n \cdot \uparrow \mathrm{x.1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1692}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_dna\_base4\_reflected}}
textbook\_dna\_base4 reflected by its shape (examples: fold-dna, fold-dna-decode)
\[ \forall (m\ n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{Fin}\ m \times \mathrm{Fin}\ n), x = \mathrm{finProdFinEquiv.symm} (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) \land \uparrow \mathrm{x.2} + n \cdot \uparrow \mathrm{x.1} = \uparrow (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1698}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_doi\_well\_formed}}
a DOI is the prefix 10. and a registrant of four digits or more, a slash and a suffix that is not empty (ISO 26324), so it has nine characters at least. From Mathlib's `add\_le\_add`, `add\_le\_add\_right` and `le\_rfl`, through the instances `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `covariant\_swap\_add\_of\_covariant\_add` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{registrant}\ \mathrm{suffix} : \mathbb{N}), 4 \le \mathrm{registrant} \to 1 \le \mathrm{suffix} \to 3 + 4 + 1 + 1 \le 3 + \mathrm{registrant} + 1 + \mathrm{suffix} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2297}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_double\_entry}}
when debits equal credits the journal nets to zero both ways: a number less itself is zero. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, through the instances `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: stripe-ledger, compete-economics, verify-radar)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{debits}\ \mathrm{credits} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{debits} = \mathrm{credits} \to \mathrm{debits} - \mathrm{credits} = 0 \land \mathrm{credits} - \mathrm{debits} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L110}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_double\_entry\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_double\_entry (examples: stripe-ledger, compete-economics, verify-radar)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{debits}\ \mathrm{credits} : \mathbb{N}), \neg (\mathrm{debits} - \mathrm{credits} = 0 \land \mathrm{credits} - \mathrm{debits} = 0) \to \neg \mathrm{debits} = \mathrm{credits} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L116}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_double\_entry\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_double\_entry, proved. From Mathlib's `le\_antisymm`, `tsub\_eq\_zero\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: stripe-ledger, compete-economics, verify-radar)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{debits}\ \mathrm{credits} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{debits} - \mathrm{credits} = 0 \land \mathrm{credits} - \mathrm{debits} = 0 \to \mathrm{debits} = \mathrm{credits} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L118}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_double\_entry\_reflected}}
textbook\_double\_entry reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-ledger, compete-economics, verify-radar)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{debits}\ \mathrm{credits} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{debits} = \mathrm{credits} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (\mathrm{debits} - \mathrm{credits}) \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (\mathrm{credits} - \mathrm{debits}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L114}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_double\_torus}}
every crossing is a finite step with always a next and one step back: n < n + 1 and n + 1 − 1 = n. From Mathlib's `lt\_add\_one` and `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instZeroLEOneClass`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: stripe-fusion, algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n < n + 1 \land n + 1 - 1 = n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1427}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_double\_torus\_reflected}}
textbook\_double\_torus reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-fusion, algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ \forall (n \sigma : \mathbb{N}), n + 1 \le \sigma \to \sigma - (n + 1) < \sigma - n \land \sigma - n = \sigma - (n + 1 - 1) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1430}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_e2e\_combos}}
requests sorted by the kind each belongs to number the sum of the kinds' sizes, |s| = Σ\_b |\{a ∈ s | f a = b\}| (clay's three, processor's three and roadmap's seven are thirteen requests; six dashboard cells and seven examples the same thirteen). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: e2e-requests)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \beta ] (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum b \in t, \{a \in s | f\ a = b\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L918}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_e2e\_combos\_reflected}}
textbook\_e2e\_combos reflected by its shape (examples: e2e-requests)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \beta ] (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum b \in t, \{a \in s | f\ a = b\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L924}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_e2e\_tree\_dissent}}
decodeE2e: any two strict majorities of the same voters share a voter, so the majority answer is unique, and the voters off a majority are the flips, their count the voters less the majority. From Mathlib's `Finset.inter\_nonempty\_of\_card\_lt\_card\_add\_card` and `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` (examples: e2e-tree-outvoted)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{voters}\ A\ B : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), A \subseteq \mathrm{voters} \to B \subseteq \mathrm{voters} \to \mathrm{voters.card} < 2 \cdot \mathrm{A.card} \to \mathrm{voters.card} < 2 \cdot \mathrm{B.card} \to (A \cap B)\mathrm{.Nonempty} \land (\mathrm{voters} \setminus A)\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{voters.card} - \mathrm{A.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2064}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_eastin\_knill}}
an element fixed by a choice among a images of X and an independent choice among b images of Z is a pair, so there are a · b of them (6 · 4 = 24 single-qubit Cliffords modulo phase). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_prod` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: algorithm-eastin-knill)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \times \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = a \cdot b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1281}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_eastin\_knill\_reflected}}
textbook\_eastin\_knill reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-eastin-knill)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a \cdot b = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \times \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1286}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_echo\_qubits\_within\_willow}}
qubits a figure uses within the qubits a text names within the processor's are counted in the same order, and the first lie within the last (65 ≤ 103 ≤ 105 on Willow). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_le\_card`, `Finset.Subset.trans` (examples: echo-benchmark)
\[ \forall \{Q : \mathrm{Type}\} (\mathrm{figure}\ \mathrm{text}\ \mathrm{willow} : \mathrm{Finset}\ Q), \mathrm{figure} \subseteq \mathrm{text} \to \mathrm{text} \subseteq \mathrm{willow} \to \mathrm{figure.card} \le \mathrm{text.card} \land \mathrm{text.card} \le \mathrm{willow.card} \land \mathrm{figure} \subseteq \mathrm{willow} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1156}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ed25519\_sizes}}
a signature made of two values of n bytes each, (R, S), is 2 · n bytes: |A ⊕ A| = 2|A| (Ed25519: a 32-byte public key, a 64-byte signature). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: seal-ed25519)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ n) = 2 \cdot n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2403}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ed25519\_sizes\_reflected}}
textbook\_ed25519\_sizes reflected by its shape (examples: seal-ed25519)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot n = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ n) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2408}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_edit\_collides}}
two edits of one document either share their base address or collide, the excluded middle: there is no third case. From Mathlib's `em` (examples: legal-court-collision)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (p\ q : \alpha ), p = q \lor p \ne q \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L527}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_edit\_collides\_reflected}}
textbook\_edit\_collides reflected by its shape (examples: legal-court-collision)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (p\ q : \alpha ), p \ne q \lor p = q \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L530}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_editorial\_posts}}
when the posts and the backlog make the items, the items less the backlog are the posts. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, through the instances `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: match-editorial)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{posts}\ \mathrm{backlog}\ \mathrm{items} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{posts} + \mathrm{backlog} = \mathrm{items} \to \mathrm{items} - \mathrm{backlog} = \mathrm{posts} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1978}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_editorial\_posts\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_editorial\_posts (examples: match-editorial)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{posts}\ \mathrm{backlog}\ \mathrm{items} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{items} - \mathrm{backlog} = \mathrm{posts} \to \neg \mathrm{posts} + \mathrm{backlog} = \mathrm{items} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1984}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_editorial\_posts\_reflected}}
textbook\_editorial\_posts reflected by its shape (examples: match-editorial)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{posts}\ \mathrm{backlog}\ \mathrm{items} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{posts} + \mathrm{backlog} = \mathrm{items} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{posts} = \sigma - (\mathrm{items} - \mathrm{backlog}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1982}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_entropy\_fusion}}
fusion never multiplies: f findings fused by d reactions leave f − d leads, never more than the findings (vacuum W = 1, S = k ln W = 0). From Mathlib's `tsub\_le\_self`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-entropy, audit-entropy-vacuum, fold-integers)
\[ \forall (f\ d : \mathbb{N}), f - d \le f \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L599}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_entropy\_fusion\_reflected}}
textbook\_entropy\_fusion reflected by its shape (examples: audit-entropy, audit-entropy-vacuum, fold-integers)
\[ \forall (f\ d \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - f \le \sigma - (f - d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L602}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_entry\_canonical}}
a document of n keys can be written in n! key orders, the permutations of its keys, the writes canonicalText folds into one entry. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_perm` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: mongo-insert-canonical)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Equiv.Perm} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n)) = \mathrm{n.factorial} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2069}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_entry\_canonical\_reflected}}
textbook\_entry\_canonical reflected by its shape (examples: mongo-insert-canonical)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{n.factorial} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Equiv.Perm} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2074}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_error\_remedies}}
the errors left and the errors remedied make the errors: e − r + r = e when r ≤ e. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-remedies)
\[ \forall (e\ r : \mathbb{N}), r \le e \to e - r + r = e \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L545}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_error\_remedies\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_error\_remedies (examples: audit-remedies)
\[ \forall (e\ r : \mathbb{N}), \neg e - r + r = e \to \neg r \le e \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L550}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_error\_remedies\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_error\_remedies, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-remedies)
\[ \forall (e\ r : \mathbb{N}), e - r + r = e \to r \le e \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L552}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_error\_remedies\_reflected}}
textbook\_error\_remedies reflected by its shape (examples: audit-remedies)
\[ \forall (e\ r : \mathbb{N}), r \le e \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - e = \sigma - (e - r + r) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L548}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_eta\_from\_receipts}}
exponential smoothing weighs a time a/q and the level (q − a)/q, the weights summing to q, so equal recorded times x forecast exactly x: a·x + (q − a)·x = q·x. From Mathlib's `add\_mul`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `Distrib.rightDistribClass`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-status-eta, repository-status-forecast)
\[ \forall (a\ q\ x : \mathbb{N}), a \le q \to a \cdot x + (q - a) \cdot x = q \cdot x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1498}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_eta\_from\_receipts\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_eta\_from\_receipts (examples: repository-status-eta, repository-status-forecast)
\[ \forall (a\ q\ x : \mathbb{N}), \neg a \cdot x + (q - a) \cdot x = q \cdot x \to \neg a \le q \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1504}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_eta\_from\_receipts\_reflected}}
textbook\_eta\_from\_receipts reflected by its shape (examples: repository-status-eta, repository-status-forecast)
\[ \forall (a\ q\ x : \mathbb{N}), a \le q \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - q \cdot x = \sigma - (a \cdot x + (q - a) \cdot x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1502}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_euler\_fermat}}
Fermat's little theorem: a\^{}(p − 1) = 1 in ZMod p for a prime p and every a ≠ 0, the textbook meaning of the clay relations over powMod. From Mathlib's `ZMod.pow\_card\_sub\_one\_eq\_one` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (p : \mathbb{N}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fact} (\mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ p)] (a : \mathrm{ZMod}\ p), a \ne 0 \to a ^{(p - 1)} = 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1873}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_euler\_fermat\_reflected}}
textbook\_euler\_fermat reflected by its shape, equality (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (p : \mathbb{N}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fact} (\mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ p)] (a : \mathrm{ZMod}\ p), a \ne 0 \to 1 = a ^{(p - 1)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1878}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_euler\_totient}}
Euler's totient at twice an odd prime, φ(2p) = p − 1: φ(74) = 36, the degree of the cyclotomic factor Φ₇₄ of 2\^{}999 + 1 and the meaning of relation\_36. From Mathlib's `Nat.totient\_mul`, `Nat.coprime\_primes`, `Nat.prime\_two`, `Nat.totient\_two`, `Nat.totient\_prime` and `one\_mul` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (p : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ p \to p \ne 2 \to (2 \cdot p)\mathrm{.totient} = p - 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1886}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_euler\_totient\_reflected}}
textbook\_euler\_totient reflected by its shape, equality (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (p : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ p \to p \ne 2 \to p - 1 = (2 \cdot p)\mathrm{.totient} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1891}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_exploit\_refuses\_to\_patch}}
in any cancellative additive monoid (counts among them) the served space is unchanged exactly when the downtime is zero: s + d = s ↔ d = 0, the patch with no reboot. From Mathlib's `add\_eq\_left` (examples: match-exploit-repair)
\[ \forall \{M : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddMonoid}\ M] [\mathrm{IsLeftCancelAdd}\ M] (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{downtime} : M), \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{downtime} = \mathrm{served} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{downtime} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L576}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_exploit\_refuses\_to\_patch\_reflected}}
textbook\_exploit\_refuses\_to\_patch reflected by its shape (examples: match-exploit-repair)
\[ \forall \{M : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddMonoid}\ M] [\mathrm{IsLeftCancelAdd}\ M] (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{downtime} : M), \mathrm{downtime} = 0 \leftrightarrow \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{downtime} = \mathrm{served} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L581}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_expressions\_evaluate}}
every textbook expression is a tool: when the held statements are the closed ones, none disagrees, the closed less the held being none. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: textbook-expressions, textbook-prose)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{closed}\ \mathrm{held} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{held} = \mathrm{closed} \to \mathrm{closed} - \mathrm{held} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1445}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_expressions\_evaluate\_reflected}}
textbook\_expressions\_evaluate reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-expressions, textbook-prose)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{closed}\ \mathrm{held} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{held} = \mathrm{closed} \to 0 = \mathrm{closed} - \mathrm{held} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1449}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_extend\_inward}}
the tree's own tools plus the one door the docs add, less the tree's own, is that one door, whatever the number of tools. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left`, through the instances `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: extend-in-docs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{mine} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{mine} + 1 - \mathrm{mine} = 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1071}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_extend\_inward\_reflected}}
textbook\_extend\_inward reflected by its shape (examples: extend-in-docs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{mine} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 1 = \sigma - (\mathrm{mine} + 1 - \mathrm{mine}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1074}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_factor\_parse\_list}}
listFactors returns the prime factorisation: for every n ≠ 0 the list of prime factors multiplies back to n and each is prime, fifteen listing as three times five. From Mathlib's `Nat.prod\_primeFactorsList` and `Nat.prime\_of\_mem\_primeFactorsList` (examples: algorithm-factor-15, algorithm-one-door)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to \mathrm{n.primeFactorsList.prod} = n \land \forall p \in \mathrm{n.primeFactorsList}, \mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ p \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1602}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_factorial}}
the factorial (n + 1)! = (n + 1) · n!, the textbook meaning of the clay relations over factorial. From Mathlib's `Nat.factorial\_succ` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (n + 1)\mathrm{.factorial} = (n + 1) \cdot \mathrm{n.factorial} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1899}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_factorial\_reflected}}
textbook\_factorial reflected by its shape, equality (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (n + 1) \cdot \mathrm{n.factorial} = (n + 1)\mathrm{.factorial} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1903}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_factors\_divide}}
a part found of an integer, multiplied by the rest, gives the integer back, so it divides it: f · c = n implies f ∣ n. From Mathlib's `Dvd.intro` (examples: algorithm-factor-1000-bit, algorithm-factor-observe, algorithm-factor-mersenne)
\[ \forall (f\ c\ n : \mathbb{N}), f \cdot c = n \to f \mid n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1599}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fallback\_involution}}
correction and handling are each other's fallback: the swap is an involution, applied twice it is the identity, so the fallback terminates. From Mathlib's `Bool.involutive\_not` (examples: audit-fallbacks)
\[ \mathrm{Function.Involutive}\ \mathrm{not} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L574}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fibonacci}}
the Fibonacci recurrence F(n + 2) = F(n) + F(n + 1) for every n (5 + 8 = 13, 8 + 13 = 21, 13 + 21 = 34). From Mathlib's `Nat.fib\_add\_two` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Nat.fib} (n + 2) = \mathrm{Nat.fib}\ n + \mathrm{Nat.fib} (n + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1893}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fibonacci\_reflected}}
textbook\_fibonacci reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Nat.fib}\ n + \mathrm{Nat.fib} (n + 1) = \mathrm{Nat.fib} (n + 2) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1897}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fold\_mirror}}
piece i and its mirror n − i sum to the boundary n. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: fold-ledger)
\[ \forall (n\ i : \mathbb{N}), i \le n \to i + (n - i) = n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L71}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fold\_mirror\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_fold\_mirror (examples: fold-ledger)
\[ \forall (n\ i : \mathbb{N}), \neg i + (n - i) = n \to \neg i \le n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L76}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fold\_mirror\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_fold\_mirror, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: fold-ledger)
\[ \forall (n\ i : \mathbb{N}), i + (n - i) = n \to i \le n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L78}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fold\_mirror\_reflected}}
textbook\_fold\_mirror reflected by its shape (examples: fold-ledger)
\[ \forall (n\ i : \mathbb{N}), i \le n \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - n = \sigma - (i + (n - i)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L74}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fold\_pieces\_under\_bound}}
pieces each within a bound B total at most their number times B, so a text of length L folds into at least L / B pieces (the 40-byte text into four pieces of at most 12, 40 ≤ 4 · 12). From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_le\_card\_nsmul`, `smul\_eq\_mul`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: match-pattern-fold)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\} (\mathrm{pieces} : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (\mathrm{len} : \iota \to \mathbb{N}) (B : \mathbb{N}), (\forall i \in \mathrm{pieces}, \mathrm{len}\ i \le B) \to \sum i \in \mathrm{pieces}, \mathrm{len}\ i \le \mathrm{pieces.card} \cdot B \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1389}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_fold\_slices}}
slices of a text that never overlap have sizes summing to the whole, and k slices under a bound b hold at most k · b. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_biUnion` and `Finset.card\_biUnion\_le\_card\_mul` (examples: fold-slices-exact, fold-unfold-exact)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \iota \to \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), (\uparrow s)\mathrm{.PairwiseDisjoint}\ t \to \forall (b : \mathbb{N}), (\forall i \in s, (t\ i)\mathrm{.card} \le b) \to (\mathrm{s.biUnion}\ t)\mathrm{.card} = \sum i \in s, (t\ i)\mathrm{.card} \land (\mathrm{s.biUnion}\ t)\mathrm{.card} \le \mathrm{s.card} \cdot b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2076}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_foreign\_door\_name}}
a foreign door is named by its host, a colon and its tool: docs.mcp.cloudflare.com (23 characters), ':' and search\_cloudflare\_documentation (31), fifty-five characters (examples: extend-call-served)
\[ 23 + 1 + 31 = 55 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1076}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_foreign\_door\_name\_reflected}}
textbook\_foreign\_door\_name reflected by its shape, equality (examples: extend-call-served)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 55 = \sigma - (23 + 1 + 31) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1078}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_foreign\_verdicts}}
when each foreign word maps to one verdict of this tree, the words number the sum over the verdicts of the words mapped to each (2 served, 1 impossible, 3 unknown: 6). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: match-foreign-verdict)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L744}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_foreign\_verdicts\_reflected}}
textbook\_foreign\_verdicts reflected by its shape (examples: match-foreign-verdict)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L749}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_free\_or\_coil}}
every call is free or a coil: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when the free calls are among the calls, the coils and the free make the calls, (c − f) + f = c. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le` (examples: metrics-bill, repository-penalty-way)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{calls}\ \mathrm{free} : \alpha ), \mathrm{free} \le \mathrm{calls} \to \mathrm{calls} - \mathrm{free} + \mathrm{free} = \mathrm{calls} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1996}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_free\_or\_coil\_reflected}}
textbook\_free\_or\_coil reflected by its shape (examples: metrics-bill, repository-penalty-way)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{calls}\ \mathrm{free} : \alpha ), \mathrm{free} \le \mathrm{calls} \to \mathrm{calls} = \mathrm{calls} - \mathrm{free} + \mathrm{free} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2001}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ftqc\_t\_counts}}
the six circuits cost 68 T gates: the QFT two controlled-S at three, Grover one Toffoli at seven, teleportation one T, Shor seven controlled-S at three, QAOA twelve T, the repetition cycle three Toffolis at seven (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ 2 \cdot 3 + 1 \cdot 7 + 1 \cdot 1 + 7 \cdot 3 + 12 \cdot 1 + 3 \cdot 7 = 68 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1353}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ftqc\_t\_counts\_reflected}}
textbook\_ftqc\_t\_counts reflected by its shape, equality (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 68 = \sigma - (2 \cdot 3 + 1 \cdot 7 + 1 \cdot 1 + 7 \cdot 3 + 12 \cdot 1 + 3 \cdot 7) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1355}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gauss\_triangular}}
Gauss's triangular sum: twice 0 + 1 + … + n is (n + 1) · n (2·T(4) = 4·5, 2·T(8) = 8·9, 2·T(7) = 7·8), the textbook meaning of the clay relations triangular k = qftGates k. From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_range\_id\_mul\_two`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (\sum i \in \mathrm{Finset.range} (n + 1), i) \cdot 2 = (n + 1) \cdot n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1860}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gauss\_triangular\_reflected}}
textbook\_gauss\_triangular reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (n + 1) \cdot n = (\sum i \in \mathrm{Finset.range} (n + 1), i) \cdot 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1865}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_glagolitic\_fifteen}}
the Glagolitic numeral Ⰺⰴ is 10 + 5 = 15, factored 3 × 5 in the reflection (examples: match-glagolitic)
\[ 10 + 5 = 15 \land 3 \cdot 5 = 15 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1381}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_glagolitic\_fifteen\_reflected}}
textbook\_glagolitic\_fifteen reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: match-glagolitic)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 15 = \sigma - (10 + 5) \land \sigma - 15 = \sigma - 3 \cdot 5 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1383}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_glagolitic\_round\_trip}}
a hex digit above nine is spoken as Ⰺ (ten) and a unit and read back by adding them: 10 + (d − 10) = d for every d ≥ 10, so every hex digit returns as itself (eleven to fifteen). From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: fold-speak)
\[ \forall (d : \mathbb{N}), 10 \le d \to 10 + (d - 10) = d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L303}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_glagolitic\_round\_trip\_reflected}}
textbook\_glagolitic\_round\_trip reflected by its shape (examples: fold-speak)
\[ \forall (d : \mathbb{N}), 10 \le d \to d = 10 + (d - 10) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L307}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gltf\_required\_extensions}}
a .glb begins with a twelve-byte header (the magic glTF, 0x46546C67, the version and the length) and each chunk with eight bytes (its length and its type, JSON 0x4E4F534A or BIN); seal \{ gltf \} loads an asset unless it requires an extension the table does not list (examples: seal-gltf-glb, seal-gltf-json)
\[ 12 + 8 = 20 \land 1179937895 = 1179937895 \land 1313821514 = 1313821514 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L790}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gltf\_required\_extensions\_reflected}}
textbook\_gltf\_required\_extensions reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: seal-gltf-glb, seal-gltf-json)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 20 = \sigma - (12 + 8) \land \sigma - 1179937895 = \sigma - 1179937895 \land \sigma - 1313821514 = \sigma - 1313821514 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L792}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_golay\_code}}
a code of r parity bits has 2\^{}r syndromes, one per pattern of the parity checks (the Golay code [23, 12, 7]: 23 − 12 = 11 parity bits, 2\^{}11 = 2048 syndromes). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_bool`, `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall (r : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ r \to \mathrm{Bool}) = 2 ^{r} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1847}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_golay\_code\_reflected}}
textbook\_golay\_code reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall (r : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{r} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ r \to \mathrm{Bool}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1852}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_api\_surfaces}}
one gate per API: a one-to-one correspondence between two finite collections makes them equally many (three Google APIs, three gates). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_congr`
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype} \beta ] (e : \alpha \simeq \beta ), \mathrm{Fintype.card} \alpha = \mathrm{Fintype.card} \beta  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L728}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_api\_surfaces\_reflected}}
textbook\_google\_api\_surfaces reflected by its shape
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype} \beta ] (e : \alpha \simeq \beta ), \mathrm{Fintype.card} \beta = \mathrm{Fintype.card} \alpha  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L734}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_call\_schema}}
a Google call runs exactly when its violations sum to none, that is when each kind is none: a sum of naturals is zero iff every summand is. From Mathlib's `add\_eq\_zero` and `Unique.instSubsingleton` (examples: google-call-validated, google-call-endpoint, google-call-refused)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{unknown}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{broken} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{unknown} + \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{broken} = 0 \leftrightarrow \mathrm{unknown} = 0 \land \mathrm{missing} = 0 \land \mathrm{broken} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L857}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_call\_schema\_reflected}}
textbook\_google\_call\_schema reflected by its shape (examples: google-call-validated, google-call-endpoint, google-call-refused)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{unknown}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{broken} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{unknown} = 0 \land \mathrm{missing} = 0 \land \mathrm{broken} = 0 \leftrightarrow \mathrm{unknown} + \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{broken} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L861}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_mcps}}
a servers at the registry and b toolboxes make a + b, the cardinality of a disjoint union. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin`
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L618}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_mcps\_reflected}}
textbook\_google\_mcps reflected by its shape
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a + b = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L623}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_measured}}
the calls answered and the calls refused partition the calls, so the calls less the refused are the answered ones. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` (examples: google-measured)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Call} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Call}] (\mathrm{calls}\ \mathrm{refused} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Call}), \mathrm{refused} \subseteq \mathrm{calls} \to (\mathrm{calls} \setminus \mathrm{refused})\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{calls.card} - \mathrm{refused.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L501}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_measured\_reflected}}
textbook\_google\_measured reflected by its shape (examples: google-measured)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Call} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Call}] (\mathrm{calls}\ \mathrm{refused} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Call}), \mathrm{refused} \subseteq \mathrm{calls} \to \mathrm{calls.card} - \mathrm{refused.card} = (\mathrm{calls} \setminus \mathrm{refused})\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L505}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_one\_door}}
a commit holds the rule google-via-payload exactly when its direct Google calls are zero, that is when they are not positive. From Mathlib's `not\_lt` (examples: repository-google-via-payload, repository-genuine, repository-doored, textbook-rewrite)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{direct} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{direct} = 0 \leftrightarrow \neg 0 < \mathrm{direct} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L851}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_google\_one\_door\_reflected}}
textbook\_google\_one\_door reflected by its shape (examples: repository-google-via-payload, repository-genuine, repository-doored, textbook-rewrite)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{direct} : \mathbb{N}), \neg 0 < \mathrm{direct} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{direct} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L855}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gottesman\_knill}}
the size remark accompanying Gottesman–Knill: n stabilizer generators of 2n + 1 bits each, n(2n + 1) = 2n² + n bits (Aaronson–Gottesman 2004, §III), are fewer than the 2ⁿ amplitudes of the state for every n ≥ 7, the bound sharp (210 < 1024 at n = 10); an instance of polynomial below exponential, proved by Mathlib's `Nat.le\_induction`; the update theorem itself is not stated here (examples: algorithm-gottesman-knill)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 7 \le n \to 2 \cdot (n \cdot n) + n < 2 ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1266}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gravity\_torus\_sigma}}
the reflection σₙ(x) = n − x is an involution on 0 to n, and the half-turn c is the fixed centre of σ₂꜀ (σ₃₆₀ fixes 180); a bridge-strength statement, not a physics claim. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le` and `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd` and `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x\ c : \mathbb{N}), x \le n \to n - (n - x) = x \land c + c - c = c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1700}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gravity\_torus\_sigma\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_gravity\_torus\_sigma (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x\ c : \mathbb{N}), \neg (n - (n - x) = x \land c + c - c = c) \to \neg x \le n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1707}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gravity\_torus\_sigma\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_gravity\_torus\_sigma, proved. From Mathlib's `min\_eq\_right\_iff`, `tsub\_tsub\_eq\_min`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x\ c : \mathbb{N}), n - (n - x) = x \land c + c - c = c \to x \le n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1709}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_gravity\_torus\_sigma\_reflected}}
textbook\_gravity\_torus\_sigma reflected by its shape (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x\ c : \mathbb{N}), x \le n \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - x = \sigma - (n - (n - x)) \land \sigma - c = \sigma - (c + c - c) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1705}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_grover\_one\_iteration}}
