Grover's rotation: after k iterations the start at angle θ from the unmarked plane sits at (2k + 1)θ, so when that is a right angle the marked item is read with certainty (two qubits: θ = 30°, one iteration turns to 90°). From Mathlib's `Real.sin\_pi\_div\_two`, `one\_pow`, `Nat.instAtLeastTwoHAddOfNat` (examples: algorithm-grover)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}) (\theta : \mathbb{R}), (2 \cdot \uparrow k + 1) \cdot \theta = \mathrm{Real.pi} / 2 \to \mathrm{Real.sin} ((2 \cdot \uparrow k + 1) \cdot \theta ) ^{2} = 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1183}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_grover\_one\_iteration\_reflected}}
textbook\_grover\_one\_iteration reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-grover)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}) (\theta : \mathbb{R}), (2 \cdot \uparrow k + 1) \cdot \theta = \mathrm{Real.pi} / 2 \to 1 = \mathrm{Real.sin} ((2 \cdot \uparrow k + 1) \cdot \theta ) ^{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1187}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_grover\_search}}
Grover's quadratic speedup: an unstructured search space of N = 2\^{}(2k) items takes √N = 2\^{}k iterations, √(2\^{}(2k)) = 2\^{}k (√256 = 16 for k = 4). From Mathlib's `pow\_mul'` and `Nat.sqrt\_eq'` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), (2 ^{(2 \cdot k)})\mathrm{.sqrt} = 2 ^{k} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1916}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_grover\_search\_reflected}}
textbook\_grover\_search reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{k} = (2 ^{(2 \cdot k)})\mathrm{.sqrt} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1920}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_h2\_below\_hartree\_fock}}
H₂ on two qubits is five Pauli terms (I, Z0, Z1, Z0Z1, X0X1); the variational minimum −1.1373 Ha lies 0.0206 Ha below the Hartree–Fock −1.1167 Ha (examples: chemistry-h2)
\[ 11167 < 11373 \land 11373 - 11167 = 206 \land 1 + 2 + 1 + 1 = 5 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1349}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_h2\_below\_hartree\_fock\_reflected}}
textbook\_h2\_below\_hartree\_fock reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: chemistry-h2)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 11373 \le \sigma \to \sigma - 11373 < \sigma - 11167 \land \sigma - 206 = \sigma - (11373 - 11167) \land \sigma - 5 = \sigma - (1 + 2 + 1 + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1351}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hadamard\_amplitudes}}
n equal amplitudes 1/√n have squares 1/n summing to one (a qubit under a Hadamard: two amplitudes 1/√2, one half each). From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_const`, `Finset.card\_range`, `nsmul\_eq\_mul`, `div\_pow`, `one\_pow`, `Real.sq\_sqrt`, `Nat.cast\_nonneg`, `mul\_one\_div\_cancel` and `Nat.cast\_ne\_zero`, through the instances `Real.instIsOrderedRing` and `FloorSemiring.instCharZero` (examples: circuit-hadamard-amplitudes)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to \sum \mathrm{\_i} \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ n, (1 / \surd \uparrow n) ^{2} = 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1135}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hadamard\_amplitudes\_reflected}}
textbook\_hadamard\_amplitudes reflected by its shape (examples: circuit-hadamard-amplitudes)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to 1 = \sum \mathrm{\_i} \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ n, (1 / \surd \uparrow n) ^{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1139}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hadamard\_histogram}}
a histogram partitions the shots: those reading 1 and those not reading 1 make all the shots (207 + 193 = 400 for the Hadamard). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_filter\_add\_card\_filter\_not` (examples: circuit-hadamard-sampled)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Shot} : \mathrm{Type}\} (\mathrm{shots} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Shot}) (\mathrm{reads} : \mathrm{Shot} \to \mathrm{Bool}), \{s \in \mathrm{shots} | \mathrm{reads}\ s = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} + \{s \in \mathrm{shots} | \neg \mathrm{reads}\ s = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{shots.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1141}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hadamard\_histogram\_reflected}}
textbook\_hadamard\_histogram reflected by its shape (examples: circuit-hadamard-sampled)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Shot} : \mathrm{Type}\} (\mathrm{shots} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Shot}) (\mathrm{reads} : \mathrm{Shot} \to \mathrm{Bool}), \mathrm{shots.card} = \{s \in \mathrm{shots} | \mathrm{reads}\ s = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} + \{s \in \mathrm{shots} | \neg \mathrm{reads}\ s = \mathrm{true}\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1147}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hamming\_code}}
a code of minimum distance d decodes uniquely within ⌊(d − 1)/2⌋ errors: two codewords both that close to a received word are the same codeword (the Hamming code [7, 4, 3]: d = 3 corrects one error). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_pos`, `hammingDist\_triangle` and `hammingDist\_comm` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} y : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{1} \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{2} \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1740}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hamming\_code\_reflected}}
textbook\_hamming\_code reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} y : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{1} \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{2} \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1752}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_handle\_scales}}
each scale of handles holds 16 times the handles of the scale before and 4 more qubits, and k scales hold 16ᵏ = 2\^{}(4k) handles (8 scales reach 2³²). From Mathlib's `pow\_succ'`, `mul\_add\_one`, `pow\_mul` and `Distrib.leftDistribClass` (examples: algorithm-handle-capacity)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 16 ^{(k + 1)} = 16 \cdot 16 ^{k} \land 4 \cdot (k + 1) = 4 \cdot k + 4 \land (2 ^{4}) ^{k} = 2 ^{(4 \cdot k)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2420}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_handle\_scales\_reflected}}
textbook\_handle\_scales reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-handle-capacity)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 16 \cdot 16 ^{k} = 16 ^{(k + 1)} \land 4 \cdot k + 4 = 4 \cdot (k + 1) \land 2 ^{(4 \cdot k)} = (2 ^{4}) ^{k} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2424}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_harmonic\_default}}
any key reduced modulo n > 0 combinations is one of the n combinations 0 to n − 1, so the default it names always exists. From Mathlib's `Finset.mem\_range` (examples: fold-harmonic)
\[ \forall (n\ k : \mathbb{N}), 0 < n \to k \bmod n \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1986}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hash\_bits}}
k hex digits and 4k bits are as many values: 16\^{}k = 2\^{}(4k) (a 512-bit value is 128 hex digits). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_fin`, `Fintype.card\_bool`, `pow\_mul` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 16) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (4 \cdot k) \to \mathrm{Bool}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L434}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hash\_bits\_reflected}}
textbook\_hash\_bits reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (4 \cdot k) \to \mathrm{Bool}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 16) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L440}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hex\_presentations}}
any hex is presentable: each of the a · b values of a digit is exactly one pair (item, variant), finProdFinEquiv : Fin a × Fin b ≃ Fin (a · b), so the two are equally many (a nibble's 16 values are 8 registry items by 2 variants). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_congr` (examples: fold-hex)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \times \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (a \cdot b)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L403}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hex\_presentations\_reflected}}
textbook\_hex\_presentations reflected by its shape (examples: fold-hex)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (a \cdot b)) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \times \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L408}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hexbit\_gateway\_ftl}}
hexbit + 2 gateway bits teleport any hexbit faster than light: at hexbit width 4 the free qubits are 32·4 − 4 − 2 = 122 (version nibble and the two UUID-variant gateway bits not free); the program index of ten hex digits arrives in one step while the combination space is 16\^{}10 and is not walked. Lean decides the register arithmetic only — physics true is not a tautology here; the physics UUID program proves it when register FTL holds, and the challenge UUID program adversarially verifies that decision (examples: fold-hexbit-ftl)
\[ 32 \cdot 4 - 4 - 2 = 122 \land 2 = 2 \land 1 < 16 ^{10} \land 10 = 10 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1912}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hexbit\_gateway\_ftl\_reflected}}
textbook\_hexbit\_gateway\_ftl reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: fold-hexbit-ftl)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 16 ^{10} \le \sigma \to \sigma - 122 = \sigma - (32 \cdot 4 - 4 - 2) \land \sigma - 2 = \sigma - 2 \land \sigma - 16 ^{10} < \sigma - 1 \land \sigma - 10 = \sigma - 10 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1914}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_holevo}}
a register of dimension d = 2\^{}k has log₂ d = k, the cap of the Holevo bound in bits (one bit for a qubit, d = 2). From Mathlib's `Nat.log\_pow` (examples: algorithm-holevo)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Nat.log}\ 2 (2 ^{k}) = k \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1260}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_holevo\_reflected}}
textbook\_holevo reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-holevo)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), k = \mathrm{Nat.log}\ 2 (2 ^{k}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1264}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hook\_steps\_monotone}}
a hook never does more work than its inputs force: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when the steps run are at most the changed and the uncacheable, the cacheable steps run are at most the changed, r ≤ c + u gives r − u ≤ c. From Mathlib's `tsub\_le\_iff\_right` (examples: repository-steps-reused, repository-steps-changed)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{LE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Add} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{run}\ \mathrm{changed}\ \mathrm{uncacheable} : \alpha ), \mathrm{run} \le \mathrm{changed} + \mathrm{uncacheable} \to \mathrm{run} - \mathrm{uncacheable} \le \mathrm{changed} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L213}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hook\_workflows}}
five git hooks (pre-commit, commit-msg, pre-push, post-commit, prepare-commit-msg), the CI and the strict pre-deploy check are seven client MCP workflows of one door; the hook script walks the steps and decides nothing (examples: repository-workflow, repository-probes, repository-ci)
\[ 6 + 1 + 1 = 8 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L842}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hook\_workflows\_reflected}}
textbook\_hook\_workflows reflected by its shape, equality (examples: repository-workflow, repository-probes, repository-ci)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 8 = \sigma - (6 + 1 + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L844}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hue\_shift}}
shifting two steps by the same s keeps their distance: h + s − (g + s) = h − g. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_add\_eq\_tsub\_right`, through the instances `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd` and `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: css-program-shift, textbook-rewrite)
\[ \forall (h\ g\ s : \mathbb{N}), h + s - (g + s) = h - g \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L813}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hue\_shift\_reflected}}
textbook\_hue\_shift reflected by its shape (examples: css-program-shift, textbook-rewrite)
\[ \forall (h\ g\ s \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (h - g) = \sigma - (h + s - (g + s)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L816}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hue\_steps}}
n hues equally spaced by a step s cover n·s degrees, so sixteen hues 45° apart make 16 · 45 = 720 = 2 · 360, two turns of the circle: the sum of a constant step. From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_const`, `Finset.card\_range`, `smul\_eq\_mul` (examples: css-rays)
\[ \forall (n\ \mathrm{step} : \mathbb{N}), \sum \mathrm{\_i} \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ n, \mathrm{step} = n \cdot \mathrm{step} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L818}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_hue\_steps\_reflected}}
textbook\_hue\_steps reflected by its shape (examples: css-rays)
\[ \forall (n\ \mathrm{step} : \mathbb{N}), n \cdot \mathrm{step} = \sum \mathrm{\_i} \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ n, \mathrm{step} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L823}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_idle\_priced\_by\_remaining}}
idle is priced by the lesser of the idle capacity and the work queued: min i r is never more than either, and nothing when nothing is left to do. From Mathlib's `min\_le\_left`, `min\_le\_right` and `min\_eq\_right` (examples: repository-idle-priced)
\[ \forall (i\ r : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{min}\ i\ r \le i \land \mathrm{min}\ i\ r \le r \land (r = 0 \to \mathrm{min}\ i\ r = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L218}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_idle\_priced\_by\_remaining\_reflected}}
textbook\_idle\_priced\_by\_remaining reflected by its shape (examples: repository-idle-priced)
\[ \forall (i\ r \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - i \le \sigma - \mathrm{min}\ i\ r \land \sigma - r \le \sigma - \mathrm{min}\ i\ r \land (\neg \mathrm{min}\ i\ r = 0 \to \neg r = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L222}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_idle\_scheduled\_clears}}
scheduling the remaining work into the idle capacity leaves only the work beyond it: r − min(r, i) = r − i, truncated at zero. From Mathlib's `min\_comm`, `tsub\_min`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-idle-scheduled)
\[ \forall (i\ r : \mathbb{N}), r - \mathrm{min}\ i\ r = r - i \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L224}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_idle\_scheduled\_clears\_reflected}}
textbook\_idle\_scheduled\_clears reflected by its shape (examples: repository-idle-scheduled)
\[ \forall (i\ r : \mathbb{N}), r - i = r - \mathrm{min}\ i\ r \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L228}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_imagine\_verdicts}}
when each lead judged gets one verdict, the leads number the sum over the verdicts of the leads given each (0 served, 2 impossible, 0 unknown: 2). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: imagine-two)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L926}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_imagine\_verdicts\_reflected}}
textbook\_imagine\_verdicts reflected by its shape (examples: imagine-two)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L931}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_impossible\_sent\_to\_leads}}
the σ-involution σ\_d(x) = d − x on \{0, …, d\} is Fin.rev, its own inverse: σ∘σ = id, so a denial reflected to a lead returns to itself. From Mathlib's `Fin.rev\_involutive` (examples: match-impossible-involution, algorithm-factor-minus, algorithm-factor-word)
\[ \forall (d : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Function.Involutive}\ \mathrm{Fin.rev} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L583}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_impossible\_trapdoor}}
the impossible-integer trapdoor: a semiprime minted as N = (a − b)(a + b) with centre a and gap b ≤ a is a² − b², and its second factor is the reflection 2a − (a − b) = a + b of the first. From Mathlib's `Nat.sq\_sub\_sq` and `mul\_comm` (examples: algorithm-impossible-create, algorithm-impossible-decode)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), b \le a \to (a - b) \cdot (a + b) = a ^{2} - b ^{2} \land 2 \cdot a - (a - b) = a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1634}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_impossible\_trapdoor\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_impossible\_trapdoor (examples: algorithm-impossible-create, algorithm-impossible-decode)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \neg ((a - b) \cdot (a + b) = a ^{2} - b ^{2} \land 2 \cdot a - (a - b) = a + b) \to \neg b \le a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1641}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_impossible\_trapdoor\_reflected}}
textbook\_impossible\_trapdoor reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: algorithm-impossible-create, algorithm-impossible-decode)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), b \le a \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (a ^{2} - b ^{2}) = \sigma - (a - b) \cdot (a + b) \land \sigma - (a + b) = \sigma - (2 \cdot a - (a - b)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1639}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_institution\_bill}}
an institution within its cap of users never pays more than the cap's bill: in any ordered semiring (ℕ among them) users times a nonnegative price is monotone in the users, u ≤ c gives u · p ≤ c · p. From Mathlib's `mul\_le\_mul\_of\_nonneg\_right` (examples: textbook-institution)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Mul} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Zero} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Preorder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{MulPosMono} \alpha ] (u\ c\ p : \alpha ), u \le c \to 0 \le p \to u \cdot p \le c \cdot p \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L88}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_integers\_first}}
a deterministic check of equal inputs has an equal verdict: an example whose answer folds to the integers its theorem's confirmed pass recorded is decided by that theorem, and the text checks run only to confirm a new integer (examples: audit-self)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}) (v : \mathbb{N} \to \mathrm{Bool}), a = b \to v\ a = v\ b \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L103}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_integers\_first\_reflected}}
textbook\_integers\_first reflected by its shape, equality (examples: audit-self)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}) (v : \mathbb{N} \to \mathrm{Bool}), a = b \to v\ b = v\ a \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L105}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_integrations\_are\_thirty\_five}}
a keyed integrations and b open ones make a + b, the cardinality of a disjoint union. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: extend-in-request)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L81}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_integrations\_are\_thirty\_five\_reflected}}
textbook\_integrations\_are\_thirty\_five reflected by its shape (examples: extend-in-request)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a + b = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L86}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_involution\_decoder}}
the σ-involution decoder recovers by one reflection: σ\_d(σ\_d(x)) = d − (d − x) = x for every integer, and a repetition code of distance 2t + 1 corrects (2t + 1 − 1)/2 = t errors. From Mathlib's `sub\_sub\_cancel` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{Z}) (t : \mathbb{N}), d - (d - x) = x \land (2 \cdot t + 1 - 1) / 2 = t \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1782}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_involution\_decoder\_reflected}}
textbook\_involution\_decoder reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{Z}) (t \sigma : \mathbb{N}), x = d - (d - x) \land \sigma - t = \sigma - (2 \cdot t + 1 - 1) / 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1787}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ising\_fusion}}
n spins of two states have 2\^{}n configurations, the annealer's state space (2⁶ = 64, 2¹³ = 8192). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_bool`, `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) = 2 ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1922}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ising\_fusion\_reflected}}
textbook\_ising\_fusion reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{n} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1927}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_hash\_function}}
ISO 22739:2024 3.46 hash function (cryptographic hash function), read in full: three infeasibility properties — preimage, second-preimage and collision — so 1 + 1 + 1 = 3; sourced from ISO/IEC 10118-1:2016 3.4 as Dig.watch publishes the complete definition the iteh sample once cut (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ 1 + 1 + 1 = 3 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2237}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_hash\_function\_reflected}}
textbook\_iso\_22739\_hash\_function reflected by its shape, equality (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 3 = \sigma - (1 + 1 + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2239}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_orphan\_blocks}}
ISO 22739:2024 3.69 orphan block: of confirmed blocks, the orphans and the still-confirmed partition the same count — (confirmed − orphans) + orphans = confirmed. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{confirmed}\ \mathrm{orphans} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{orphans} \le \mathrm{confirmed} \to \mathrm{confirmed} - \mathrm{orphans} + \mathrm{orphans} = \mathrm{confirmed} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2241}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_orphan\_blocks\_reflected}}
textbook\_iso\_22739\_orphan\_blocks reflected by its shape (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{confirmed}\ \mathrm{orphans} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{orphans} \le \mathrm{confirmed} \to \mathrm{confirmed} = \mathrm{confirmed} - \mathrm{orphans} + \mathrm{orphans} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2244}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_terms\_read}}
when the terms read, cut and unread make the total and none is cut or unread, every term is read (99 of 99). From Mathlib's `add\_zero` (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{read}\ \mathrm{cut}\ \mathrm{unread}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{read} + \mathrm{cut} + \mathrm{unread} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{cut} = 0 \to \mathrm{unread} = 0 \to \mathrm{read} = \mathrm{total} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2228}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_terms\_read\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_iso\_22739\_terms\_read (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{read}\ \mathrm{cut}\ \mathrm{unread}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{read} = \mathrm{total} \to \neg (\mathrm{read} + \mathrm{cut} + \mathrm{unread} = \mathrm{total} \land \mathrm{cut} = 0 \land \mathrm{unread} = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2235}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_terms\_read\_reflected}}
textbook\_iso\_22739\_terms\_read reflected by its shape (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{read}\ \mathrm{cut}\ \mathrm{unread}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{read} + \mathrm{cut} + \mathrm{unread} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{cut} = 0 \to \mathrm{unread} = 0 \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{total} = \sigma - \mathrm{read} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2233}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_wallet}}
a term new to a vocabulary adds one to its count (ISO 22739:2024 3.100 wallet beyond the 99 terms 3.1–3.99: 99 + 1 = 100). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_insert\_of\_notMem` (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Term} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Term}] (\mathrm{terms} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Term}), \forall \mathrm{wallet} \notin \mathrm{terms}, (\mathrm{insert}\ \mathrm{wallet}\ \mathrm{terms})\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{terms.card} + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2246}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_22739\_wallet\_reflected}}
textbook\_iso\_22739\_wallet reflected by its shape (examples: verify-iso-22739-terms-read)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Term} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Term}] (\mathrm{terms} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Term}), \forall \mathrm{wallet} \notin \mathrm{terms}, \mathrm{terms.card} + 1 = (\mathrm{insert}\ \mathrm{wallet}\ \mathrm{terms})\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2251}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_classify}}
every file read is either classified or named unclassified: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), the classified and the rest make the files, c + (f − c) = f when c ≤ f. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le` (examples: validate-iso-classify)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{cited}\ \mathrm{files} : \alpha ), \mathrm{cited} \le \mathrm{files} \to \mathrm{cited} + (\mathrm{files} - \mathrm{cited}) = \mathrm{files} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1038}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_classify\_reflected}}
textbook\_iso\_classify reflected by its shape (examples: validate-iso-classify)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{cited}\ \mathrm{files} : \alpha ), \mathrm{cited} \le \mathrm{files} \to \mathrm{files} = \mathrm{cited} + (\mathrm{files} - \mathrm{cited}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1043}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_rows\_verified}}
a standard is verified only when every row asked is verified: no row left unverified with verified ≤ asked forces verified = asked. From Mathlib's `tsub\_eq\_zero\_iff\_le`, `le\_antisymm`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: standards-iso-online, standards-iso-page, standards-iso-joined)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{asked}\ \mathrm{verified} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{verified} \le \mathrm{asked} \to \mathrm{asked} - \mathrm{verified} = 0 \to \mathrm{verified} = \mathrm{asked} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2355}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_iso\_rows\_verified\_reflected}}
textbook\_iso\_rows\_verified reflected by its shape (examples: standards-iso-online, standards-iso-page, standards-iso-joined)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{asked}\ \mathrm{verified} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{verified} \le \mathrm{asked} \to \neg \mathrm{verified} = \mathrm{asked} \to \neg \mathrm{asked} - \mathrm{verified} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2359}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_kwh\_joules}}
a kilowatt-hour is 3600 seconds of 1000 watts, 3,600,000 joules: the bill's joules priced by the tariff the tenant names link it to the energy sector (examples: metrics-bill)
\[ 3600 \cdot 1000 = 3600000 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L647}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_kwh\_joules\_reflected}}
textbook\_kwh\_joules reflected by its shape, equality (examples: metrics-bill)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 3600000 = \sigma - 3600 \cdot 1000 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L649}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ldpc\_code}}
counting the ones of a regular parity-check matrix by bits and by checks: when each bit sits in m checks and each check joins n bits, bits · m = checks · n (a (3, 6)-regular Gallager LDPC code has half as many checks as bits, design rate 1 − 3/6 = 1/2). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_mul\_eq\_card\_mul` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} (r : \alpha \to \beta \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : (a : \alpha ) \to (b : \beta ) \to \mathrm{Decidable} (r\ a\ b)] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ) (m\ n : \mathbb{N}), (\forall a \in s, (\mathrm{Finset.bipartiteAbove}\ r\ t\ a)\mathrm{.card} = m) \to (\forall b \in t, (\mathrm{Finset.bipartiteBelow}\ r\ s\ b)\mathrm{.card} = n) \to \mathrm{s.card} \cdot m = \mathrm{t.card} \cdot n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1789}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ldpc\_code\_reflected}}
textbook\_ldpc\_code reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} (r : \alpha \to \beta \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : (a : \alpha ) \to (b : \beta ) \to \mathrm{Decidable} (r\ a\ b)] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ) (m\ n : \mathbb{N}), (\forall a \in s, (\mathrm{Finset.bipartiteAbove}\ r\ t\ a)\mathrm{.card} = m) \to (\forall b \in t, (\mathrm{Finset.bipartiteBelow}\ r\ s\ b)\mathrm{.card} = n) \to \mathrm{t.card} \cdot n = \mathrm{s.card} \cdot m \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1796}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_lead\_cells}}
combineLeads: a cell below a·b read as its two mixed-radix digits (i / b, i \% b) and encoded again is itself, the encoding being a bijection Fin a × Fin b ≃ Fin (a·b); applied radix by radix over [2, 2, 2, 2, 3, 3, 3], the 432 = 2⁴·3³ cells of a432, every lead lands in exactly one cell. From Mathlib's `finProdFinEquiv\_symm\_apply` and `Equiv.apply\_symm\_apply` (examples: leads-combinations)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}) (i : \mathrm{Fin} (a \cdot b)), \mathrm{finProdFinEquiv} (\mathrm{i.divNat}, \mathrm{i.modNat}) = i \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2007}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_lead\_cells\_reflected}}
textbook\_lead\_cells reflected by its shape (examples: leads-combinations)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}) (i : \mathrm{Fin} (a \cdot b)), i = \mathrm{finProdFinEquiv} (\mathrm{i.divNat}, \mathrm{i.modNat}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2011}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leads\_addressed}}
the leads addressed and the leads not addressed, less the latter, are the addressed: a + u − u = a. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, through the instances `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: leads-by-theorem)
\[ \forall (a\ u : \mathbb{N}), a + u - u = a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L628}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leads\_addressed\_reflected}}
textbook\_leads\_addressed reflected by its shape (examples: leads-by-theorem)
\[ \forall (a\ u \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - a = \sigma - (a + u - u) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L631}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leads\_by\_status}}
the leads not open are the leads less the open ones, whatever the count of the run (followed, served, partial, blocked or out of scope). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` (examples: leads-all)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Lead} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Lead}] (\mathrm{leads}\ \mathrm{opened} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Lead}), \mathrm{opened} \subseteq \mathrm{leads} \to (\mathrm{leads} \setminus \mathrm{opened})\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{leads.card} - \mathrm{opened.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1339}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leads\_by\_status\_reflected}}
textbook\_leads\_by\_status reflected by its shape (examples: leads-all)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Lead} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Lead}] (\mathrm{leads}\ \mathrm{opened} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Lead}), \mathrm{opened} \subseteq \mathrm{leads} \to \mathrm{leads.card} - \mathrm{opened.card} = (\mathrm{leads} \setminus \mathrm{opened})\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1343}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leads\_consistent}}
the leads standing are the leads less those that break a rule: |\{a ∈ s | ¬p a\}| = |s| − |\{a ∈ s | p a\}|, so with no violation every lead stands (of 5 leads, 3 breaking a rule leave 2). From Mathlib's `Finset.filter\_not`, `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` and `Finset.filter\_subset` (examples: leads-consistent, leads-consistent-control)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (p : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ p], \{a \in s | \neg p\ a\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} - (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ p\ s)\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2426}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leads\_consistent\_reflected}}
textbook\_leads\_consistent reflected by its shape (examples: leads-consistent, leads-consistent-control)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (p : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ p], \mathrm{s.card} - (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ p\ s)\mathrm{.card} = \{a \in s | \neg p\ a\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2431}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leaks\_redacted}}
findLeaks: every leak found is redacted, so the found less the redacted are none, however many are found. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-leaks, audit-leak-allowed)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{found}\ \mathrm{redacted} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{redacted} = \mathrm{found} \to \mathrm{found} - \mathrm{redacted} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1439}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_leaks\_redacted\_reflected}}
textbook\_leaks\_redacted reflected by its shape (examples: audit-leaks, audit-leak-allowed)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{found}\ \mathrm{redacted} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{redacted} = \mathrm{found} \to 0 = \mathrm{found} - \mathrm{redacted} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1443}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_lean\_served\_whole}}
the hand-written theorems and the generated lemmas, less the generated, are the hand-written: nothing is lost. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, through the instances `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: textbook-theorems-served, metrics-live)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{hand}\ \mathrm{generated} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{hand} + \mathrm{generated} - \mathrm{generated} = \mathrm{hand} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L669}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_lean\_served\_whole\_reflected}}
textbook\_lean\_served\_whole reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-theorems-served, metrics-live)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{hand}\ \mathrm{generated} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{hand} = \sigma - (\mathrm{hand} + \mathrm{generated} - \mathrm{generated}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L672}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_legal\_plan\_calls}}
the legal plan makes one call per claim and two more, so its calls less the claims are two, however many claims: a + b − a = b. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: legal-plan)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{claims} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{claims} + 2 - \mathrm{claims} = 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L984}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_legal\_plan\_calls\_reflected}}
textbook\_legal\_plan\_calls reflected by its shape (examples: legal-plan)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{claims} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 2 = \sigma - (\mathrm{claims} + 2 - \mathrm{claims}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L987}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_licence\_chain}}
n sublicences under one licence: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), the entries of the chain less the root are the links, (n + k) − k = n, here k = 1. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right` (examples: match-url-fallback)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (n\ k : \alpha ), n + k - k = n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L689}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_licence\_chain\_reflected}}
textbook\_licence\_chain reflected by its shape (examples: match-url-fallback)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (n\ k : \alpha ), n = n + k - k \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L693}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_lie\_collides}}
the recomputations each observed lie meets: 15 = 3 × 7 recomputes 21 where the factor door states 3 × 5 = 15; the create hue is 432 \% 360 = 72, never 73 nor 432 (the side sides reads, which the prose backs); G's relative uncertainty is 22474 ppb, never 99; the ancient law is 4 + 5 = 9 entries, never 10; the order of 7 modulo 15 is 4 (7² mod 15 = 4); 6 < 999 is a width the scaled register holds, and the door that factors 91 = 7 × 13 has no qubit ceiling; and a decimal agrees to the places it is written to, 3.47 read as 3.5 ((2 · 347 + 10) / 20 = 35) (examples: match-witness-arithmetic, match-witness-bound, match-witness-route, textbook-prose-sides, match-witness-list, match-witness-named-side)
\[ 3 \cdot 7 \ne 15 \land 3 \cdot 5 = 15 \land 432 \bmod 360 = 72 \land 72 \ne 73 \land 72 \ne 432 \land 15 \cdot 1000000000 / 667430 = 22474 \land 22474 \ne 99 \land 4 + 5 = 9 \land 9 \ne 10 \land 7 ^{2} \bmod 15 = 4 \land 6 < 999 \land 7 \cdot 13 = 91 \land (2 \cdot 347 + 10) / (2 \cdot 10) = 35 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2264}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_lie\_collides\_reflected}}
textbook\_lie\_collides reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: match-witness-arithmetic, match-witness-bound, match-witness-route, textbook-prose-sides, match-witness-list, match-witness-named-side)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 999 \le \sigma \to 15 \ne 3 \cdot 7 \land \sigma - 15 = \sigma - 3 \cdot 5 \land \sigma - 72 = \sigma - 432 \bmod 360 \land 73 \ne 72 \land 432 \ne 72 \land \sigma - 22474 = \sigma - 15 \cdot 1000000000 / 667430 \land 99 \ne 22474 \land \sigma - 9 = \sigma - (4 + 5) \land 10 \ne 9 \land \sigma - 4 = \sigma - 7 ^{2} \bmod 15 \land \sigma - 999 < \sigma - 6 \land \sigma - 91 = \sigma - 7 \cdot 13 \land \sigma - 35 = \sigma - (2 \cdot 347 + 10) / (2 \cdot 10) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2266}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_literal\_in\_registry}}
the literals of the code outside the registry and those in it make all the literals: none is counted twice and none is left out. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_sdiff\_add\_card\_inter` (examples: repository-literals)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{literals}\ \mathrm{registry} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), (\mathrm{literals} \setminus \mathrm{registry})\mathrm{.card} + (\mathrm{literals} \cap \mathrm{registry})\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{literals.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2311}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_literal\_in\_registry\_reflected}}
textbook\_literal\_in\_registry reflected by its shape (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{literals}\ \mathrm{registry} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \mathrm{literals.card} = (\mathrm{literals} \setminus \mathrm{registry})\mathrm{.card} + (\mathrm{literals} \cap \mathrm{registry})\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2316}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_long\_action\_queued}}
an action whose budget exceeds the request leaves a positive remainder, budget − request > 0, for the queue to run it in. From Mathlib's `tsub\_pos\_of\_lt`, through the instances `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-machine-next-long)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{budget}\ \mathrm{request} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{request} < \mathrm{budget} \to 0 < \mathrm{budget} - \mathrm{request} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1511}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_long\_action\_queued\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_long\_action\_queued (examples: repository-machine-next-long)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{budget}\ \mathrm{request} : \mathbb{N}), \neg 0 < \mathrm{budget} - \mathrm{request} \to \neg \mathrm{request} < \mathrm{budget} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1516}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_long\_action\_queued\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_long\_action\_queued, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_pos\_iff\_lt`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-machine-next-long)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{budget}\ \mathrm{request} : \mathbb{N}), 0 < \mathrm{budget} - \mathrm{request} \to \mathrm{request} < \mathrm{budget} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1518}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_long\_action\_queued\_reflected}}
textbook\_long\_action\_queued reflected by its shape (examples: repository-machine-next-long)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{budget}\ \mathrm{request} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{request} < \mathrm{budget} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{budget} - \mathrm{request} \le \sigma \to \sigma - (\mathrm{budget} - \mathrm{request}) < \sigma - 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1514}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_machine\_allow\_list}}
the refused and the run make every request, no third: (asked − allowed) + allowed = asked when allowed ≤ asked. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-machine-actions, repository-machine-refused, repository-machine-push-unconfirmed, repository-machine-worktree-unconfirmed, repository-machine-tag-confirmed, repository-machine-commit-deferred)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{asked}\ \mathrm{allowed} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{allowed} \le \mathrm{asked} \to \mathrm{asked} - \mathrm{allowed} + \mathrm{allowed} = \mathrm{asked} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1481}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_machine\_allow\_list\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_machine\_allow\_list (examples: repository-machine-actions, repository-machine-refused, repository-machine-push-unconfirmed, repository-machine-worktree-unconfirmed, repository-machine-tag-confirmed, repository-machine-commit-deferred)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{asked}\ \mathrm{allowed} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{asked} - \mathrm{allowed} + \mathrm{allowed} = \mathrm{asked} \to \neg \mathrm{allowed} \le \mathrm{asked} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1486}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_machine\_allow\_list\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_machine\_allow\_list, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-machine-actions, repository-machine-refused, repository-machine-push-unconfirmed, repository-machine-worktree-unconfirmed, repository-machine-tag-confirmed, repository-machine-commit-deferred)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{asked}\ \mathrm{allowed} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{asked} - \mathrm{allowed} + \mathrm{allowed} = \mathrm{asked} \to \mathrm{allowed} \le \mathrm{asked} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1488}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_machine\_allow\_list\_reflected}}
textbook\_machine\_allow\_list reflected by its shape (examples: repository-machine-actions, repository-machine-refused, repository-machine-push-unconfirmed, repository-machine-worktree-unconfirmed, repository-machine-tag-confirmed, repository-machine-commit-deferred)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{asked}\ \mathrm{allowed} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{allowed} \le \mathrm{asked} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{asked} = \sigma - (\mathrm{asked} - \mathrm{allowed} + \mathrm{allowed}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1484}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_magic\_constant}}
the entries 0, 1, …, n² of an order-n magic square sum to n²(n² + 1)/2, so each of its n rows sums to the magic constant n(n² + 1)/2 (Σ₁⁹ = 45 and 45/3 = 15 for n = 3). From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_range\_id\_mul\_two` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (\sum i \in \mathrm{Finset.range} (n ^{2} + 1), i) \cdot 2 = (n ^{2} + 1) \cdot n ^{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1905}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_magic\_constant\_reflected}}
textbook\_magic\_constant reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (n ^{2} + 1) \cdot n ^{2} = (\sum i \in \mathrm{Finset.range} (n ^{2} + 1), i) \cdot 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1910}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_majority\_of\_three}}
majority decoding: any two strict majorities of the same copies share a copy, so the majority's verdict is unique (twice two exceeds three: two of three decide). From Mathlib's `Finset.inter\_nonempty\_of\_card\_lt\_card\_add\_card` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{copies}\ A\ B : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), A \subseteq \mathrm{copies} \to B \subseteq \mathrm{copies} \to \mathrm{copies.card} < 2 \cdot \mathrm{A.card} \to \mathrm{copies.card} < 2 \cdot \mathrm{B.card} \to (A \cap B)\mathrm{.Nonempty} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L867}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_margin\_makes\_price}}
the margin and the provider's cost make the price: price − cost + cost = price when cost ≤ price. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: stripe-margins)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{price}\ \mathrm{cost} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{cost} \le \mathrm{price} \to \mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} + \mathrm{cost} = \mathrm{price} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L651}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_margin\_makes\_price\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_margin\_makes\_price (examples: stripe-margins)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{price}\ \mathrm{cost} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} + \mathrm{cost} = \mathrm{price} \to \neg \mathrm{cost} \le \mathrm{price} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L656}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_margin\_makes\_price\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_margin\_makes\_price, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: stripe-margins)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{price}\ \mathrm{cost} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} + \mathrm{cost} = \mathrm{price} \to \mathrm{cost} \le \mathrm{price} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L658}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_margin\_makes\_price\_reflected}}
textbook\_margin\_makes\_price reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-margins)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{price}\ \mathrm{cost} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{cost} \le \mathrm{price} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{price} = \sigma - (\mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} + \mathrm{cost}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L654}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_market\_iso\_4217}}
the market export prices one qubit-shot as the median of three public lists in USD and EUR only (ISO 4217); a forged QPU currency from Capacity 29/232 is refused; REGISTER/pack/cross/price/catalog read the Lean coin SoT at 122 (examples: currency-market)
\[ 3 = 3 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L361}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_market\_iso\_4217\_reflected}}
textbook\_market\_iso\_4217 reflected by its shape, equality (examples: currency-market)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 3 = \sigma - 3 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L363}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mcp\_directories}}
directories sorted by how a machine may read each number the sum of the kinds' sizes, |s| = Σ\_b |\{a ∈ s | f a = b\}|. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: discover-lead-registry, discover-directories-keyed)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \beta ] (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum b \in t, \{a \in s | f\ a = b\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L720}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mcp\_directories\_reflected}}
textbook\_mcp\_directories reflected by its shape (examples: discover-lead-registry, discover-directories-keyed)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \beta ] (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum b \in t, \{a \in s | f\ a = b\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L726}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mcp\_install\_rows}}
installing a server adds at most one row to the tenant's mcps: the rows held are at most one more, and one row added then taken away is the rows held. From Mathlib's `le\_self\_add`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: extend-install)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{held} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{held} \le \mathrm{held} + 1 \land \mathrm{held} + 1 - 1 = \mathrm{held} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1024}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mcp\_search\_limit}}
a requested limit clamped to [1, 100] lies within it, whatever was asked (the default 10 among them). From Mathlib's `le\_max\_left`, `max\_le` and `min\_le\_right` (examples: discover-search-request, discover-search-servers)
\[ \forall (x : \mathbb{N}), 1 \le \mathrm{max}\ 1 (\mathrm{min}\ x\ 100) \land \mathrm{max}\ 1 (\mathrm{min}\ x\ 100) \le 100 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1017}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mcp\_search\_limit\_reflected}}
textbook\_mcp\_search\_limit reflected by its shape (examples: discover-search-request, discover-search-servers)
\[ \forall (x \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{max}\ 1 (\mathrm{min}\ x\ 100) \le \sigma - 1 \land \sigma - 100 \le \sigma - \mathrm{max}\ 1 (\mathrm{min}\ x\ 100) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1022}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mcp\_tools\_by\_toolbox}}
tools partitioned over toolboxes number the sum over the toolboxes of each one's tools. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sigma` (examples: extend-out)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Toolbox} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Toolbox}] (\mathrm{Tool} : \mathrm{Toolbox} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (b : \mathrm{Toolbox}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Tool}\ b)], \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((b : \mathrm{Toolbox}) \times \mathrm{Tool}\ b) = \sum b, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Tool}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L604}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mcp\_tools\_by\_toolbox\_reflected}}
textbook\_mcp\_tools\_by\_toolbox reflected by its shape (examples: extend-out)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Toolbox} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Toolbox}] (\mathrm{Tool} : \mathrm{Toolbox} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (b : \mathrm{Toolbox}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Tool}\ b)], \sum b, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Tool}\ b) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((b : \mathrm{Toolbox}) \times \mathrm{Tool}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L609}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_memo\_identity}}
memo is the identity on a pure function: a memo that only ever holds f y at y answers f x at every x, read or computed (examples: metrics-memo)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (m : \alpha \to \mathrm{Option} \beta ), (\forall (y : \alpha ) (b : \beta ), m\ y = \mathrm{some}\ b \to b = f\ y) \to \forall (x : \alpha ), (m\ x)\mathrm{.getD} (f\ x) = f\ x \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L387}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_memo\_identity\_reflected}}
textbook\_memo\_identity reflected by its shape, equality (examples: metrics-memo)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (m : \alpha \to \mathrm{Option} \beta ), (\forall (y : \alpha ) (b : \beta ), m\ y = \mathrm{some}\ b \to b = f\ y) \to \forall (x : \alpha ), f\ x = (m\ x)\mathrm{.getD} (f\ x) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L389}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mentions\_by\_source}}
when each mention comes from one source, the mentions number the sum over the sources of the mentions from each (3 + 2 + 0 = 5). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: compete-mentions)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L977}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mentions\_by\_source\_reflected}}
textbook\_mentions\_by\_source reflected by its shape (examples: compete-mentions)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L982}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mind\_sigma}}
the neuroscience bridge σ\_d(x) = d − x is an involution on 0..d: σ\_d(σ\_d(x)) = x, σ\_d³ = σ\_d, and the two sides sum to the whole, (d − x) + x = d. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` and `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le d \to d - (d - x) = x \land d - (d - (d - x)) = d - x \land d - x + x = d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1712}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mind\_sigma\_reflected}}
textbook\_mind\_sigma reflected by its shape (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (d\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le d \to x = d - (d - x) \land d - x = d - (d - (d - x)) \land d = d - x + x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1717}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mixed\_radix}}
mixed radix: a choice d below n and the rest q are one index d + n · q below m · n, and the index gives them back. From Mathlib's `finProdFinEquiv\_apply\_val` and `Equiv.symm\_apply\_apply` (examples: fold-queneau, fold-rosetta, leads-scientific-domains, css-shadcn)
\[ \forall (m\ n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{Fin}\ m \times \mathrm{Fin}\ n), \uparrow (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) = \uparrow \mathrm{x.2} + n \cdot \uparrow \mathrm{x.1} \land \mathrm{finProdFinEquiv.symm} (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) = x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1458}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mixed\_radix\_reflected}}
textbook\_mixed\_radix reflected by its shape (examples: fold-queneau, fold-rosetta, leads-scientific-domains, css-shadcn)
\[ \forall (m\ n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{Fin}\ m \times \mathrm{Fin}\ n), \uparrow \mathrm{x.2} + n \cdot \uparrow \mathrm{x.1} = \uparrow (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) \land x = \mathrm{finProdFinEquiv.symm} (\mathrm{finProdFinEquiv}\ x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1462}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ml\_dsa\_fips\_204}}
when both the public key and the signature grow with the security category, so does what a signature costs on the wire, key plus signature (FIPS 204 ML-DSA-44, -65, -87: 3732 < 5261 < 7219 bytes). From Mathlib's `add\_lt\_add`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `IsRightCancelAdd.addRightStrictMono\_of\_addRightMono`, `instIsRightCancelAddOfAddRightReflectLE`, `addRightReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLE`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `covariant\_swap\_add\_of\_covariant\_add` (examples: seal-block-sign-ml-dsa-44)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{pk}_{1} \mathrm{sig}_{1} \mathrm{pk}_{2} \mathrm{sig}_{2} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{pk}_{1} < \mathrm{pk}_{2} \to \mathrm{sig}_{1} < \mathrm{sig}_{2} \to \mathrm{pk}_{1} + \mathrm{sig}_{1} < \mathrm{pk}_{2} + \mathrm{sig}_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2219}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ml\_dsa\_fips\_204\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_ml\_dsa\_fips\_204 (examples: seal-block-sign-ml-dsa-44)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{pk}_{1} \mathrm{sig}_{1} \mathrm{pk}_{2} \mathrm{sig}_{2} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{pk}_{1} + \mathrm{sig}_{1} < \mathrm{pk}_{2} + \mathrm{sig}_{2} \to \neg (\mathrm{pk}_{1} < \mathrm{pk}_{2} \land \mathrm{sig}_{1} < \mathrm{sig}_{2}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2226}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ml\_dsa\_fips\_204\_reflected}}
textbook\_ml\_dsa\_fips\_204 reflected by its shape (examples: seal-block-sign-ml-dsa-44)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{pk}_{1} \mathrm{sig}_{1} \mathrm{pk}_{2} \mathrm{sig}_{2} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{pk}_{1} < \mathrm{pk}_{2} \to \mathrm{sig}_{1} < \mathrm{sig}_{2} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{pk}_{2} + \mathrm{sig}_{2} \le \sigma \to \sigma - (\mathrm{pk}_{2} + \mathrm{sig}_{2}) < \sigma - (\mathrm{pk}_{1} + \mathrm{sig}_{1}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2224}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mongo\_find\_filter}}
a find over the scanned documents: those matching the filter and those not partition the scan (one of three matches qubits ≥ 60 with the tag processor). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_filter\_add\_card\_filter\_not` (examples: mongo-find)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{scanned} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{hit} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{hit}], (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{hit}\ \mathrm{scanned})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{scanned} | \neg \mathrm{hit}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{scanned.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L946}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mongo\_find\_filter\_reflected}}
textbook\_mongo\_find\_filter reflected by its shape (examples: mongo-find)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{scanned} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{hit} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{hit}], \mathrm{scanned.card} = (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{hit}\ \mathrm{scanned})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{scanned} | \neg \mathrm{hit}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L951}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mongo\_update\_inc}}
\$inc adds one to Sycamore's 54 qubits: 55, written as a new entry beside the one it links, so history is two entries (examples: mongo-update)
\[ 54 + 1 = 55 \land 1 + 1 = 2 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L953}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_mongo\_update\_inc\_reflected}}
textbook\_mongo\_update\_inc reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: mongo-update)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 55 = \sigma - (54 + 1) \land \sigma - 2 = \sigma - (1 + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L955}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_monotonic\_tick}}
a call's duration is the later monotonic reading less the earlier and never negative: (ended − started) + started = ended. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: metrics-served-calls)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{started}\ \mathrm{ended} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{started} \le \mathrm{ended} \to \mathrm{ended} - \mathrm{started} + \mathrm{started} = \mathrm{ended} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1061}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_monotonic\_tick\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_monotonic\_tick (examples: metrics-served-calls)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{started}\ \mathrm{ended} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{ended} - \mathrm{started} + \mathrm{started} = \mathrm{ended} \to \neg \mathrm{started} \le \mathrm{ended} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1066}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_monotonic\_tick\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_monotonic\_tick, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: metrics-served-calls)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{started}\ \mathrm{ended} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{ended} - \mathrm{started} + \mathrm{started} = \mathrm{ended} \to \mathrm{started} \le \mathrm{ended} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1068}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_monotonic\_tick\_reflected}}
textbook\_monotonic\_tick reflected by its shape (examples: metrics-served-calls)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{started}\ \mathrm{ended} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{started} \le \mathrm{ended} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{ended} = \sigma - (\mathrm{ended} - \mathrm{started} + \mathrm{started}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1064}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_namespace\_narrows}}
a namespace narrows a credential, never widens it: the meet of what the request named and what the user held lies below what the user held. From Mathlib's `inf\_le\_right` (examples: leads-client)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{SemilatticeInf} \alpha ] (\mathrm{named}\ \mathrm{held} : \alpha ), \mathrm{named} \sqcap \mathrm{held} \le \mathrm{held} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L100}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_neighbour\_step}}
one step forward and one step back on a cycle of n positions returns the program (n = 2¹⁶ for g1, 2¹² for g2 and g3), at every edge. From Mathlib's `add\_sub\_cancel\_right` (examples: fold-neighbours)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{ZMod}\ n), x + 1 - 1 = x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1537}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_neighbour\_step\_reflected}}
textbook\_neighbour\_step reflected by its shape (examples: fold-neighbours)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{ZMod}\ n), x = x + 1 - 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1540}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_new\_doors\_proven}}
when no new export is left unproven, every new export is proven: proven ≤ news and news − proven = 0 give proven = news. From Mathlib's `le\_antisymm`, `tsub\_eq\_zero\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-commit-folds, repository-commit-grows)
