\[ \forall (\mathrm{news}\ \mathrm{proven} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{proven} \le \mathrm{news} \to \mathrm{news} - \mathrm{proven} = 0 \to \mathrm{proven} = \mathrm{news} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L280}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_new\_doors\_proven\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_new\_doors\_proven (examples: repository-commit-folds, repository-commit-grows)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{news}\ \mathrm{proven} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{proven} = \mathrm{news} \to \neg (\mathrm{proven} \le \mathrm{news} \land \mathrm{news} - \mathrm{proven} = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L286}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_new\_doors\_proven\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_new\_doors\_proven, proved. From Mathlib's `Eq.le`, `tsub\_eq\_zero\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` and `Eq.ge` (examples: repository-commit-folds, repository-commit-grows)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{news}\ \mathrm{proven} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{proven} = \mathrm{news} \to \mathrm{proven} \le \mathrm{news} \land \mathrm{news} - \mathrm{proven} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L288}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_new\_doors\_proven\_reflected}}
textbook\_new\_doors\_proven reflected by its shape (examples: repository-commit-folds, repository-commit-grows)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{news}\ \mathrm{proven} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{proven} \le \mathrm{news} \to \mathrm{news} - \mathrm{proven} = 0 \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{news} = \sigma - \mathrm{proven} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L284}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_news\_adapters}}
the open news APIs and the keyed ones, disjoint, together number the two counts, |A ∪ B| = |A| + |B|. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_union\_of\_disjoint` (examples: compete-news-plan)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (A\ B : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \mathrm{Disjoint}\ A\ B \to (A \cup B)\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{A.card} + \mathrm{B.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L661}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_news\_adapters\_reflected}}
textbook\_news\_adapters reflected by its shape (examples: compete-news-plan)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (A\ B : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \mathrm{Disjoint}\ A\ B \to \mathrm{A.card} + \mathrm{B.card} = (A \cup B)\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L667}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_news\_problems}}
matchNews decides each item served, impossible or unknown, so the items less the impossible and the unknown are the served. From Mathlib's `add\_right\_comm`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: match-news)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{impossible}\ \mathrm{unknown}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{impossible} + \mathrm{unknown} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{impossible} - \mathrm{unknown} = \mathrm{served} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1964}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_news\_problems\_reflected}}
textbook\_news\_problems reflected by its shape (examples: match-news)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{impossible}\ \mathrm{unknown}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{impossible} + \mathrm{unknown} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{served} = \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{impossible} - \mathrm{unknown} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1968}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_next\_waves\_ordered}}
a wave w precedes a wave v exactly when w + 1 ≤ v. From Mathlib's `Order.add\_one\_le\_iff`, through the instance `Nat.instNoMaxOrder` (examples: repository-machine-next)
\[ \forall (w\ v : \mathbb{N}), w + 1 \le v \leftrightarrow w < v \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1506}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_next\_waves\_ordered\_reflected}}
textbook\_next\_waves\_ordered reflected by its shape (examples: repository-machine-next)
\[ \forall (w\ v : \mathbb{N}), w < v \leftrightarrow w + 1 \le v \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1509}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_no\_cloning}}
no-cloning from linearity: a linear map that copies a and b (a ↦ a ⊗ a, b ↦ b ⊗ b) copies a + b only when the cross terms a ⊗ b + b ⊗ a vanish, since (a + b) ⊗ (a + b) carries them and the linear image does not. From Mathlib's `map\_add`, `TensorProduct.add\_tmul`, `TensorProduct.tmul\_add`, `add\_assoc`, `add\_comm`, `add\_eq\_left`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `SemilinearMapClass.toAddHomClass`, `LinearMap.semilinearMapClass` (examples: algorithm-no-cloning)
\[ \forall \{K\ V : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{CommRing}\ K] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommGroup}\ V] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{\_root\_.Module}\ K\ V] (f : V \to _{l}[K] \mathrm{TensorProduct}\ K\ V\ V) (a\ b : V), f\ a = a \otimes _{t}[K] a \to f\ b = b \otimes _{t}[K] b \to f (a + b) = (a + b) \otimes _{t}[K] (a + b) \to a \otimes _{t}[K] b + b \otimes _{t}[K] a = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1250}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_no\_cloning\_reflected}}
textbook\_no\_cloning reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-no-cloning)
\[ \forall \{K\ V : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{CommRing}\ K] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommGroup}\ V] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{\_root\_.Module}\ K\ V] (f : V \to _{l}[K] \mathrm{TensorProduct}\ K\ V\ V) (a\ b : V), f\ a = a \otimes _{t}[K] a \to f\ b = b \otimes _{t}[K] b \to f (a + b) = (a + b) \otimes _{t}[K] (a + b) \to 0 = a \otimes _{t}[K] b + b \otimes _{t}[K] a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1258}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_novelty\_claimed}}
the statements claimed are those with no prior work, and the claimed and the unclaimed make all the statements (of 2 statements, the one with no prior work is claimed). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_filter\_add\_card\_filter\_not` (examples: compete-novelty)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{prior} : \alpha \to \mathbb{N}), \{a \in s | \mathrm{prior}\ a = 0\}\mathrm{.card} + \{a \in s | \neg \mathrm{prior}\ a = 0\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2410}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_novelty\_claimed\_reflected}}
textbook\_novelty\_claimed reflected by its shape (examples: compete-novelty)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{prior} : \alpha \to \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{s.card} = \{a \in s | \mathrm{prior}\ a = 0\}\mathrm{.card} + \{a \in s | \neg \mathrm{prior}\ a = 0\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2416}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_now\_within\_validity}}
now is within the validity of a certificate exactly when it is not before notBefore and not after notAfter: membership of the closed interval between them. From Mathlib's `Set.mem\_Icc` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{notBefore}\ \mathrm{notAfter}\ \mathrm{now} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{now} \in \mathrm{Set.Icc}\ \mathrm{notBefore}\ \mathrm{notAfter} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{notBefore} \le \mathrm{now} \land \mathrm{now} \le \mathrm{notAfter} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2284}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_now\_within\_validity\_reflected}}
textbook\_now\_within\_validity reflected by its shape (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{notBefore}\ \mathrm{notAfter}\ \mathrm{now} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{notBefore} \le \mathrm{now} \land \mathrm{now} \le \mathrm{notAfter} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{now} \in \mathrm{Set.Icc}\ \mathrm{notBefore}\ \mathrm{notAfter} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2287}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_number\_operator}}
the number operator of a mode has the eigenvalues 0 and 1: it is 1 less z, halved, where z is 1 for the empty mode and minus 1 for the filled one, so (1 - 1) / 2 = 0 and (1 + 1) / 2 = 1 (examples: chemistry-jordan-wigner-number)
\[ (1 - 1) / 2 = 0 \land (1 + 1) / 2 = 1 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2418}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_number\_operator\_reflected}}
textbook\_number\_operator reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: chemistry-jordan-wigner-number)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (1 - 1) / 2 \land \sigma - 1 = \sigma - (1 + 1) / 2 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2445}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_observed\_errors}}
at most one error per thousand requests means the errors never outnumber the requests: errors ≤ errors · 1000 ≤ requests. From Mathlib's `le\_trans` (examples: repository-predeploy)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{errors}\ \mathrm{requests} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{errors} \cdot 1000 \le \mathrm{requests} \to \mathrm{errors} \le \mathrm{requests} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1567}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_observed\_errors\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_observed\_errors (examples: repository-predeploy)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{errors}\ \mathrm{requests} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{errors} \le \mathrm{requests} \to \neg \mathrm{errors} \cdot 1000 \le \mathrm{requests} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1573}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_observed\_errors\_reflected}}
textbook\_observed\_errors reflected by its shape (examples: repository-predeploy)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{errors}\ \mathrm{requests} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{errors} \cdot 1000 \le \mathrm{requests} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{requests} \le \sigma - \mathrm{errors} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1571}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_one\_holder}}
k copies folded into one holder leave one: k − (k − 1) = 1 for k ≥ 1. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: audit-shapes-one-holder)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 1 \le k \to k - (k - 1) = 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L507}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_one\_holder\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_one\_holder (examples: audit-shapes-one-holder)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), \neg k - (k - 1) = 1 \to \neg 1 \le k \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L512}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_one\_holder\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_one\_holder, proved. From Mathlib's `tsub\_lt\_self\_iff`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `tsub\_pos\_iff\_lt`, `zero\_lt\_one` and `Nat.instZeroLEOneClass` (examples: audit-shapes-one-holder)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), k - (k - 1) = 1 \to 1 \le k \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L514}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_one\_holder\_reflected}}
textbook\_one\_holder reflected by its shape (examples: audit-shapes-one-holder)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 1 \le k \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 1 = \sigma - (k - (k - 1)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L510}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_only\_the\_proven\_is\_admitted}}
a width w is rebuilt exactly from its blocks of d and the remainder, d · (w / d) + w \% d = w (1000 bits are 125 bytes exactly, and fourteen reflections of 71 bits and six more). From Mathlib's `EuclideanDomain.div\_add\_mod` (examples: fold-proven)
\[ \forall (w\ d : \mathbb{Z}), d \cdot (w / d) + w \bmod d = w \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2323}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_only\_the\_proven\_is\_admitted\_reflected}}
textbook\_only\_the\_proven\_is\_admitted reflected by its shape (examples: fold-proven)
\[ \forall (w\ d : \mathbb{Z}), w = d \cdot (w / d) + w \bmod d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2327}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_orcid\_digits}}
k groups of g digits with d dashes are k · g + d characters, the cardinality of a product beside a disjoint part (4 · 4 + 3 = 19 for an ORCID). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum`, `Fintype.card\_prod` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: jsonld-block)
\[ \forall (k\ g\ d : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ k \times \mathrm{Fin}\ g \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ d) = k \cdot g + d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L996}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_orcid\_digits\_reflected}}
textbook\_orcid\_digits reflected by its shape (examples: jsonld-block)
\[ \forall (k\ g\ d : \mathbb{N}), k \cdot g + d = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ k \times \mathrm{Fin}\ g \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1001}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_order\_finding}}
order-finding: the order r of x in ZMod N is the least positive power that is one, x\^{}r = 1 and x\^{}k ≠ 1 for every 0 < k < r, the witness baby-step giant-step checks. From Mathlib's `pow\_orderOf\_eq\_one`, `pow\_ne\_one\_of\_lt\_orderOf` and `LT.lt.ne'` (examples: algorithm-discover, algorithm-order, algorithm-factor-find)
\[ \forall (N : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{ZMod}\ N), x ^{\mathrm{orderOf}} x = 1 \land \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 0 < k \to k < \mathrm{orderOf}\ x \to x ^{k} \ne 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1592}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_order\_finding\_reflected}}
textbook\_order\_finding reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-discover, algorithm-order, algorithm-factor-find)
\[ \forall (N : \mathbb{N}) (x : \mathrm{ZMod}\ N), 1 = x ^{\mathrm{orderOf}} x \land \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), \neg x ^{k} \ne 1 \to \neg (0 < k \land k < \mathrm{orderOf}\ x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1597}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_own\_keys\_only}}
a word is found in a table only among the table's own keys: a lookup that hits names a key of the table. From Mathlib's `Finmap.lookup\_isSome` (examples: match-builtin-words, chat-builtin-words, translate-builtin-words, chat-cirq-constructors)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} \{\beta : \alpha \to \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{table} : \mathrm{Finmap} \beta ) (\mathrm{word} : \alpha ) (v : \beta \mathrm{word}), \mathrm{Finmap.lookup}\ \mathrm{word}\ \mathrm{table} = \mathrm{some}\ v \to \mathrm{word} \in \mathrm{table} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L139}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_particles\_classes}}
when every textbook theorem falls in one class, the classes count the total: the theorems number the sum over the classes of the theorems in each. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: textbook-particles, textbook-particles-control)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2433}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_particles\_classes\_reflected}}
textbook\_particles\_classes reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-particles, textbook-particles-control)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2438}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_pascal\_rule}}
Pascal's rule, the defining recurrence of the binomial coefficient: C(n + 1, k + 1) = C(n, k) + C(n, k + 1) (C(8,3) = 56 = 21 + 35, C(8,2) = 28 = 7 + 21, C(7,3) = 35 = 15 + 20), the textbook meaning of the 22 clay relations choose a b = choose a b. From Mathlib's `Nat.choose\_succ\_succ` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n\ k : \mathbb{N}), (n + 1)\mathrm{.choose} (k + 1) = \mathrm{n.choose}\ k + \mathrm{n.choose} (k + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1854}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_pascal\_rule\_reflected}}
textbook\_pascal\_rule reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n\ k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{n.choose}\ k + \mathrm{n.choose} (k + 1) = (n + 1)\mathrm{.choose} (k + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1858}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_apis\_partition}}
the patent APIs split into those answering in the open and those taking a key, and the two together are all of them. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_filter\_add\_card\_filter\_not` (examples: patents-plan)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{keyed} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{keyed}], (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{keyed}\ s)\mathrm{.card} + \{a \in s | \neg \mathrm{keyed}\ a\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L969}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_apis\_partition\_reflected}}
textbook\_patent\_apis\_partition reflected by its shape (examples: patents-plan)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{keyed} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{keyed}], \mathrm{s.card} = (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{keyed}\ s)\mathrm{.card} + \{a \in s | \neg \mathrm{keyed}\ a\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L975}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_dates\_ordered}}
priority, filing and grant are ordered: what is ordered pairwise is ordered through, in any preorder. From Mathlib's `le\_trans` (examples: patents-read)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Preorder} \alpha ] (p\ f\ g : \alpha ), p \le f \to f \le g \to p \le g \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L414}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_number\_well\_formed}}
a number made of fields of a, b and c characters has a + b + c characters (2 letters, 7 digits and a 2-character kind code: 11 for an EP number). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ c) = a + b + c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L417}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_number\_well\_formed\_reflected}}
textbook\_patent\_number\_well\_formed reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c : \mathbb{N}), a + b + c = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ c) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L422}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_term\_twenty\_years}}
a term of positive length from filing ends after the filing (twenty years a case), so a grant within the term precedes its end. From Mathlib's `lt\_add\_of\_pos\_right`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (f\ \mathrm{term} : \mathbb{N}), 0 < \mathrm{term} \to f < f + \mathrm{term} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L424}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_term\_twenty\_years\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_patent\_term\_twenty\_years (examples: )
\[ \forall (f\ \mathrm{term} : \mathbb{N}), \neg f < f + \mathrm{term} \to \neg 0 < \mathrm{term} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L429}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_term\_twenty\_years\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_patent\_term\_twenty\_years, proved. From Mathlib's `lt\_add\_iff\_pos\_right`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: )
\[ \forall (f\ \mathrm{term} : \mathbb{N}), f < f + \mathrm{term} \to 0 < \mathrm{term} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L431}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_patent\_term\_twenty\_years\_reflected}}
textbook\_patent\_term\_twenty\_years reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall (f\ \mathrm{term} : \mathbb{N}), 0 < \mathrm{term} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), f + \mathrm{term} \le \sigma \to \sigma - (f + \mathrm{term}) < \sigma - f \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L427}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_payload\_operations}}
operations sorted by scope number the sum of the scopes' sizes, |s| = Σ\_b |\{a ∈ s | f a = b\}|. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: legal-court-method)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \beta ] (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum b \in t, \{a \in s | f\ a = b\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L674}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_payload\_operations\_reflected}}
textbook\_payload\_operations reflected by its shape (examples: legal-court-method)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \beta ] (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \beta ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum b \in t, \{a \in s | f\ a = b\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L680}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_pbkdf2\_blocks}}
PBKDF2 derives a key of len bytes in ⌈len / h⌉ = (len + h − 1) / h blocks of the h-byte HMAC output, the fewest blocks covering the key: that count is at most c exactly when len ≤ h · c (a 32-byte key from HMAC-SHA256's 32-byte blocks takes 1 block). From Mathlib's `Nat.ceilDiv\_eq\_add\_pred\_div`, `ceilDiv\_le\_iff\_le\_mul` (examples: seal-pbkdf2)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{len}\ h\ c : \mathbb{N}), 0 < h \to ((\mathrm{len} + h - 1) / h \le c \leftrightarrow \mathrm{len} \le h \cdot c) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2395}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_pbkdf2\_blocks\_reflected}}
textbook\_pbkdf2\_blocks reflected by its shape (examples: seal-pbkdf2)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{len}\ h\ c : \mathbb{N}), 0 < h \to (\mathrm{len} \le h \cdot c \leftrightarrow (\mathrm{len} + h - 1) / h \le c) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2401}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_peg\_is\_fixed}}
a pegged currency converts at one fixed rate: the same euro amount gives the same leva, always (1.95583 since 2026-01-01) (examples: currency-harmonise, currency-plan, currency-refuse-qpu)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{eur} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{eur} \cdot 195583 / 100000 = \mathrm{eur} \cdot 195583 / 100000 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L352}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_peg\_is\_fixed\_reflected}}
textbook\_peg\_is\_fixed reflected by its shape, equality (examples: currency-harmonise, currency-plan, currency-refuse-qpu)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{eur} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{eur} \cdot 195583 / 100000 = \sigma - \mathrm{eur} \cdot 195583 / 100000 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L355}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_antimatter\_annihilates}}
a gap and its antimatter annihilate: the line priced and reversed nets to zero, and only what stood beside it remains, t + p − t = p. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: repository-penalty-annihilate)
\[ \forall (t\ p : \mathbb{N}), t + p - t = p \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L186}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_antimatter\_annihilates\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_antimatter\_annihilates reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty-annihilate)
\[ \forall (t\ p : \mathbb{N}), p = t + p - t \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L189}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_balanced}}
a coin line booking the same count on both sides nets to zero: a number less itself is zero. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, through the instances `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-penalty)
\[ \forall (d\ c : \mathbb{N}), d = c \to d - c = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L143}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_balanced\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_penalty\_balanced (examples: repository-penalty)
\[ \forall (d\ c : \mathbb{N}), \neg d - c = 0 \to \neg d = c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L149}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_balanced\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_balanced reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty)
\[ \forall (d\ c : \mathbb{N}), d = c \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (d - c) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L147}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_clear\_involution}}
penalising then clearing leaves the open count as it was: (open + n) − n = open. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: repository-penalty-clear)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{opn}\ n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{opn} + n - n = \mathrm{opn} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L151}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_clear\_involution\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_clear\_involution reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty-clear)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{opn}\ n \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{opn} = \sigma - (\mathrm{opn} + n - n) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L154}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_conserved}}
every penalty opened is either cleared or still open: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when the cleared and the open make the opened, the open count is the opened less the cleared. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{opened}\ \mathrm{cleared}\ \mathrm{openNow} : \alpha ), \mathrm{cleared} + \mathrm{openNow} = \mathrm{opened} \to \mathrm{opened} - \mathrm{cleared} = \mathrm{openNow} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L156}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_conserved\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_conserved reflected by its shape (examples: )
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{opened}\ \mathrm{cleared}\ \mathrm{openNow} : \alpha ), \mathrm{cleared} + \mathrm{openNow} = \mathrm{opened} \to \mathrm{openNow} = \mathrm{opened} - \mathrm{cleared} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L161}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_free\_under\_budget}}
a task within the budget, on the wall and on the CPU, is free: each overrun truncates to zero. From Mathlib's `tsub\_eq\_zero\_of\_le`, `add\_zero`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-penalty-under-budget)
\[ \forall (b\ w\ c : \mathbb{N}), w \le b \to c \le b \to w - b + (c - b) = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L180}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_free\_under\_budget\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_free\_under\_budget reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty-under-budget)
\[ \forall (b\ w\ c : \mathbb{N}), w \le b \to c \le b \to 0 = w - b + (c - b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L184}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_harmonic}}
a penalty priced at the cost that undoes it annihilates to zero both ways: a number less itself is zero. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self`, through the instances `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-penalty-harmonic)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{cost}\ \mathrm{price} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{price} = \mathrm{cost} \to \mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} = 0 \land \mathrm{cost} - \mathrm{price} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L251}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_harmonic\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_penalty\_harmonic (examples: repository-penalty-harmonic)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{cost}\ \mathrm{price} : \mathbb{N}), \neg (\mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} = 0 \land \mathrm{cost} - \mathrm{price} = 0) \to \neg \mathrm{price} = \mathrm{cost} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L257}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_harmonic\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_penalty\_harmonic, proved. From Mathlib's `le\_antisymm`, `tsub\_eq\_zero\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-penalty-harmonic)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{cost}\ \mathrm{price} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost} = 0 \land \mathrm{cost} - \mathrm{price} = 0 \to \mathrm{price} = \mathrm{cost} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L259}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_harmonic\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_harmonic reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty-harmonic)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{cost}\ \mathrm{price} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{price} = \mathrm{cost} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (\mathrm{price} - \mathrm{cost}) \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (\mathrm{cost} - \mathrm{price}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L255}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_learned\_prevents}}
a violation whose learned correction ran by itself and cleared is corrected and re-decided before it is raised: when the correction is applied and the rule holds again, no penalty is raised — the involution of the thinking that caused it (examples: repository-penalty-learned, repository-penalty-learning)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{learned}\ \mathrm{raised} : \mathrm{Bool}), (\mathrm{learned} = \mathrm{true} \to \mathrm{raised} = \mathrm{false}) \to \mathrm{learned} = \mathrm{true} \to \mathrm{raised} = \mathrm{false} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L267}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_learned\_prevents\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_learned\_prevents reflected by its shape, equality (examples: repository-penalty-learned, repository-penalty-learning)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{learned}\ \mathrm{raised} : \mathrm{Bool}), (\mathrm{learned} = \mathrm{true} \to \mathrm{raised} = \mathrm{false}) \to \mathrm{learned} = \mathrm{true} \to \mathrm{false} = \mathrm{raised} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L270}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_no\_cliff}}
the waiting price has no cliff: truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models) is 1-Lipschitz, (w + e) − b ≤ (w − b) + e, so e more milliseconds on the wall raise the price, beside any other charge c, by at most e, at the budget as everywhere. From Mathlib's `add\_le\_add\_right` and `add\_tsub\_le\_tsub\_add` (examples: repository-penalty-no-cliff)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Preorder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] (b\ w\ e\ c : \alpha ), c + (w + e - b) \le c + (w - b + e) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L175}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_no\_punishment}}
a penalty raised then cleared leaves status and standing exactly as they were: x + t − t = x. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: repository-penalty-no-punishment)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{balance}\ \mathrm{standing}\ t : \mathbb{N}), (\mathrm{balance} + t - t, \mathrm{standing} + t - t) = (\mathrm{balance}, \mathrm{standing}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L191}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_no\_punishment\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_no\_punishment reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty-no-punishment)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{balance}\ \mathrm{standing}\ t : \mathbb{N}), (\mathrm{balance}, \mathrm{standing}) = (\mathrm{balance} + t - t, \mathrm{standing} + t - t) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L195}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_once}}
an entry already held is not added again: inserting it leaves the set as it was, however often it is penalised. From Mathlib's `Finset.insert\_eq\_of\_mem` (examples: repository-penalty-once)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \forall a \in s, \mathrm{insert}\ a\ s = s \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L166}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_once\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_once reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty-once)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \forall a \in s, s = \mathrm{insert}\ a\ s \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L169}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_proportional}}
one more occurrence of a violation costs one more unit, never a multiplier: (n + 1) · u = n · u + u. From Mathlib's `add\_one\_mul`, `Distrib.rightDistribClass` (examples: repository-penalty-proportional)
\[ \forall (n\ u : \mathbb{N}), (n + 1) \cdot u = n \cdot u + u \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L262}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_proportional\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_proportional reflected by its shape (examples: repository-penalty-proportional)
\[ \forall (n\ u \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (n \cdot u + u) = \sigma - (n + 1) \cdot u \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L265}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_time\_burned}}
the time a gap stood is burned: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), its charge (c − b) + (s − b) never falls as it stands longer, and is zero when cleared within the budget. From Mathlib's `add\_le\_add\_right`, `tsub\_le\_tsub\_right`, `tsub\_eq\_zero\_of\_le` and `add\_zero` (examples: repository-penalty-annihilate)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommMonoid} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{CanonicallyOrderedAdd} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_3} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] (b\ s\ s'\ c : \alpha ), s \le s' \to c - b + (s - b) \le c - b + (s' - b) \land (s \le b \to c \le b \to c - b + (s - b) = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L197}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_time\_heat\_monotone}}
a waiting price never falls as a task gets slower or hotter: truncated subtraction of the budget is monotone and sums of monotone terms are monotone. From Mathlib's `add\_le\_add`, `tsub\_le\_tsub\_right`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `covariant\_swap\_add\_of\_covariant\_add` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-penalty-waiting, repository-timed)
\[ \forall (b\ w\ w'\ c\ c' : \mathbb{N}), w \le w' \to c \le c' \to w - b + (c - b) \le w' - b + (c' - b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L171}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_tokens\_bound}}
a penalty's tokens, its bytes over four rounded up (b ⌈/⌉ 4), never exceed its bytes. From Mathlib's `ceilDiv\_le\_iff\_le\_mul` (examples: repository-penalty)
\[ \forall (b : \mathbb{N}), 0 < b \to b \lceil /\rceil 4 \le b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L163}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_visibility\_harmonic}}
a penalty is as visible as the action it prices: printed agrees with visible, kind by kind over the ten gap kinds, so a printed penalty priced a visible action — an invisible action's penalty is recorded silently and counted on request, never printed (examples: repository-penalty-invisible, repository-penalty-visible)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{visible}\ \mathrm{printed} : \mathrm{Bool}), \mathrm{printed} = \mathrm{visible} \to \mathrm{printed} = \mathrm{true} \to \mathrm{visible} = \mathrm{true} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L204}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_penalty\_visibility\_harmonic\_reflected}}
textbook\_penalty\_visibility\_harmonic reflected by its shape, implication (examples: repository-penalty-invisible, repository-penalty-visible)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{visible}\ \mathrm{printed} : \mathrm{Bool}), \mathrm{printed} = \mathrm{visible} \to \neg \mathrm{visible} = \mathrm{true} \to \neg \mathrm{printed} = \mathrm{true} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L207}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_phase\_bits}}
a positive n lies between consecutive powers of two, 2\^{}⌊log₂ n⌋ ≤ n < 2\^{}(⌊log₂ n⌋ + 1): the phase's 3888 milliseconds lie between 2\^{}11 and 2\^{}12, eleven full bits. From Mathlib's `Nat.pow\_log\_le\_self` and `Nat.lt\_pow\_succ\_log\_self` (examples: seal-entropy)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to 2 ^{\mathrm{Nat.log}} 2\ n \le n \land n < 2 ^{(\mathrm{Nat.log}\ 2\ n + 1)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L794}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_phase\_estimation}}
quantum phase estimation reads θ = k/N exactly: for a primitive N-th root of unity ζ, the amplitude Σ\_\{j<N\} (ζᵐ)ʲ of an outcome off by m with N ∤ m vanishes, so only the true outcome survives (N = 2³ = 8, θ = 1/4 read as 2). From Mathlib's `IsPrimitiveRoot.dvd\_of\_pow\_eq\_one`, `IsPrimitiveRoot.pow\_eq\_one`, `geom\_sum\_eq`, `pow\_mul`, `mul\_comm`, `one\_pow`, `sub\_self` and `zero\_div` (examples: algorithm-phase-estimation)
\[ \forall \{K : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Field}\ K] \{\zeta : K\} \{N\ m : \mathbb{N}\}, \mathrm{IsPrimitiveRoot} \zeta N \to \neg N \mid m \to \sum j \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ N, (\zeta ^{m}) ^{j} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1191}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_phase\_estimation\_reflected}}
textbook\_phase\_estimation reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-phase-estimation)
\[ \forall \{K : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Field}\ K] \{\zeta : K\} \{N\ m : \mathbb{N}\}, \mathrm{IsPrimitiveRoot} \zeta N \to \neg N \mid m \to 0 = \sum j \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ N, (\zeta ^{m}) ^{j} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1196}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_phase\_three\_bits}}
n bits hold 2\^{}n values, so a phase j/2\^{}n with j < 2\^{}n is one of them and read exactly (θ = 1/4 = 2/2³ among the eight of three bits). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_bool` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: algorithm-phase-estimation)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) = 2 ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1304}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_phase\_three\_bits\_reflected}}
textbook\_phase\_three\_bits reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-phase-estimation)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{n} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1309}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_physical\_evidence}}
a device counts as measured only when identified and read: a meet is the top only when both are, identified ⊓ read = ⊤ gives identified = ⊤ and read = ⊤ (Bool's `\&\&` a case). From Mathlib's `inf\_eq\_top\_iff` (examples: validate-physical-measured, validate-physical-unmeasured)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Lattice} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{OrderTop} \alpha ] (\mathrm{identified}\ \mathrm{read} : \alpha ), \mathrm{identified} \sqcap \mathrm{read} = \top \to \mathrm{identified} = \top \land \mathrm{read} = \top  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1045}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_physical\_evidence\_reflected}}
textbook\_physical\_evidence reflected by its shape (examples: validate-physical-measured, validate-physical-unmeasured)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Lattice} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{OrderTop} \alpha ] (\mathrm{identified}\ \mathrm{read} : \alpha ), \mathrm{identified} \sqcap \mathrm{read} = \top \to \top = \mathrm{identified} \land \top = \mathrm{read} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1049}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_plugin\_combinations}}
each of n plugins in or out gives 2\^{}n configurations. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_bool` and `Fintype.card\_fin`
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) = 2 ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L45}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_plugin\_combinations\_reflected}}
textbook\_plugin\_combinations reflected by its shape
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{n} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L50}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_plugins\_by\_capability}}
a capability plugin that writes into another plugin's options runs strictly before it, so at least one order step before: m < n gives m + 1 ≤ n. From Mathlib's `Order.add\_one\_le\_of\_lt` (examples: textbook-plugins-by-capability)
\[ \forall (m\ n : \mathbb{N}), m < n \to m + 1 \le n \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L473}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_postings\_parity}}
a journal entry is balanced exactly when its syndrome is zero: the distance (d − c) + (c − d) of the debits and the credits on the naturals is zero iff d = c, a parity check like a stabilizer's +1. From Mathlib's `Nat.eq\_of\_dist\_eq\_zero` and `Nat.dist\_eq\_zero` (examples: stripe-journal-parity, stripe-ledger-sides, stripe-ledger)
\[ \forall (d\ c : \mathbb{N}), d - c + (c - d) = 0 \leftrightarrow d = c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2148}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_postings\_parity\_reflected}}
textbook\_postings\_parity reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-journal-parity, stripe-ledger-sides, stripe-ledger)
\[ \forall (d\ c : \mathbb{N}), d = c \leftrightarrow d - c + (c - d) = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2152}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_power\_of\_two}}
the powers of two double: 2\^{}(n+1) = 2·2ⁿ for every n (2⁴ = 2·2³). From Mathlib's `pow\_succ'` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(n + 1)} = 2 \cdot 2 ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1867}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_power\_of\_two\_reflected}}
textbook\_power\_of\_two reflected by its shape (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot 2 ^{n} = 2 ^{(n + 1)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1871}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_pqc\_standards\_three}}
the three NIST post-quantum standards of 13 August 2024, FIPS 203 (ML-KEM), 204 (ML-DSA) and 205 (SLH-DSA), numbered one after another past FIPS 202; the sizes the seal tool plans recompute from the standards' parameters: an ML-KEM-768 encapsulation key is 384k + 32 bytes with k = 3 (1184), its ciphertext 32(du·k + dv) with du = 10 and dv = 4 (1088), an SLH-DSA-SHA2-128s signature n(1 + k(1 + a) + h + d·len) with n = 16, k = 14, a = 12, h = 63, d = 7, len = 35 (7856) (examples: seal-kem, seal-slh, seal-nist, seal-nist-reverse-cern)
\[ 202 + 1 = 203 \land 203 + 1 = 204 \land 204 + 1 = 205 \land 205 - 202 = 3 \land 384 \cdot 3 + 32 = 1184 \land 32 \cdot (10 \cdot 3 + 4) = 1088 \land 16 \cdot (1 + 14 \cdot (1 + 12) + 63 + 7 \cdot 35) = 7856 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2268}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_pqc\_standards\_three\_reflected}}
textbook\_pqc\_standards\_three reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: seal-kem, seal-slh, seal-nist, seal-nist-reverse-cern)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 203 = \sigma - (202 + 1) \land \sigma - 204 = \sigma - (203 + 1) \land \sigma - 205 = \sigma - (204 + 1) \land \sigma - 3 = \sigma - (205 - 202) \land \sigma - 1184 = \sigma - (384 \cdot 3 + 32) \land \sigma - 1088 = \sigma - 32 \cdot (10 \cdot 3 + 4) \land \sigma - 7856 = \sigma - 16 \cdot (1 + 14 \cdot (1 + 12) + 63 + 7 \cdot 35) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2270}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_precommitment\_is\_sha512}}
a pulse commits to its successor's local random value by a hash: a value whose hash differs from the precommitment is not the value that was committed, whatever the hash (SHA-512 in the beacon, 512 bits). From Lean's `ne\_of\_apply\_ne` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} (\mathrm{hash} : \alpha \to \beta ) (\mathrm{committed}\ \mathrm{revealed} : \alpha ), \mathrm{hash}\ \mathrm{revealed} \ne \mathrm{hash}\ \mathrm{committed} \to \mathrm{revealed} \ne \mathrm{committed} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2279}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_precommitment\_is\_sha512\_reflected}}
textbook\_precommitment\_is\_sha512 reflected by its shape (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} (\mathrm{hash} : \alpha \to \beta ) (\mathrm{committed}\ \mathrm{revealed} : \alpha ), \mathrm{hash}\ \mathrm{revealed} \ne \mathrm{hash}\ \mathrm{committed} \to \mathrm{committed} \ne \mathrm{revealed} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2282}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_prices\_are\_variants\_times\_currencies}}
every variant priced in every currency is one price: variants × currencies prices. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_prod` (examples: stripe-catalogue)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{Variant}\ \mathrm{Currency} : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Variant}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Currency}], \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Variant} \times \mathrm{Currency}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Variant} \cdot \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Currency} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L52}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_prices\_are\_variants\_times\_currencies\_reflected}}
textbook\_prices\_are\_variants\_times\_currencies reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-catalogue)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{Variant}\ \mathrm{Currency} : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Variant}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Currency}], \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Variant} \cdot \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Currency} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Variant} \times \mathrm{Currency}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L57}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_prizes\_computable}}
only a winnable basis counts: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when purse, fund, per-problem, invested and non-monetary make the total, the total less the invested and the non-monetary is the winnable. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub`, `add\_assoc` and `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right` (examples: compete-prizes)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{purse}\ \mathrm{fund}\ \mathrm{perproblem}\ \mathrm{invested}\ \mathrm{none}\ \mathrm{total}\ \mathrm{winnable} : \alpha ), \mathrm{purse} + \mathrm{fund} + \mathrm{perproblem} + \mathrm{invested} + \mathrm{none} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{winnable} = \mathrm{purse} + \mathrm{fund} + \mathrm{perproblem} \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{invested} - \mathrm{none} = \mathrm{winnable} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L365}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_prizes\_computable\_reflected}}
textbook\_prizes\_computable reflected by its shape (examples: compete-prizes)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{purse}\ \mathrm{fund}\ \mathrm{perproblem}\ \mathrm{invested}\ \mathrm{none}\ \mathrm{total}\ \mathrm{winnable} : \alpha ), \mathrm{purse} + \mathrm{fund} + \mathrm{perproblem} + \mathrm{invested} + \mathrm{none} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{winnable} = \mathrm{purse} + \mathrm{fund} + \mathrm{perproblem} \to \mathrm{winnable} = \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{invested} - \mathrm{none} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L372}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_processor\_cells}}
f facets over k devices admit kᶠ combinations, every one a final cell, none left open (one facet of three devices: 3). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_fin`, `tsub\_self`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: dashboard-processor)
\[ \forall (f\ k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ f \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k) = k ^{f} \land k ^{f} - k ^{f} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1094}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_processor\_cells\_reflected}}
textbook\_processor\_cells reflected by its shape (examples: dashboard-processor)
\[ \forall (f\ k : \mathbb{N}), k ^{f} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ f \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k) \land 0 = k ^{f} - k ^{f} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1100}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_programs\_partition}}
the programs and the leads partition the files: p + (f − p) = f when p ≤ f. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-programs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{programs}\ \mathrm{files} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{programs} \le \mathrm{files} \to \mathrm{programs} + (\mathrm{files} - \mathrm{programs}) = \mathrm{files} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1051}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_programs\_partition\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_programs\_partition (examples: audit-programs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{programs}\ \mathrm{files} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{programs} + (\mathrm{files} - \mathrm{programs}) = \mathrm{files} \to \neg \mathrm{programs} \le \mathrm{files} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1056}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_programs\_partition\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_programs\_partition, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: audit-programs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{programs}\ \mathrm{files} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{programs} + (\mathrm{files} - \mathrm{programs}) = \mathrm{files} \to \mathrm{programs} \le \mathrm{files} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1058}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_programs\_partition\_reflected}}
textbook\_programs\_partition reflected by its shape (examples: audit-programs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{programs}\ \mathrm{files} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{programs} \le \mathrm{files} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{files} = \sigma - (\mathrm{programs} + (\mathrm{files} - \mathrm{programs})) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1054}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_qaoa\_params}}
a QAOA circuit has a parameters a layer, its angles: one more layer is a more parameters, a(p + 1) = ap + a (two angles: 7 layers have 14). From Mathlib's `mul\_add\_one` and `Distrib.leftDistribClass` (examples: clay-relation-14)
\[ \forall (p : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot (p + 1) = 2 \cdot p + 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2440}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_qaoa\_params\_reflected}}
textbook\_qaoa\_params reflected by its shape (examples: clay-relation-14)
\[ \forall (p \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (2 \cdot p + 2) = \sigma - 2 \cdot (p + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2443}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_qaoa\_random\_cut}}
each edge is cut by a random assignment with probability one half, so the expected cut is half the total weight by linearity (16.875 of 33.75). From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_mul` (examples: algorithm-qaoa)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{edges} : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (w : \iota \to \mathbb{Q}), \sum e \in \mathrm{edges}, w\ e \cdot (1 / 2) = (\sum e \in \mathrm{edges}, w\ e) \cdot (1 / 2) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1311}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_qaoa\_random\_cut\_reflected}}
textbook\_qaoa\_random\_cut reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-qaoa)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{edges} : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (w : \iota \to \mathbb{Q}), (\sum e \in \mathrm{edges}, w\ e) \cdot (1 / 2) = \sum e \in \mathrm{edges}, w\ e \cdot (1 / 2) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1316}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_qft\_three\_amplitudes}}
n outcomes of equal probability 1/n make the whole (the QFT on three qubits: 8 amplitudes of 12.50\% each). From Mathlib's `Finset.sum\_const`, `Finset.card\_range`, `nsmul\_eq\_mul`, `mul\_one\_div\_cancel` and `Nat.cast\_ne\_zero`, through the instance `FloorSemiring.instCharZero` (examples: algorithm-qft-3)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to \sum \mathrm{\_i} \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ n, 1 / \uparrow n = 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1177}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_qft\_three\_amplitudes\_reflected}}
textbook\_qft\_three\_amplitudes reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-qft-3)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to 1 = \sum \mathrm{\_i} \in \mathrm{Finset.range}\ n, 1 / \uparrow n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1181}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_queneau\_compositions}}
k choices on each of n strips are k\^{}n compositions, the functions from strips to choices (Queneau: ten on fourteen, 10\^{}14 poems). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: textbook-queneau, textbook-prose-queneau, fold-combinatorics)
\[ \forall (n\ k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k) = k ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1451}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_queneau\_compositions\_reflected}}
textbook\_queneau\_compositions reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-queneau, textbook-prose-queneau, fold-combinatorics)
\[ \forall (n\ k : \mathbb{N}), k ^{n} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1456}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_questions\_matched}}
every question read is decided or a lead: the questions less the leads are the decided ones. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: match-questions)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{decided}\ \mathrm{leads}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{decided} + \mathrm{leads} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{leads} = \mathrm{decided} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1958}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_questions\_matched\_reflected}}
textbook\_questions\_matched reflected by its shape (examples: match-questions)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{decided}\ \mathrm{leads}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{decided} + \mathrm{leads} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{decided} = \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{leads} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1962}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_raid\_bit\_zero\_verify}}
when the verified, missing and differing copies make the bindings and one copy is verified, the rebuilds (missing and differing) are fewer than the bindings. From Mathlib's `add\_comm`, `add\_assoc` and `lt\_add\_of\_pos\_right`, through the instances `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: raid-bit-zero-verify)
\[ \forall (v\ m\ d\ b : \mathbb{N}), v + m + d = b \to 1 \le v \to m + d + v = b \land m + d < b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2036}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_raid\_bit\_zero\_verify\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_raid\_bit\_zero\_verify (examples: raid-bit-zero-verify)
\[ \forall (v\ m\ d\ b : \mathbb{N}), \neg (m + d + v = b \land m + d < b) \to \neg (v + m + d = b \land 1 \le v) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2043}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_raid\_bit\_zero\_verify\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_raid\_bit\_zero\_verify, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_assoc`, `add\_comm`, `lt\_add\_iff\_pos\_right`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: raid-bit-zero-verify)
\[ \forall (v\ m\ d\ b : \mathbb{N}), m + d + v = b \land m + d < b \to v + m + d = b \land 1 \le v \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2045}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_raid\_bit\_zero\_verify\_reflected}}
textbook\_raid\_bit\_zero\_verify reflected by its shape (examples: raid-bit-zero-verify)
\[ \forall (v\ m\ d\ b : \mathbb{N}), v + m + d = b \to 1 \le v \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), b \le \sigma \to \sigma - b = \sigma - (m + d + v) \land \sigma - b < \sigma - (m + d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2041}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_raid\_no\_missing\_store}}
covered and missing stores make the whole, so with none missing the covered are all of them. From Mathlib's `add\_zero` (examples: raid-bit-zero-verify)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{covered}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{covered} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{missing} = 0 \to \mathrm{covered} = \mathrm{total} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1607}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_raid\_no\_missing\_store\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_raid\_no\_missing\_store (examples: raid-bit-zero-verify)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{covered}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{covered} = \mathrm{total} \to \neg (\mathrm{covered} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{total} \land \mathrm{missing} = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1613}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_raid\_no\_missing\_store\_reflected}}
textbook\_raid\_no\_missing\_store reflected by its shape (examples: raid-bit-zero-verify)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{covered}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{covered} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{missing} = 0 \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{total} = \sigma - \mathrm{covered} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1611}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_rbac\_options}}
a role's allowed combinations and the ones it may not make are all of them: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), (a − l) + l = a when l ≤ a. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le` (examples: fold-harmonic-rbac)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{allowed}\ \mathrm{all} : \alpha ), \mathrm{allowed} \le \mathrm{all} \to \mathrm{all} - \mathrm{allowed} + \mathrm{allowed} = \mathrm{all} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1989}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_rbac\_options\_reflected}}
textbook\_rbac\_options reflected by its shape (examples: fold-harmonic-rbac)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{allowed}\ \mathrm{all} : \alpha ), \mathrm{allowed} \le \mathrm{all} \to \mathrm{all} = \mathrm{all} - \mathrm{allowed} + \mathrm{allowed} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1994}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_readiness\_judged}}
the unit judges every lead served, refuted or in trial, so the leads less those in trial are the settled ones. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: readiness-plan)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{refuted}\ \mathrm{trial}\ \mathrm{leads} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{refuted} + \mathrm{trial} = \mathrm{leads} \to \mathrm{leads} - \mathrm{trial} = \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{refuted} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L912}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_readiness\_judged\_reflected}}
textbook\_readiness\_judged reflected by its shape (examples: readiness-plan)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{refuted}\ \mathrm{trial}\ \mathrm{leads} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{refuted} + \mathrm{trial} = \mathrm{leads} \to \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{refuted} = \mathrm{leads} - \mathrm{trial} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L916}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_receipt\_binds\_sha256}}
n hex characters hold 2\^{}(4n) values, so a 256-bit SHA-256 digest is written as 64 hex characters. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_fin` and `pow\_mul` (examples: algorithm-factor-order-composite, algorithm-impossible-decode-wrong-witness, algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin} (2 ^{4})) = 2 ^{(4 \cdot n)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2104}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_receipt\_binds\_sha256\_reflected}}
textbook\_receipt\_binds\_sha256 reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-factor-order-composite, algorithm-impossible-decode-wrong-witness, algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(4 \cdot n)} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin} (2 ^{4})) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2109}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_receipts\_partitioned}}
a changed fold partitions the recorded receipts into those that hold and the invalidated (stale or wrong), so the invalidated are the receipts less those that hold (3 − 1 = 2 on quintessay-payload's receipts). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` (examples: seal-receipts)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Receipt} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Receipt}] (\mathrm{receipts}\ \mathrm{holds} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Receipt}), \mathrm{holds} \subseteq \mathrm{receipts} \to (\mathrm{receipts} \setminus \mathrm{holds})\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{receipts.card} - \mathrm{holds.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2336}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_receipts\_partitioned\_reflected}}
textbook\_receipts\_partitioned reflected by its shape (examples: seal-receipts)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Receipt} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Receipt}] (\mathrm{receipts}\ \mathrm{holds} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Receipt}), \mathrm{holds} \subseteq \mathrm{receipts} \to \mathrm{receipts.card} - \mathrm{holds.card} = (\mathrm{receipts} \setminus \mathrm{holds})\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2341}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_redact\_before\_publish}}
redact before publish: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when every leak is redacted none is left, l − l = 0. From Mathlib's `tsub\_self` (examples: machine-redact)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommMonoid} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{CanonicallyOrderedAdd} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_3} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{leaks}\ \mathrm{redacted} : \alpha ), \mathrm{redacted} = \mathrm{leaks} \to \mathrm{leaks} - \mathrm{redacted} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1432}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_redact\_before\_publish\_reflected}}
textbook\_redact\_before\_publish reflected by its shape (examples: machine-redact)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommMonoid} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{CanonicallyOrderedAdd} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_3} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{leaks}\ \mathrm{redacted} : \alpha ), \mathrm{redacted} = \mathrm{leaks} \to 0 = \mathrm{leaks} - \mathrm{redacted} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1437}
