\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reed\_muller}}
a code of minimum distance d decodes uniquely up to ⌊(d−1)/2⌋ errors: two codewords within that Hamming distance of one received word coincide (RM(1, 3) = [8, 4, 4] corrects 1). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle` and `hammingDist\_comm` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}), 0 < d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} r : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{2} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1798}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reed\_muller\_reflected}}
textbook\_reed\_muller reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d : \mathbb{N}), 0 < d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} r : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{2} r \le (d - 1) / 2 \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1807}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reed\_solomon\_mds}}
a code of minimum distance d decodes uniquely up to t errors when 2t < d: a word within t of two codewords forces them equal (RS(255, 223), d = 33, corrects 16 symbol errors). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{F : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ F] \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (d\ t : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to F), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to 2 \cdot t < d \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} x \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1754}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reed\_solomon\_mds\_reflected}}
textbook\_reed\_solomon\_mds reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{F : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ F] \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (d\ t : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to F), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to 2 \cdot t < d \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} x \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1762}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_referer\_binds\_not\_entropy}}
a component with a single possible value, the public recomputable referer, adds no states: the secret paired with it has the secret's cardinality (2\^{}256 for 32 bytes, 0 extra bits). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_prod`, `Fintype.card\_unit` and `mul\_one` (examples: seal-referer)
\[ \forall (\alpha : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \alpha ], \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\alpha \times \mathrm{Unit}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} \alpha  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L713}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_referer\_binds\_not\_entropy\_reflected}}
textbook\_referer\_binds\_not\_entropy reflected by its shape (examples: seal-referer)
\[ \forall (\alpha : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \alpha ], \mathrm{Fintype.card} \alpha = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\alpha \times \mathrm{Unit}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L718}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reflect\_widths}}
a cut of t nibbles into n faces gives each face the quotient q = t / n wide and the first r = t \% n faces one wider, and the widths add back to t: (n − r) · q + r · = t. From Mathlib's `mul\_add\_one`, `add\_assoc`, `add\_mul`, `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `Nat.div\_add\_mod`, `Nat.mod\_lt`, `Distrib.leftDistribClass`, `Distrib.rightDistribClass`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `LT.lt.le` (examples: fold-reflect-widths)
\[ \forall (t\ n : \mathbb{N}), 0 < n \to (n - t \bmod n) \cdot (t / n) + t \bmod n \cdot (t / n + 1) = t \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2083}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reflect\_widths\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_reflect\_widths (examples: fold-reflect-widths)
\[ \forall (t\ n : \mathbb{N}), \neg (n - t \bmod n) \cdot (t / n) + t \bmod n \cdot (t / n + 1) = t \to \neg 0 < n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2090}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reflect\_widths\_reflected}}
textbook\_reflect\_widths reflected by its shape (examples: fold-reflect-widths)
\[ \forall (t\ n : \mathbb{N}), 0 < n \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - t = \sigma - ((n - t \bmod n) \cdot (t / n) + t \bmod n \cdot (t / n + 1)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2088}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reflection\_by\_shape}}
σ reverses order under truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), a ≤ b gives d − b ≤ d − a, and an implication gives its contrapositive. From Mathlib's `tsub\_le\_tsub\_left` (examples: textbook-reflected)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Preorder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] (d\ a\ b : \alpha ), a \le b \to \forall (P\ Q : \mathrm{Prop}), (P \to Q) \to d - b \le d - a \land (\neg Q \to \neg P) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2048}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reflection\_sums\_to\_ten}}
a seed and its reflection σ₁₀(s) = 10 − s sum to ten. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: css-stylesheet)
\[ \forall s \le 10, s + (10 - s) = 10 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L66}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_reflection\_sums\_to\_ten\_reflected}}
textbook\_reflection\_sums\_to\_ten reflected by its shape (examples: css-stylesheet)
\[ \forall s \le 10, 10 = s + (10 - s) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L69}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_refuse\_is\_refuse}}
an even order r with x = a\^{}(r/2) gives N ∣ x² − 1 = (x + 1)(x − 1), so gcd(x ± 1, N) are the candidate factors (2 has order 6 modulo 21, x = 8, splitting 21 into 3 and 7). From Mathlib's `Nat.sq\_sub\_sq` and `one\_pow` (examples: algorithm-shor-odd-order)
\[ \forall (N\ x : \mathbb{N}), N \mid x ^{2} - 1 \to N \mid (x + 1) \cdot (x - 1) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2447}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_register\_held}}
30 digits of w qubits, the version digit none and the variant digit w − 2 hold 31w − 2 = 32w − w − 2 qubits at every width w ≥ 2. From Mathlib's `add\_zero`, `add\_tsub\_assoc\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: processor-register)
\[ \forall (w : \mathbb{N}), 2 \le w \to 30 \cdot w + 0 + (w - 2) = 32 \cdot w - w - 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1125}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_register\_held\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_register\_held (examples: processor-register)
\[ \forall (w : \mathbb{N}), \neg 30 \cdot w + 0 + (w - 2) = 32 \cdot w - w - 2 \to \neg 2 \le w \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1133}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_register\_held\_reflected}}
textbook\_register\_held reflected by its shape (examples: processor-register)
\[ \forall (w : \mathbb{N}), 2 \le w \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (32 \cdot w - w - 2) = \sigma - (30 \cdot w + 0 + (w - 2)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1131}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_registry\_words\_searched}}
k searches of at most b results each find at most k · b results, |⋃ t i| ≤ |s| · b (three words, ten servers each: thirty at most). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_biUnion\_le\_card\_mul` (examples: discover-lead-registry)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \iota \to \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (b : \mathbb{N}), (\forall i \in s, (t\ i)\mathrm{.card} \le b) \to (\mathrm{s.biUnion}\ t)\mathrm{.card} \le \mathrm{s.card} \cdot b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L892}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_relation\_rsa}}
Euler's theorem, the law RSA rests on: x\^{}φ(n) ≡ 1 (mod n) for every x coprime to n, of which the register's shorBase 8 and shorModulus 91 are one case. From Mathlib's `Nat.ModEq.pow\_totient` (examples: clay-relation-rsa)
\[ \forall (x\ n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{x.Coprime}\ n \to x ^{\mathrm{n.totient}} \equiv 1 [\mathrm{MOD}\ n] \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2053}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repair\_kernel\_top}}
the kernel advisory outranks any package advisory: the maximum of a priority and one at least as high is the higher. From Mathlib's `max\_eq\_right` (examples: audit-repair)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{kernel}\ \mathrm{pkg} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{pkg} \le \mathrm{kernel} \to \mathrm{max}\ \mathrm{pkg}\ \mathrm{kernel} = \mathrm{kernel} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L569}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repair\_kernel\_top\_reflected}}
textbook\_repair\_kernel\_top reflected by its shape (examples: audit-repair)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{kernel}\ \mathrm{pkg} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{pkg} \le \mathrm{kernel} \to \mathrm{kernel} = \mathrm{max}\ \mathrm{pkg}\ \mathrm{kernel} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L572}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repair\_live\_sla}}
when the served and the failed make the requests and none fails, every request is served. From Mathlib's `add\_zero` (examples: audit-repair)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{failed}\ \mathrm{requests} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{failed} = \mathrm{requests} \to \mathrm{failed} = 0 \to \mathrm{served} = \mathrm{requests} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L561}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repair\_live\_sla\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_repair\_live\_sla (examples: audit-repair)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{failed}\ \mathrm{requests} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{served} = \mathrm{requests} \to \neg (\mathrm{served} + \mathrm{failed} = \mathrm{requests} \land \mathrm{failed} = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L567}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repair\_live\_sla\_reflected}}
textbook\_repair\_live\_sla reflected by its shape (examples: audit-repair)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{served}\ \mathrm{failed}\ \mathrm{requests} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{served} + \mathrm{failed} = \mathrm{requests} \to \mathrm{failed} = 0 \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{requests} = \sigma - \mathrm{served} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L565}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repair\_partitions}}
the advisories less the unfixable are the fixable ones, which the public MCP repairs by an override. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset` (examples: audit-repair)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Advisory} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Advisory}] (\mathrm{advisories}\ \mathrm{unfixable} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Advisory}), \mathrm{unfixable} \subseteq \mathrm{advisories} \to (\mathrm{advisories} \setminus \mathrm{unfixable})\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{advisories.card} - \mathrm{unfixable.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L555}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repair\_partitions\_reflected}}
textbook\_repair\_partitions reflected by its shape (examples: audit-repair)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Advisory} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Advisory}] (\mathrm{advisories}\ \mathrm{unfixable} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Advisory}), \mathrm{unfixable} \subseteq \mathrm{advisories} \to \mathrm{advisories.card} - \mathrm{unfixable.card} = (\mathrm{advisories} \setminus \mathrm{unfixable})\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L559}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repetition\_code\_tenth}}
at p = 1/10 the repetition code fails with 3p² − 2p³ = (3·10 − 2)/10³ = 28/1000, below the raw 100/1000, on three data and two syndrome qubits (examples: qec-bit-flip-0.1)
\[ 3 \cdot 10 - 2 = 28 \land 28 < 100 \land 3 + 2 = 5 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1345}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repetition\_code\_tenth\_reflected}}
textbook\_repetition\_code\_tenth reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: qec-bit-flip-0.1)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 100 \le \sigma \to \sigma - 28 = \sigma - (3 \cdot 10 - 2) \land \sigma - 100 < \sigma - 28 \land \sigma - 5 = \sigma - (3 + 2) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1347}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repositories\_by\_language}}
when each repository has one language, the repositories number the sum over the languages of the repositories in each. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` (examples: repositories)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \mathrm{s.card} = \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1332}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repositories\_by\_language\_reflected}}
textbook\_repositories\_by\_language reflected by its shape (examples: repositories)
\[ \forall \{\iota \kappa : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \kappa ] (f : \iota \to \kappa ) (s : \mathrm{Finset} \iota ) (t : \mathrm{Finset} \kappa ), (\forall x \in s, f\ x \in t) \to \sum k \in t, \{x \in s | f\ x = k\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{s.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1337}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repository\_watch\_leads}}
leads grouped by kind number the sum over the kinds of each kind's leads (workflow, branch, tag, release, Zenodo, workspace, npm: 3 + 3 + 3 + 2 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 14). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sigma` (examples: repository-watch)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Kind} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Kind}] (\mathrm{Lead} : \mathrm{Kind} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (k : \mathrm{Kind}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Lead}\ k)], \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((k : \mathrm{Kind}) \times \mathrm{Lead}\ k) = \sum k, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Lead}\ k) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L989}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_repository\_watch\_leads\_reflected}}
textbook\_repository\_watch\_leads reflected by its shape (examples: repository-watch)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Kind} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Kind}] (\mathrm{Lead} : \mathrm{Kind} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (k : \mathrm{Kind}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Lead}\ k)], \sum k, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Lead}\ k) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((k : \mathrm{Kind}) \times \mathrm{Lead}\ k) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L994}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_rest\_rbac}}
2 directions under r roles are 2 · r role-direction pairs, |Bool × Fin r| = 2r (REST in and out under three tenant roles: six). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_prod`, `Fintype.card\_bool` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: legal-court-role)
\[ \forall (r : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Bool} \times \mathrm{Fin}\ r) = 2 \cdot r \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L611}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_rest\_rbac\_reflected}}
textbook\_rest\_rbac reflected by its shape (examples: legal-court-role)
\[ \forall (r : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot r = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Bool} \times \mathrm{Fin}\ r) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L616}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_retry\_supersedes}}
a retried step is its last attempt: whatever the earlier attempts were, the status the green rule reads is the last one, so a step next retried to green no longer refuses the commit; every attempt stays in the receipt as history (examples: repository-retry-supersedes)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{attempts} : \mathrm{List}\ \mathrm{Bool}) (\mathrm{last} : \mathrm{Bool}), (\mathrm{attempts} ++ [\mathrm{last}])\mathrm{.getLast}? = \mathrm{some}\ \mathrm{last} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L863}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_retry\_supersedes\_reflected}}
textbook\_retry\_supersedes reflected by its shape, equality (examples: repository-retry-supersedes)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{attempts} : \mathrm{List}\ \mathrm{Bool}) (\mathrm{last} : \mathrm{Bool}), \mathrm{some}\ \mathrm{last} = (\mathrm{attempts} ++ [\mathrm{last}])\mathrm{.getLast}? \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L865}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_roadmap\_decades}}
n decades from bᵐ reach b\^{}(m+n): powers multiply by adding exponents (100 · 10⁴ = 10⁶). From Mathlib's `pow\_add` (examples: roadmap-all)
\[ \forall (b\ m\ n : \mathbb{N}), b ^{m} \cdot b ^{n} = b ^{(m + n)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1162}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_roadmap\_decades\_reflected}}
textbook\_roadmap\_decades reflected by its shape (examples: roadmap-all)
\[ \forall (b\ m\ n : \mathbb{N}), b ^{(m + n)} = b ^{m} \cdot b ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1166}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_roadmap\_milestone\_three}}
milestone three of the six is one long-lived logical qubit from 10³ = 1000 physical qubits, and the four application bands span three decades, 50,000 to 50,000,000 physical qubits (examples: roadmap-milestone-3)
\[ 10 ^{3} = 1000 \land 3 \le 6 \land 50000 \cdot 10 ^{3} = 50000000 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1168}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_roadmap\_milestone\_three\_reflected}}
textbook\_roadmap\_milestone\_three reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: roadmap-milestone-3)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 1000 = \sigma - 10 ^{3} \land \sigma - 6 \le \sigma - 3 \land \sigma - 50000000 = \sigma - 50000 \cdot 10 ^{3} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1170}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_rosetta\_conventions}}
the Clay rosetta: the tree seeds a value by its digital root, n ≡ the sum of its decimal digits mod 9, and the unit by its last digit in ZMod 10, where σ₁₀ cancels, x + (−x) = 0; the two name the same seed only when n mod 9 and n mod 10 agree (3432: 3 against 2), a difference of convention, never of error. From Mathlib's `Nat.modEq\_nine\_digits\_sum` and `add\_neg\_cancel` (examples: clay-rosetta)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \equiv (\mathrm{Nat.digits}\ 10\ n)\mathrm{.sum} [\mathrm{MOD}\ 9] \land \uparrow n + -\uparrow n = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2025}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_route\_or\_lead}}
every prompt is routed or is a lead, no third case: a Boolean is true or false. From Mathlib's `Bool.dichotomy` (examples: match-route)
\[ \forall (b : \mathrm{Bool}), b = \mathrm{true} \lor b = \mathrm{false} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L878}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_route\_or\_lead\_reflected}}
textbook\_route\_or\_lead reflected by its shape (examples: match-route)
\[ \forall (b : \mathrm{Bool}), b = \mathrm{false} \lor b = \mathrm{true} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L881}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_route\_waves}}
items run at once last as long as the longest, never longer than one after another: max a b ≤ a + b. From Mathlib's `max\_le\_add\_of\_nonneg`, through the instances `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `covariant\_swap\_add\_of\_covariant\_add` (examples: leads-session-route)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{max}\ a\ b \le a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1548}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_route\_waves\_reflected}}
textbook\_route\_waves reflected by its shape (examples: leads-session-route)
\[ \forall (a\ b \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (a + b) \le \sigma - \mathrm{max}\ a\ b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1551}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_royalty\_conserved}}
royalty: shares paid p under the fee f and the remainder x = f − p make the fee exactly, p + x = f, and nothing is left, f − p − x = 0. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `tsub\_self`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` (examples: stripe-royalty, stripe-royalty-escrow)
\[ \forall (f\ p\ x : \mathbb{N}), p \le f \to x = f - p \to p + x = f \land f - p - x = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2161}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_royalty\_conserved\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_royalty\_conserved (examples: stripe-royalty, stripe-royalty-escrow)
\[ \forall (f\ p\ x : \mathbb{N}), \neg (p + x = f \land f - p - x = 0) \to \neg (p \le f \land x = f - p) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2168}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_royalty\_conserved\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_royalty\_conserved, proved. From Mathlib's `le\_self\_add`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: stripe-royalty, stripe-royalty-escrow)
\[ \forall (f\ p\ x : \mathbb{N}), p + x = f \land f - p - x = 0 \to p \le f \land x = f - p \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2170}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_royalty\_conserved\_reflected}}
textbook\_royalty\_conserved reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-royalty, stripe-royalty-escrow)
\[ \forall (f\ p\ x : \mathbb{N}), p \le f \to x = f - p \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - f = \sigma - (p + x) \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - (f - p - x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2166}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_royalty\_fractal}}
royalty shares f·q, f·q², … over any number of levels sum to at most f · q/(1 − q) for 0 ≤ q < 1, so the shares stay under the fee. From Mathlib's `Finset.mul\_sum`, `mul\_le\_mul\_of\_nonneg\_left`, `geom\_sum\_Ico\_le\_of\_lt\_one`, `Rat.instPosMulMono` and `Rat.instIsStrictOrderedRing` (examples: stripe-royalty)
\[ \forall (f\ q : \mathbb{Q}) (n : \mathbb{N}), 0 \le f \to 0 \le q \to q < 1 \to \sum i \in \mathrm{Finset.Ico}\ 1\ n, f \cdot q ^{i} \le f \cdot (q ^{1} / (1 - q)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2154}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_rsa\_beyond\_bound}}
every positive integer n sits between 2\^{}k and 2\^{}(k+1) for k = log₂ n, so its bit length is k + 1 and a comparison of widths is a comparison of these powers, not a register ceiling. From Mathlib's `Nat.pow\_log\_le\_self` and `Nat.lt\_pow\_succ\_log\_self` (examples: match-rsa-2048)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to 2 ^{\mathrm{Nat.log}} 2\ n \le n \land n < 2 ^{(\mathrm{Nat.log}\ 2\ n + 1)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1403}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_runnable\_leads}}
listRunnableLeads runs a lead exactly when it is unserved and names its door: a meet is the top only when both are. From Mathlib's `inf\_eq\_top\_iff` (examples: leads-runnable)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{SemilatticeInf} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{OrderTop} \alpha ] (\mathrm{unserved}\ \mathrm{door} : \alpha ), \mathrm{unserved} \sqcap \mathrm{door} = \top \to \mathrm{unserved} = \top \land \mathrm{door} = \top  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L784}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_runnable\_leads\_reflected}}
textbook\_runnable\_leads reflected by its shape (examples: leads-runnable)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{SemilatticeInf} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{OrderTop} \alpha ] (\mathrm{unserved}\ \mathrm{door} : \alpha ), \neg (\mathrm{unserved} = \top \land \mathrm{door} = \top ) \to \neg \mathrm{unserved} \sqcap \mathrm{door} = \top  \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L788}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_scan\_links}}
the links of a chain of frames are its consecutive pairs, one fewer than the frames (35 links between 36 frames). From Mathlib's `min\_eq\_right` and `tsub\_le\_self`, through the instances `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: verify-scan, verify-scan-one-frame)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} (l : \mathrm{List} \alpha ), (\mathrm{l.zip}\ \mathrm{l.tail})\mathrm{.length} = \mathrm{l.length} - 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L799}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_scan\_links\_reflected}}
textbook\_scan\_links reflected by its shape (examples: verify-scan, verify-scan-one-frame)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\} (l : \mathrm{List} \alpha ), \mathrm{l.length} - 1 = (\mathrm{l.zip}\ \mathrm{l.tail})\mathrm{.length} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L804}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_scan\_stream}}
the scannable stream: thirty-six frames (one per character of a UUID) of 108 milliseconds (the third harmonic) are 3888 milliseconds, the vortex period (432 × 9); a single frame links nothing, a stream reads the address (examples: verify-scan, css-media)
\[ 36 \cdot 108 = 432 \cdot 9 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L709}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_scan\_stream\_reflected}}
textbook\_scan\_stream reflected by its shape, equality (examples: verify-scan, css-media)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 432 \cdot 9 = \sigma - 36 \cdot 108 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L711}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_seal\_and\_content\_decided}}
an address and its σ mirror sum to the all-f word: x + (m − x) = m for every x ≤ m (0x70a13419 + 0x8f5ecbe6 = 0xffffffff). From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: verify-seal-and-content)
\[ \forall (m\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le m \to x + (m - x) = m \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1367}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_seal\_and\_content\_decided\_reflected}}
textbook\_seal\_and\_content\_decided reflected by its shape (examples: verify-seal-and-content)
\[ \forall (m\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le m \to m = x + (m - x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1371}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_search\_proven}}
when the proven and the unproven make the answers, the answers less the unproven are the proven. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, through the instances `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: match-search, textbook-search-programs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{proven}\ \mathrm{unproven}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{proven} + \mathrm{unproven} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{unproven} = \mathrm{proven} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1970}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_search\_proven\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_search\_proven (examples: match-search, textbook-search-programs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{proven}\ \mathrm{unproven}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{unproven} = \mathrm{proven} \to \neg \mathrm{proven} + \mathrm{unproven} = \mathrm{total} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1976}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_search\_proven\_reflected}}
textbook\_search\_proven reflected by its shape (examples: match-search, textbook-search-programs)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{proven}\ \mathrm{unproven}\ \mathrm{total} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{proven} + \mathrm{unproven} = \mathrm{total} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{proven} = \sigma - (\mathrm{total} - \mathrm{unproven}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1974}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_search\_resumes}}
a search resumed from the centre it reached covers the same centres as one search of both budgets: (start + k) + m = start + (k + m). From Mathlib's `add\_assoc` (examples: algorithm-factor-continues)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{start}\ k\ m : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{start} + k + m = \mathrm{start} + (k + m) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1629}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_search\_resumes\_reflected}}
textbook\_search\_resumes reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-factor-continues)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{start}\ k\ m \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (\mathrm{start} + (k + m)) = \sigma - (\mathrm{start} + k + m) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1632}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_security\_perspectives}}
the security audit from the client and the developer perspectives merged as one: the same count of checks from either side, c + d = d + c. From Mathlib's `add\_comm` (examples: audit-security)
\[ \forall (c\ d : \mathbb{N}), c + d = d + c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L540}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_security\_perspectives\_reflected}}
textbook\_security\_perspectives reflected by its shape (examples: audit-security)
\[ \forall (c\ d \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (d + c) = \sigma - (c + d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L543}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_session\_partition}}
runSession: every directive lands in exactly one of four classes, so the directives read are the sum of the four class counts. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` and `Finset.mem\_univ` (examples: leads-session)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{directives} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{cls} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 4), \mathrm{directives.card} = \sum j, \{x \in \mathrm{directives} | \mathrm{cls}\ x = j\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2013}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_session\_partition\_reflected}}
textbook\_session\_partition reflected by its shape (examples: leads-session)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{directives} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{cls} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Fin}\ 4), \sum j, \{x \in \mathrm{directives} | \mathrm{cls}\ x = j\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{directives.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2017}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sha256\_fips\_180\_4}}
a string of m · k bits is m chunks of k bits, the same 2\^{}(mk) values (FIPS 180-4: a SHA-256 digest of 256 bits is 32 bytes of 8 and 64 hexadecimal characters of 4; a 512-bit block is sixteen 32-bit words). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_bool`, `Fintype.card\_fin` and `pow\_mul'` (examples: seal-block-sha256)
\[ \forall (m\ k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (m \cdot k) \to \mathrm{Bool}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ m \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathrm{Bool}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2208}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sha256\_fips\_180\_4\_reflected}}
textbook\_sha256\_fips\_180\_4 reflected by its shape (examples: seal-block-sha256)
\[ \forall (m\ k : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ m \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathrm{Bool}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin} (m \cdot k) \to \mathrm{Bool}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2213}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sha256\_is\_64}}
a hex digit is four bits: n hex digits span 16ⁿ = 2\^{}(4n) values, so 64 hex digits hold a 256-bit SHA-256 digest. From Mathlib's `pow\_mul` (examples: seal-text, match-pattern-seal)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (2 ^{4}) ^{n} = 2 ^{(4 \cdot n)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L695}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sha256\_is\_64\_reflected}}
textbook\_sha256\_is\_64 reflected by its shape (examples: seal-text, match-pattern-seal)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(4 \cdot n)} = (2 ^{4}) ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L699}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sha3\_fips\_202}}
FIPS 202 §5.2 and §6.1: KECCAK[c] = SPONGE[KECCAK-p[1600, 24], pad10*1, 1600 − c], SHA3-256(M) = KECCAK[512](M || 01, 256) and SHA3-512(M) = KECCAK[1024](M || 01, 512): the capacity is twice the digest and the rate the rest of the 1600-bit state, 1088 and 576 bits; the seal tool computes both with sha3 (examples: seal-block-sha3)
\[ 1600 - 512 = 1088 \land 512 = 2 \cdot 256 \land 1600 - 1024 = 576 \land 1024 = 2 \cdot 512 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2215}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sha3\_fips\_202\_reflected}}
textbook\_sha3\_fips\_202 reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: seal-block-sha3)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 1088 = \sigma - (1600 - 512) \land \sigma - 2 \cdot 256 = \sigma - 512 \land \sigma - 576 = \sigma - (1600 - 1024) \land \sigma - 2 \cdot 512 = \sigma - 1024 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2217}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shards\_cover}}
the shard of item k among n shards is k mod n, a residue in ZMod n and so one of the n shards: every item is shared. From Mathlib's `ZMod.val\_natCast`, `ZMod.val\_lt` (examples: shard-share, audit-shard)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}) [\mathrm{NeZero}\ n] (k : \mathbb{N}), (\uparrow k)\mathrm{.val} = k \bmod n \land (\uparrow k)\mathrm{.val} < n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L330}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shards\_disjoint}}
an item belongs to one shard only: the shards \{k | k mod n = i\} are the fibres of one function, pairwise disjoint. From Mathlib's `pairwise\_disjoint\_fiber` (examples: shard-share, audit-shard)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Pairwise} (\mathrm{Function.onFun}\ \mathrm{Disjoint}\ \lambda \ i \mapsto (\lambda \ k \mapsto k \bmod n) ^{-}^{1}' \{i\}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L334}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shared\_verbs}}
listDeclarations sorts declarations into k kinds, so the declarations are the sum of the kinds' counts (2 + 2 + 1 + 1 = 6). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_eq\_sum\_card\_fiberwise` and `Finset.mem\_univ` (examples: audit-declarations)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{k : \mathbb{N}\} (\mathrm{decls} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{kind} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k), \mathrm{decls.card} = \sum j, \{x \in \mathrm{decls} | \mathrm{kind}\ x = j\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L751}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shared\_verbs\_reflected}}
textbook\_shared\_verbs reflected by its shape (examples: audit-declarations)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{k : \mathbb{N}\} (\mathrm{decls} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{kind} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Fin}\ k), \sum j, \{x \in \mathrm{decls} | \mathrm{kind}\ x = j\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{decls.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L756}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_fifteen\_order}}
the order of every unit a modulo n divides φ(n), the number of units, so the order Shor's algorithm measures divides the totient: the powers of 7 modulo 15 run 7, 4, 13, 1: the order is 4, which divides φ(15) = 8. From Mathlib's `orderOf\_dvd\_card` and `ZMod.card\_units\_eq\_totient` (examples: algorithm-shor-15)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}) [\mathrm{NeZero}\ n] (a : (\mathrm{ZMod}\ n)^{\times}), \mathrm{orderOf}\ a \mid \mathrm{n.totient} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1172}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_narrowed\_cells}}
the integers from a to b are b + 1 − a values, a finite menu (3 to 21: 19). From Mathlib's `Nat.card\_Icc` (examples: dashboard-algorithm-narrowed)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), (\mathrm{Finset.Icc}\ a\ b)\mathrm{.card} = b + 1 - a \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1102}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_narrowed\_cells\_reflected}}
textbook\_shor\_narrowed\_cells reflected by its shape (examples: dashboard-algorithm-narrowed)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), b + 1 - a = (\mathrm{Finset.Icc}\ a\ b)\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1106}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_nine\_code}}
a code of distance d ≥ 2t + 1 corrects t errors: no word lies within t of two codewords, by the triangle inequality of the Hamming distance (Shor's [[9, 1, 3]], a 3 × 3 concatenation, corrects ⌊(3 − 1)/2⌋ = 1 error of each kind). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle`, `hammingDist\_comm` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (x\ y\ w : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) (t : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot t + 1 \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ y \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ w\ x \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ w\ y \le t \to \mathrm{False} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1840}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_reduction}}
Shor's reduction, factoring as order-finding: if a\^{}(2s) ≡ 1 (mod N) and N divides neither a\^{}s − 1 nor a\^{}s + 1, then gcd(a\^{}s − 1, N) is a factor of N other than 1 and N, since (a\^{}s − 1)(a\^{}s + 1) = a\^{}(2s) − 1. From Mathlib's `Nat.modEq\_iff\_dvd'`, `Nat.ModEq.symm`, `pow\_mul'`, `mul\_comm`, `Nat.sq\_sub\_sq`, `one\_pow` and `Nat.Coprime.dvd\_mul\_left` (examples: algorithm-shor-reduction, algorithm-shor-beyond-bound)
\[ \forall (N\ a\ s : \mathbb{N}), a ^{(2 \cdot s)} \equiv 1 [\mathrm{MOD}\ N] \to 1 \le a ^{s} \to \neg N \mid a ^{s} - 1 \to \neg N \mid a ^{s} + 1 \to (a ^{s} - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \ne 1 \land (a ^{s} - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \ne N \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1580}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_reduction\_reflected}}
textbook\_shor\_reduction reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: algorithm-shor-reduction, algorithm-shor-beyond-bound)
\[ \forall (N\ a\ s : \mathbb{N}), a ^{(2 \cdot s)} \equiv 1 [\mathrm{MOD}\ N] \to 1 \le a ^{s} \to \neg N \mid a ^{s} - 1 \to \neg N \mid a ^{s} + 1 \to 1 \ne (a ^{s} - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \land N \ne (a ^{s} - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1590}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_sigma\_root}}
a nontrivial square root of one splits its modulus: if N divides (x − 1)(x + 1) but neither x − 1 nor x + 1, then gcd(x − 1, N) is neither 1 nor N, as 64 is for 91 = 7 · 13. From Mathlib's `Nat.Coprime.dvd\_mul\_left` (examples: algorithm-shor-91, clay-relation-rsa)
\[ \forall (N\ x : \mathbb{N}), N \mid (x - 1) \cdot (x + 1) \to \neg N \mid x - 1 \to \neg N \mid x + 1 \to (x - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \ne 1 \land (x - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \ne N \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1621}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_shor\_sigma\_root\_reflected}}
textbook\_shor\_sigma\_root reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: algorithm-shor-91, clay-relation-rsa)
\[ \forall (N\ x : \mathbb{N}), N \mid (x - 1) \cdot (x + 1) \to \neg N \mid x - 1 \to \neg N \mid x + 1 \to 1 \ne (x - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \land N \ne (x - 1)\mathrm{.gcd}\ N \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1627}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sigma\_involution}}
σ\_d(x) = d − x is an involution on 0, …, d under truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models): reflected twice it is x again (on a nibble d = 15), so every triangular witness maps back exactly to the UUID it witnesses. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le` (examples: verify-witnesses, verify-witnesses-merge, verify-radar)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (d\ x : \alpha ), x \le d \to d - (d - x) = x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L324}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sigma\_involution\_reflected}}
textbook\_sigma\_involution reflected by its shape (examples: verify-witnesses, verify-witnesses-merge, verify-radar)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{ExistsAddOfLE} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftMono} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_4} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (d\ x : \alpha ), x \le d \to x = d - (d - x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L328}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_signature\_over\_digest}}
a byte is eight bits: n bytes span 256ⁿ = 2\^{}(8n) values, so the 32-byte digest signed is 256 bits whatever the length of the text. From Mathlib's `pow\_mul` (examples: seal-sign)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), (2 ^{8}) ^{n} = 2 ^{(8 \cdot n)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L339}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_signature\_over\_digest\_reflected}}
textbook\_signature\_over\_digest reflected by its shape (examples: seal-sign)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(8 \cdot n)} = (2 ^{8}) ^{n} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L343}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_simon}}
counting by the first isomorphism theorem: a homomorphism's kernel times its image is the whole group, so the strings y with y·s = 0 over GF(2), the kernel of y ↦ y·s for s ≠ 0, are half of all strings (00 and 11 of the 4 for s = 11). From Mathlib's `AddSubgroup.index\_ker` and `AddSubgroup.card\_mul\_index` (examples: algorithm-simon)
\[ \forall \{G\ H : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddGroup}\ G] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddGroup}\ H] (\varphi : G \to + H), \mathrm{Nat.card} \uparrow \varphi \mathrm{.ker} \cdot \mathrm{Nat.card} \uparrow \varphi \mathrm{.range} = \mathrm{Nat.card}\ G \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1213}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_simon\_reflected}}
textbook\_simon reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-simon)
\[ \forall \{G\ H : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{AddGroup}\ G] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddGroup}\ H] (\varphi : G \to + H), \mathrm{Nat.card}\ G = \mathrm{Nat.card} \uparrow \varphi \mathrm{.ker} \cdot \mathrm{Nat.card} \uparrow \varphi \mathrm{.range} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1218}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sitemap\_leads}}
sitemapLeads: of u urls, the v refused or covered (c + l of them) and the u − v leads of their own sum to the urls handed over. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: leads-sitemap)
\[ \forall (u\ v\ c\ l : \mathbb{N}), v \le u \to c + l = v \to c + l + (u - v) = u \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2019}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sitemap\_leads\_reflected}}
textbook\_sitemap\_leads reflected by its shape (examples: leads-sitemap)
\[ \forall (u\ v\ c\ l : \mathbb{N}), v \le u \to c + l = v \to u = c + l + (u - v) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2023}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_size\_nonnegative}}
a size counted in bytes is never below zero, and neither is the sum of the sizes of a record's files. From Mathlib's `zero\_le`, through the instance `LinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero.toIsBotZeroClass` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{sizes} : \mathrm{List} \mathbb{N}), 0 \le \mathrm{sizes.sum} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2305}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_size\_ratio\_bound}}
a size ratio rounded down never overstates the exact ratio. From Mathlib's `Nat.cast\_div\_le`, through the instance `Rat.instIsStrictOrderedRing` (examples: css-compare, extend-compare)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{framework}\ \mathrm{qpu} : \mathbb{N}), \uparrow (\mathrm{framework} / \mathrm{qpu}) \le \uparrow \mathrm{framework} / \uparrow \mathrm{qpu} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L107}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_skills\_complete}}
the skills are complete when every toolbox has its skill, the index one more, and the named and unnamed terms make the vocabulary: named + (terms − named) = terms. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: textbook-scripts-skills)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{boxes}\ \mathrm{skilled}\ \mathrm{terms}\ \mathrm{named} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{skilled} = \mathrm{boxes} \to \mathrm{named} \le \mathrm{terms} \to \mathrm{skilled} + 1 = \mathrm{boxes} + 1 \land \mathrm{named} + (\mathrm{terms} - \mathrm{named}) = \mathrm{terms} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1521}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_skills\_complete\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_skills\_complete (examples: textbook-scripts-skills)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{boxes}\ \mathrm{skilled}\ \mathrm{terms}\ \mathrm{named} : \mathbb{N}), \neg (\mathrm{skilled} + 1 = \mathrm{boxes} + 1 \land \mathrm{named} + (\mathrm{terms} - \mathrm{named}) = \mathrm{terms}) \to \neg (\mathrm{skilled} = \mathrm{boxes} \land \mathrm{named} \le \mathrm{terms}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1527}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_skills\_complete\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_skills\_complete, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_right\_cancel`, `instIsRightCancelAddOfAddRightReflectLE`, `addRightReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLE`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_iff\_le`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: textbook-scripts-skills)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{boxes}\ \mathrm{skilled}\ \mathrm{terms}\ \mathrm{named} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{skilled} + 1 = \mathrm{boxes} + 1 \land \mathrm{named} + (\mathrm{terms} - \mathrm{named}) = \mathrm{terms} \to \mathrm{skilled} = \mathrm{boxes} \land \mathrm{named} \le \mathrm{terms} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1529}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_skills\_complete\_reflected}}
textbook\_skills\_complete reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-scripts-skills)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{boxes}\ \mathrm{skilled}\ \mathrm{terms}\ \mathrm{named} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{skilled} = \mathrm{boxes} \to \mathrm{named} \le \mathrm{terms} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (\mathrm{boxes} + 1) = \sigma - (\mathrm{skilled} + 1) \land \sigma - \mathrm{terms} = \sigma - (\mathrm{named} + (\mathrm{terms} - \mathrm{named})) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1525}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_software\_cirq\_implemented}}
the Software page's tools split into the implemented and the named: a predicate splits a finite set into those that satisfy it and those that do not. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_filter\_add\_card\_filter\_not` (examples: software)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{tools} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{implemented} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{implemented}], (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{implemented}\ \mathrm{tools})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{tools} | \neg \mathrm{implemented}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{tools.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1318}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_software\_cirq\_implemented\_reflected}}
textbook\_software\_cirq\_implemented reflected by its shape (examples: software)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{tools} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{implemented} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{implemented}], \mathrm{tools.card} = (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{implemented}\ \mathrm{tools})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{tools} | \neg \mathrm{implemented}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1323}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_solovay\_kitaev}}
e\^{}(2πi/n) has order n, so T's phase ω = e\^{}(2πi/8) has order 8, above the order 4 of S's i = e\^{}(2πi/4). From Mathlib's `Complex.isPrimitiveRoot\_exp` and `IsPrimitiveRoot.eq\_orderOf`, through the instance `Nat.instAtLeastTwoHAddOfNat` (examples: algorithm-solovay-kitaev)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to \mathrm{orderOf} (\mathrm{Complex.exp} (2 \cdot \uparrow \mathrm{Real.pi} \cdot \mathrm{Complex.I} / \uparrow n)) = n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1288}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_solovay\_kitaev\_reflected}}
textbook\_solovay\_kitaev reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-solovay-kitaev)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \ne 0 \to n = \mathrm{orderOf} (\mathrm{Complex.exp} (2 \cdot \uparrow \mathrm{Real.pi} \cdot \mathrm{Complex.I} / \uparrow n)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1293}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_stale\_receipt\_refuses\_deploy}}
a deploy waits on every receipt: the invalidated receipts are the recorded less those that hold, and when fewer hold than were recorded at least one is invalidated, enough to refuse (2 − 1 = 1). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_sdiff\_of\_subset`, `tsub\_pos\_of\_lt`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: repository-predeploy-receipts)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Receipt} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ \mathrm{Receipt}] (\mathrm{receipts}\ \mathrm{holds} : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathrm{Receipt}), \mathrm{holds} \subseteq \mathrm{receipts} \to \mathrm{holds.card} < \mathrm{receipts.card} \to (\mathrm{receipts} \setminus \mathrm{holds})\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{receipts.card} - \mathrm{holds.card} \land 0 < (\mathrm{receipts} \setminus \mathrm{holds})\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2343}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_standards\_sliced}}
the covered and the uncovered make the standards: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when c + u is the total, the total less u is c. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right` (examples: standards-validate, textbook-education)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{covered}\ \mathrm{uncovered}\ \mathrm{total} : \alpha ), \mathrm{covered} + \mathrm{uncovered} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{uncovered} = \mathrm{covered} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L121}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_standards\_sliced\_reflected}}
textbook\_standards\_sliced reflected by its shape (examples: standards-validate, textbook-education)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{covered}\ \mathrm{uncovered}\ \mathrm{total} : \alpha ), \mathrm{covered} + \mathrm{uncovered} = \mathrm{total} \to \mathrm{covered} = \mathrm{total} - \mathrm{uncovered} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L126}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_statements\_one\_topic}}
one statement chosen by its topic and the rest make the statements: the chosen one plus the others is the whole. From Mathlib's `add\_comm` and `Finset.card\_erase\_add\_one` (examples: statements-cirq)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{statements} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \forall a \in \mathrm{statements}, 1 + (\mathrm{statements.erase}\ a)\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{statements.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1325}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_statements\_one\_topic\_reflected}}
textbook\_statements\_one\_topic reflected by its shape (examples: statements-cirq)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (\mathrm{statements} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \forall a \in \mathrm{statements}, \mathrm{statements.card} = 1 + (\mathrm{statements.erase}\ a)\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1330}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_steane}}
a code of minimum distance d corrects t errors when 2t < d: a word within t of two codewords forces them equal (the Steane code, d = 3, corrects any single-qubit error). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle` (examples: algorithm-steane)
\[ \forall \{F : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ F] \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (d\ t : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to F), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to 2 \cdot t < d \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} x \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1295}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_steane\_css}}
a code of distance d ≥ 2t + 1 corrects t errors: a received word within t of a codeword is farther than t from every other, by the triangle inequality of the Hamming distance (the Steane [[7, 1, 3]] code, two Hamming(7, 4) checks, corrects ⌊(3 − 1)/2⌋ = 1 error of each kind). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle`, `hammingDist\_comm` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (x\ y\ w : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Bool}) (t : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot t + 1 \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ y \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ w\ x \le t \to t < \mathrm{hammingDist}\ w\ y \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1775}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_steane\_reflected}}
textbook\_steane reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-steane)
\[ \forall \{F : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ F] \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (d\ t : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to F), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to 2 \cdot t < d \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} x \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1302}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_step\_cache\_sound}}
a deterministic step of equal input addresses gives an equal receipt: a step whose inputs are unchanged since it last ran green is reused from its sealed record, never rerun; a step that reads the network, the clock or its own artefacts is never cached (examples: repository-steps-reused)
\[ \forall (f : \mathbb{N} \to \mathbb{N}) (k_{1} k_{2} : \mathbb{N}), k_{1} = k_{2} \to f\ k_{1} = f\ k_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L209}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_step\_cache\_sound\_reflected}}
textbook\_step\_cache\_sound reflected by its shape, equality (examples: repository-steps-reused)
\[ \forall (f : \mathbb{N} \to \mathbb{N}) (k_{1} k_{2} : \mathbb{N}), k_{1} = k_{2} \to f\ k_{2} = f\ k_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L211}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_stream\_filter}}
a filtered stream keeps f of its n rows and drops the rest: the stream less the dropped is the kept, whatever the hexes. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd` and `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: fold-stream)
\[ \forall (n\ f : \mathbb{N}), f \le n \to n - (n - f) = f \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L398}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_stream\_filter\_reflected}}
textbook\_stream\_filter reflected by its shape (examples: fold-stream)
\[ \forall (n\ f : \mathbb{N}), f \le n \to f = n - (n - f) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L401}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_stream\_no\_silence}}
every gap within the heartbeat and the heartbeat within a second put every gap within a second. From Mathlib's `le\_trans` (examples: repository-status-no-silence)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{gap}\ \mathrm{beat} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{gap} \le \mathrm{beat} \to \mathrm{beat} \le 1000 \to \mathrm{gap} \le 1000 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1491}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_stream\_no\_silence\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_stream\_no\_silence (examples: repository-status-no-silence)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{gap}\ \mathrm{beat} : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{gap} \le 1000 \to \neg (\mathrm{gap} \le \mathrm{beat} \land \mathrm{beat} \le 1000) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1496}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_stream\_no\_silence\_reflected}}
textbook\_stream\_no\_silence reflected by its shape (examples: repository-status-no-silence)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{gap}\ \mathrm{beat} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{gap} \le \mathrm{beat} \to \mathrm{beat} \le 1000 \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 1000 \le \sigma - \mathrm{gap} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1494}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_surface\_distance\_ladder}}
a distance-d code corrects t errors whenever 2t + 1 ≤ d: two codewords both within t of a received word are the same (d = 3 corrects 1, d = 5 corrects 2, d = 7 corrects 3, the surface code's distance ladder). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_pos`, `hammingDist\_triangle` and `hammingDist\_comm` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d\ t : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot t + 1 \le d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} y : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{1} \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1820}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_surface\_distance\_ladder\_reflected}}
textbook\_surface\_distance\_ladder reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{\iota : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype} \iota ] \{\beta : \iota \to \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (i : \iota ) \to \mathrm{DecidableEq} (\beta i)] (d\ t : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot t + 1 \le d \to \forall (c_{1} c_{2} y : (i : \iota ) \to \beta i), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{1} \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ y\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1831}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_sycamore\_couplers}}
the handshake lemma: a coupler graph's edge ends are twice its edges, so when no qubit has more than four neighbours, 2·\#couplers ≤ 4·\#qubits (Sycamore: 176 ends of 88 couplers within 4 × 54). From Mathlib's `SimpleGraph.sum\_degrees\_eq\_twice\_card\_edges`, `Finset.sum\_le\_sum`, `Finset.sum\_const`, `Finset.card\_univ`, `smul\_eq\_mul`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: processor-sycamore)
\[ \forall \{V : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ V] [\mathrm{DecidableEq}\ V] (G : \mathrm{SimpleGraph}\ V) [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{DecidableRel}\ \mathrm{G.Adj}], (\forall (v : V), \mathrm{G.degree}\ v \le 4) \to 2 \cdot \mathrm{G.edgeFinset.card} \le \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ V \cdot 4 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1119}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tamper\_detected}}
tampering is covered by the digest, not by refusing to search: a block's SHA-256 has 256 bits, so even a crafted collision takes about 2\^{}128 trials, more than 10\^{}38, while any modified copy is caught by one comparison and answered with the court's case (examples: seal-tamper-authentic)
\[ 256 / 2 = 128 \land 10 ^{38} < 2 ^{128} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1471}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tamper\_detected\_reflected}}
textbook\_tamper\_detected reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: seal-tamper-authentic)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{128} \le \sigma \to \sigma - 128 = \sigma - 256 / 2 \land \sigma - 2 ^{128} < \sigma - 10 ^{38} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1473}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tamper\_one\_nibble}}
one nibble changed, a (10) to b (11), moves the decided version to 6 against the stated 2: the seal alone decides, one check of four, no content needed (examples: verify-tampered)
\[ 11 - 10 = 1 \land 2 \ne 6 \land 1 + 3 = 4 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1373}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tamper\_one\_nibble\_reflected}}
textbook\_tamper\_one\_nibble reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: verify-tampered)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 1 = \sigma - (11 - 10) \land 6 \ne 2 \land \sigma - 4 = \sigma - (1 + 3) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1375}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_teamwork\_navigation}}
on n axes, two steps each, an actor has n · 2 neighbours, and with itself n · 2 + 1 (three axes: 6 and 7). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_option`, `Fintype.card\_prod`, `Fintype.card\_fin` and `Fintype.card\_bool` (examples: fold-navigate)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Option} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \times \mathrm{Bool})) = n \cdot 2 + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1542}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_teamwork\_navigation\_reflected}}
textbook\_teamwork\_navigation reflected by its shape (examples: fold-navigate)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), n \cdot 2 + 1 = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Option} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \times \mathrm{Bool})) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1546}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_teleport\_three\_qubits}}
one message qubit and a Bell pair of two are the three qubits; the two measured bits choose one of 2² = 4 corrections, and the three Pauli expectations X, Y, Z agree (examples: algorithm-teleportation)
\[ \forall b < 2, \forall r < 8, \mathrm{QuantumCommunication.teleport}\ b\ r = \mathrm{if}\ r \bmod 2 = b\ \mathrm{then}\ 1\ \mathrm{else}\ 0 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1189}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_textbook\_categories}}
toolboxes at most t, t at most e and examples at least e put the toolboxes at most the examples (12 ≤ 49). From Mathlib's `le\_trans` (examples: textbook-categories)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{examples}\ \mathrm{toolboxes}\ t\ e : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{toolboxes} \le t \to t \le e \to e \le \mathrm{examples} \to \mathrm{toolboxes} \le \mathrm{examples} \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1087}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_textbook\_categories\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_textbook\_categories (examples: textbook-categories)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{examples}\ \mathrm{toolboxes}\ t\ e : \mathbb{N}), \neg \mathrm{toolboxes} \le \mathrm{examples} \to \neg (\mathrm{toolboxes} \le t \land t \le e \land e \le \mathrm{examples}) \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1092}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_textbook\_categories\_reflected}}
textbook\_textbook\_categories reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-categories)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{examples}\ \mathrm{toolboxes}\ t\ e : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{toolboxes} \le t \to t \le e \to e \le \mathrm{examples} \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{examples} \le \sigma - \mathrm{toolboxes} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1090}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_textbooks\_partition}}
every open textbook is reached by a theorem, a lead of the domain, or outside it, so removing the outside and the domain ones leaves the routed: r + d + o − o − d = r. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: leads-sources-judged)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{routed}\ \mathrm{domain}\ \mathrm{outside} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{routed} + \mathrm{domain} + \mathrm{outside} - \mathrm{outside} - \mathrm{domain} = \mathrm{routed} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L886}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_textbooks\_partition\_reflected}}
textbook\_textbooks\_partition reflected by its shape (examples: leads-sources-judged)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{routed}\ \mathrm{domain}\ \mathrm{outside} \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{routed} = \sigma - (\mathrm{routed} + \mathrm{domain} + \mathrm{outside} - \mathrm{outside} - \mathrm{domain}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L890}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_thermal\_time\_sigma}}
the reflection σₙ(x) = n − x is an involution on 0 to n and pairs x with n − x summing to n (σ₁₀: 3 + 7 = 6 + 4 = 9 + 1 = 2 + 8 = 10, fixed at 5); a bridge-strength statement, not a physics claim. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le` and `add\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, through the instances `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd` and `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le n \to n - (n - x) = x \land x + (n - x) = n \]
