\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1643}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_thermal\_time\_sigma\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_thermal\_time\_sigma (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x : \mathbb{N}), \neg (n - (n - x) = x \land x + (n - x) = n) \to \neg x \le n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1650}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_thermal\_time\_sigma\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_thermal\_time\_sigma, proved. From Mathlib's `min\_eq\_right\_iff`, `tsub\_tsub\_eq\_min`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono` and `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x : \mathbb{N}), n - (n - x) = x \land x + (n - x) = n \to x \le n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1652}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_thermal\_time\_sigma\_reflected}}
textbook\_thermal\_time\_sigma reflected by its shape (examples: clay-reflection)
\[ \forall (n\ x : \mathbb{N}), x \le n \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - x = \sigma - (n - (n - x)) \land \sigma - n = \sigma - (x + (n - x)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1648}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_thousand\_bit\_three\_divides}}
three divides 2\^{}n + 1 for every odd n, since x + y divides x\^{}n + y\^{}n for odd n (2\^{}999 + 1 mod 3 = 0, the thousand-bit test integer's smallest prime factor). From Mathlib's `Odd.nat\_add\_dvd\_pow\_add\_pow`, `one\_pow` (examples: algorithm-factor-1000-bit)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Odd}\ n \to 3 \mid 2 ^{n} + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1946}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_thousand\_bit\_width}}
2\^{}n + 1 has exactly n + 1 bits for n ≥ 1: ⌊log₂(2\^{}n + 1)⌋ = n, since 2\^{}n ≤ 2\^{}n + 1 < 2\^{}(n+1), proved structurally (N = 2\^{}999 + 1 has 1000 bits, never computed). From Mathlib's `Nat.log\_eq\_iff` (examples: algorithm-factor-1000-bit, fold-speak-thousand, chat-thousand)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 1 \le n \to \mathrm{Nat.log}\ 2 (2 ^{n} + 1) = n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1938}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_thousand\_bit\_width\_reflected}}
textbook\_thousand\_bit\_width reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-factor-1000-bit, fold-speak-thousand, chat-thousand)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 1 \le n \to n = \mathrm{Nat.log}\ 2 (2 ^{n} + 1) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1944}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tools\_listed}}
a list read whole leaves nothing out: filtering by the always-true test returns the list, so every tool is counted. From Mathlib's `List.filter\_true` (examples: e2e-plan-reads-every-tool)
\[ \forall (l : \mathrm{List} \mathbb{N}), (\mathrm{List.filter} (\lambda \ x \mapsto \mathrm{true}) l)\mathrm{.length} = \mathrm{l.length} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L297}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tools\_listed\_reflected}}
textbook\_tools\_listed reflected by its shape (examples: e2e-plan-reads-every-tool)
\[ \forall (l : \mathrm{List} \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{l.length} = (\mathrm{List.filter} (\lambda \ x \mapsto \mathrm{true}) l)\mathrm{.length} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L301}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_toric\_torus\_code}}
a code of minimum distance d corrects t errors when 2t < d: a word within t of two codewords forces them equal (the toric code at d = 3 corrects ⌊(3 − 1)/2⌋ = 1 error). From Mathlib's `hammingDist\_triangle` (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{F : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ F] \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (d\ t : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to F), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to 2 \cdot t < d \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} x \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{1} = c_{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1730}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_toric\_torus\_code\_reflected}}
textbook\_toric\_torus\_code reflected by its shape (examples: ftqc-all)
\[ \forall \{F : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq}\ F] \{n : \mathbb{N}\} (d\ t : \mathbb{N}) (c_{1} c_{2} x : \mathrm{Fin}\ n \to F), (c_{1} \ne c_{2} \to d \le \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} c_{2}) \to 2 \cdot t < d \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ c_{1} x \le t \to \mathrm{hammingDist}\ x\ c_{2} \le t \to c_{2} = c_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1738}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_total\_at\_least\_page}}
the records read on a page are among all the records, so the page holds at most the total. From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_le\_card` (examples: none yet)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{page}\ \mathrm{all} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), \mathrm{page} \subseteq \mathrm{all} \to \mathrm{page.card} \le \mathrm{all.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2308}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_translate\_phrases}}
words translated, kept and untranslated number the sum of the three (5 + 1 + 0 = 6 distinct words). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` (examples: translate-bg)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{Translated}\ \mathrm{Kept}\ \mathrm{Untranslated} : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Translated}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Kept}] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Untranslated}], \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Translated} \oplus \mathrm{Kept} \oplus \mathrm{Untranslated}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Translated} + (\mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Kept} + \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Untranslated}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L933}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_translate\_phrases\_reflected}}
textbook\_translate\_phrases reflected by its shape (examples: translate-bg)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{Translated}\ \mathrm{Kept}\ \mathrm{Untranslated} : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Translated}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Kept}] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Untranslated}], \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Translated} + (\mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Kept} + \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{Untranslated}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Translated} \oplus \mathrm{Kept} \oplus \mathrm{Untranslated}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L939}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tree\_size\_positive}}
RFC 6962 §2.1: a Merkle tree over n > 1 leaves has depth ⌈log₂ n⌉, the least d with n ≤ 2\^{}d: 2\^{}(d − 1) < n ≤ 2\^{}d (three leaves: depth 2, one hash per level). From Mathlib's `Nat.pow\_pred\_clog\_lt\_self` and `Nat.le\_pow\_clog` (examples: seal-merkle)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 1 < n \to 2 ^{(\mathrm{Nat.clog}\ 2\ n)}\mathrm{.pred} < n \land n \le 2 ^{\mathrm{Nat.clog}} 2\ n \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2318}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_trotter}}
the first-order Trotter–Suzuki product (e\^{}(−iX t/n) e\^{}(−iZ t/n))\^{}n approximates e\^{}(−i(X+Z)t) with error O(t²/n), so the fidelity rises to 1 as the step count n grows (here from 1 to 32 steps); the fidelity is bounded by 1; proven by trotterFidelity (examples: algorithm-trotter)
\[ 32 > 1 \land 1 \le 1 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1233}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_trotter\_reflected}}
textbook\_trotter reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: algorithm-trotter)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 32 \le \sigma \to \sigma - 32 < \sigma - 1 \land \sigma - 1 \le \sigma - 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1235}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tsirelson}}
(a√x)² = a²x for x ≥ 0, so the quantum CHSH value 2√2, which chshValue computes in floating point, squares to 4 · 2 = 8, above the classical bound 2 squared. From Mathlib's `mul\_pow` and `Real.sq\_sqrt` (examples: algorithm-chsh)
\[ \forall (a\ x : \mathbb{R}), 0 \le x \to (a \cdot \surd x) ^{2} = a ^{2} \cdot x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1226}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tsirelson\_reflected}}
textbook\_tsirelson reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-chsh)
\[ \forall (a\ x : \mathbb{R}), 0 \le x \to a ^{2} \cdot x = (a \cdot \surd x) ^{2} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1231}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_tuning\_ratio}}
two frequencies divided by their gcd give coprime quotients with the same cross product, the ratio in lowest terms: gcd(432, 440) = 8, so 432 : 440 = 54 : 55. ISO 16 states the standard tuning frequency, the note A at 440 Hz, and does not state this ratio. From Lean core's `Nat.coprime\_div\_gcd\_div\_gcd`, `Nat.mul\_div\_assoc`, `Nat.gcd\_dvd\_left` and `Nat.gcd\_dvd\_right`, and Mathlib's `mul\_comm` (examples: clay-register)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), 0 < \mathrm{a.gcd}\ b \to (a / \mathrm{a.gcd}\ b)\mathrm{.Coprime} (b / \mathrm{a.gcd}\ b) \land a \cdot (b / \mathrm{a.gcd}\ b) = b \cdot (a / \mathrm{a.gcd}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1951}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_two\_pow\_odd\_mod\_three}}
two raised to any odd exponent is two modulo three: 4 ≡ 1 mod 3, so 2\^{}(2n+1) = 4\^{}n · 2 ≡ 2, no exponent evaluated (the lemma behind 3 dividing 2\^{}999 + 1). From Mathlib's `Nat.ModEq.mul\_right`, `Nat.ModEq.pow`, `one\_pow`, `pow\_succ` and `pow\_mul` (examples: algorithm-factor-1000-bit)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(2 \cdot n + 1)} \bmod 3 = 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1929}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_two\_pow\_odd\_mod\_three\_reflected}}
textbook\_two\_pow\_odd\_mod\_three reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-factor-1000-bit)
\[ \forall (n \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - 2 = \sigma - 2 ^{(2 \cdot n + 1)} \bmod 3 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1936}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ui\_deterministic}}
the app shell is a function of the key alone: the same key gives the same shell, at every host, every time
\[ \forall (f : \mathbb{N} \to \mathbb{N}) (k : \mathbb{N}), f\ k = f\ k \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L410}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_ui\_deterministic\_reflected}}
textbook\_ui\_deterministic reflected by its shape, equality
\[ \forall (f : \mathbb{N} \to \mathbb{N}) (k : \mathbb{N}), f\ k = f\ k \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L412}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_unlimited\_qubits}}
no register is the last: every qubit count has a larger one, and one more qubit doubles the states, 2\^{}(q + 1) = 2 · 2\^{}q. From Mathlib's `lt\_add\_one`, `pow\_succ'`, `Nat.instZeroLEOneClass`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` (examples: processor-unlimited, circuit-unlimited, echo-unlimited, match-unlimited, prose-unlimited, seal-unlimited, verify-unlimited, stripe-unlimited, fold-unlimited, repository-unlimited)
\[ \forall (q : \mathbb{N}), q < q + 1 \land 2 ^{(q + 1)} = 2 \cdot 2 ^{q} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2350}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_unlimited\_qubits\_reflected}}
textbook\_unlimited\_qubits reflected by its shape (examples: processor-unlimited, circuit-unlimited, echo-unlimited, match-unlimited, prose-unlimited, seal-unlimited, verify-unlimited, stripe-unlimited, fold-unlimited, repository-unlimited)
\[ \forall (q \sigma : \mathbb{N}), q + 1 \le \sigma \to \sigma - (q + 1) < \sigma - q \land \sigma - 2 \cdot 2 ^{q} = \sigma - 2 ^{(q + 1)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2353}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_url\_combinations}}
a set of n kinds has 2\^{}n − 1 non-empty combinations, its subsets but the empty one (four kinds of cached asset: fifteen). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_erase\_of\_mem`, `Finset.empty\_mem\_powerset` and `Finset.card\_powerset` (examples: match-url-fallback)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), (\mathrm{s.powerset.erase} \emptyset )\mathrm{.card} = 2 ^{\mathrm{s.card}} - 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L682}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_url\_combinations\_reflected}}
textbook\_url\_combinations reflected by its shape (examples: match-url-fallback)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidableEq} \alpha ] (s : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ), 2 ^{\mathrm{s.card}} - 1 = (\mathrm{s.powerset.erase} \emptyset )\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L687}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_use\_completes}}
the more the QPU is used the more complete it gets: adding what its sources answered never lowers what it held, n ≤ n + k. From Mathlib's `le\_self\_add` and `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd` (examples: leads-sources-plan)
\[ \forall (n\ k : \mathbb{N}), n \le n + k \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L883}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_core}}
fields of a, b, c and d nibbles make a + b + c + d nibbles (handle 8, program 10, tail 12, stamp 2: 32). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: fold-hex, match-pattern-uuid, imagine-all, fold-speak-thousand, chat-thousand)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c\ d : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ c \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ d) = a + b + c + d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L391}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_core\_reflected}}
textbook\_uuid\_core reflected by its shape (examples: fold-hex, match-pattern-uuid, imagine-all, fold-speak-thousand, chat-thousand)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c\ d : \mathbb{N}), a + b + c + d = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ c \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L396}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_is\_36}}
a string of a digits and b dashes has a + b characters (a UUID: 32 hex digits and 4 dashes, 36 characters). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum`, `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: legal-audit-offline)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) = a + b \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L59}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_is\_36\_reflected}}
textbook\_uuid\_is\_36 reflected by its shape (examples: legal-audit-offline)
\[ \forall (a\ b : \mathbb{N}), a + b = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L64}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_schemas}}
the RFC 9562 layouts split into the versioned and the rest (nil and max): a predicate splits a finite set into those that satisfy it and those that do not (7 + 2 = 9). From Mathlib's `Finset.card\_filter\_add\_card\_filter\_not` (examples: algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{layouts} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{versioned} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{versioned}], (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{versioned}\ \mathrm{layouts})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{layouts} | \neg \mathrm{versioned}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} = \mathrm{layouts.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L380}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_schemas\_reflected}}
textbook\_uuid\_schemas reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-factor-test-integer)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} (\mathrm{layouts} : \mathrm{Finset} \alpha ) (\mathrm{versioned} : \alpha \to \mathrm{Prop}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{DecidablePred}\ \mathrm{versioned}], \mathrm{layouts.card} = (\mathrm{Finset.filter}\ \mathrm{versioned}\ \mathrm{layouts})\mathrm{.card} + \{x \in \mathrm{layouts} | \neg \mathrm{versioned}\ x\}\mathrm{.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L385}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_time\_space}}
fields of a, b, c and d bits make a + b + c + d bits (time 48, version 4, variant 2, space 74: 128 for a version 7 UUID). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: fold-split)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c\ d : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ c \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ d) = a + b + c + d \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L132}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_uuid\_time\_space\_reflected}}
textbook\_uuid\_time\_space reflected by its shape (examples: fold-split)
\[ \forall (a\ b\ c\ d : \mathbb{N}), a + b + c + d = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ a \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ b \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ c \oplus \mathrm{Fin}\ d) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L137}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_validation\_helpers}}
helpers answered by a field option and by a validate function number the sum of both (5 + 9 = 14 Rails validation helpers). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sum` (examples: rails-validations-overview, rails-validations-helpers, rails-validations-options, rails-validations-conditional, rails-validations-strict, rails-validations-listing, rails-validations-custom, rails-validations-errors)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{ByOption}\ \mathrm{ByFunction} : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{ByOption}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{ByFunction}], \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{ByOption} \oplus \mathrm{ByFunction}) = \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{ByOption} + \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{ByFunction} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L777}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_validation\_helpers\_reflected}}
textbook\_validation\_helpers reflected by its shape (examples: rails-validations-overview, rails-validations-helpers, rails-validations-options, rails-validations-conditional, rails-validations-strict, rails-validations-listing, rails-validations-custom, rails-validations-errors)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{ByOption}\ \mathrm{ByFunction} : \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{ByOption}] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{ByFunction}], \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{ByOption} + \mathrm{Fintype.card}\ \mathrm{ByFunction} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{ByOption} \oplus \mathrm{ByFunction}) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L782}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_vocabulary\_measured}}
every term of a locale's vocabulary is known or missing, so the missing and the known make the terms whatever the vocabulary has grown to. From Mathlib's `tsub\_add\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `Nat.instOrderedSub` (examples: translate-coverage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{terms}\ \mathrm{known} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{known} \le \mathrm{terms} \to \mathrm{terms} - \mathrm{known} + \mathrm{known} = \mathrm{terms} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L941}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_vocabulary\_measured\_reflected}}
textbook\_vocabulary\_measured reflected by its shape (examples: translate-coverage)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{terms}\ \mathrm{known} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{known} \le \mathrm{terms} \to \mathrm{terms} = \mathrm{terms} - \mathrm{known} + \mathrm{known} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L944}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_vqe}}
the variational bound for H = Z: probabilities p₀ + p₁ = 1 give the energy ⟨Z⟩ = p₀ − p₁ ≥ −1, the least eigenvalue of Z, reached at p₁ = 1. From Mathlib's `neg\_le\_sub\_iff\_le\_add`, `add\_assoc`, `le\_add\_of\_nonneg\_left` and `add\_nonneg`, through the instances `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Real.instIsOrderedAddMonoid` and `covariant\_swap\_add\_of\_covariant\_add` (examples: algorithm-vqe, circuit-phased-xz)
\[ \forall (p_{0} p_{1} : \mathbb{R}), 0 \le p_{0} \to p_{0} + p_{1} = 1 \to -1 \le p_{0} - p_{1} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1220}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_wave\_requests\_by\_lead}}
a wave's requests grouped by lead number the sum over the leads of each lead's requests (3 + 2 + 7 · 3 + 1 + 1 = 28). From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_sigma` (examples: wave-requests)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Lead} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Lead}] (\mathrm{Request} : \mathrm{Lead} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (l : \mathrm{Lead}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Request}\ l)], \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((l : \mathrm{Lead}) \times \mathrm{Request}\ l) = \sum l, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Request}\ l) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L905}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_wave\_requests\_by\_lead\_reflected}}
textbook\_wave\_requests\_by\_lead reflected by its shape (examples: wave-requests)
\[ \forall \{\mathrm{Lead} : \mathrm{Type}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ \mathrm{Lead}] (\mathrm{Request} : \mathrm{Lead} \to \mathrm{Type}) [\mathrm{inst\_1} : (l : \mathrm{Lead}) \to \mathrm{Fintype} (\mathrm{Request}\ l)], \sum l, \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Request}\ l) = \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((l : \mathrm{Lead}) \times \mathrm{Request}\ l) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L910}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_webhook\_tolerance}}
a signature checked at time c is within the tolerance τ of its signing time s exactly when c ≤ s + τ: under truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models) c − s ≤ τ ↔ c ≤ s + τ (signed at 1700000000, checked 100 seconds later, within 300). From Mathlib's `tsub\_le\_iff\_left` (examples: stripe-webhook)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Preorder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{signed}\ \mathrm{checked}\ \mathrm{tol} : \alpha ), \mathrm{checked} - \mathrm{signed} \le \mathrm{tol} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{checked} \le \mathrm{signed} + \mathrm{tol} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L961}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_webhook\_tolerance\_reflected}}
textbook\_webhook\_tolerance reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-webhook)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Preorder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] (\mathrm{signed}\ \mathrm{checked}\ \mathrm{tol} : \alpha ), \mathrm{checked} \le \mathrm{signed} + \mathrm{tol} \leftrightarrow \mathrm{checked} - \mathrm{signed} \le \mathrm{tol} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L967}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_willow\_connectivity}}
the handshake lemma: the degrees of a graph sum to twice its edges, so the average connectivity is 2E/N (Willow's 182 couplers on 105 qubits: 364/105 ≈ 3.47). From Mathlib's `SimpleGraph.sum\_degrees\_eq\_twice\_card\_edges` (examples: processor-willow)
\[ \forall \{V : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ V] [\mathrm{DecidableEq}\ V] (G : \mathrm{SimpleGraph}\ V) [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{DecidableRel}\ \mathrm{G.Adj}], \sum v, \mathrm{G.degree}\ v = 2 \cdot \mathrm{G.edgeFinset.card} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L736}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_willow\_connectivity\_reflected}}
textbook\_willow\_connectivity reflected by its shape (examples: processor-willow)
\[ \forall \{V : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{Fintype}\ V] [\mathrm{DecidableEq}\ V] (G : \mathrm{SimpleGraph}\ V) [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{DecidableRel}\ \mathrm{G.Adj}], 2 \cdot \mathrm{G.edgeFinset.card} = \sum v, \mathrm{G.degree}\ v \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L742}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_willow\_distance\_seven}}
Willow's 105 qubits hold the 101 its distance-7 code uses, 97 of them the code's 49 data and 48 measure qubits (examples: match-willow)
\[ 7 \cdot 7 + (7 \cdot 7 - 1) = 97 \land 97 < 101 \land 101 \le 105 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1377}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_willow\_distance\_seven\_reflected}}
textbook\_willow\_distance\_seven reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: match-willow)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 101 \le \sigma \to \sigma - 97 = \sigma - (7 \cdot 7 + (7 \cdot 7 - 1)) \land \sigma - 101 < \sigma - 97 \land \sigma - 105 \le \sigma - 101 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1379}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_willow\_holds\_codes}}
Willow's 105 qubits hold the distance-7 code's 101 and the random-circuit application's 103, and its stated average connectivity 3.47 is below the typical 4 (hundredths) (examples: processor-willow)
\[ 101 \le 105 \land 103 \le 105 \land 347 < 400 \]
\noindent axioms: none; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1115}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_willow\_holds\_codes\_reflected}}
textbook\_willow\_holds\_codes reflected by its shape, conjunction (examples: processor-willow)
\[ \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 400 \le \sigma \to \sigma - 105 \le \sigma - 101 \land \sigma - 105 \le \sigma - 103 \land \sigma - 400 < \sigma - 347 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1117}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_books\_nothing}}
items passed, missing or contradicted sum to the items, and the coins booked are the passed alone, so k + m + c = n and k ≤ n − m − c. From Mathlib's `tsub\_tsub`, `add\_assoc`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right`, `le\_of\_eq`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `le\_refl` (examples: stripe-mint-witnesses, stripe-idea-contribution, stripe-royalty-escrow, verify-witnesses-merge)
\[ \forall (n\ p\ m\ c\ k : \mathbb{N}), p + m + c = n \to k = p \to k + m + c = n \land k \le n - m - c \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2173}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_books\_nothing\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_witness\_books\_nothing (examples: stripe-mint-witnesses, stripe-idea-contribution, stripe-royalty-escrow, verify-witnesses-merge)
\[ \forall (n\ p\ m\ c\ k : \mathbb{N}), \neg (k + m + c = n \land k \le n - m - c) \to \neg (p + m + c = n \land k = p) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2181}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_books\_nothing\_reflected}}
textbook\_witness\_books\_nothing reflected by its shape (examples: stripe-mint-witnesses, stripe-idea-contribution, stripe-royalty-escrow, verify-witnesses-merge)
\[ \forall (n\ p\ m\ c\ k : \mathbb{N}), p + m + c = n \to k = p \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - n = \sigma - (k + m + c) \land \sigma - (n - m - c) \le \sigma - k \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2179}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_clock\_move}}
a superposition at index k carries 2k witnesses, and a ±1 move in k is ±2 in the span: 2(k + 1) = 2k + 2 and 2(k − 1) = 2k − 2 (from 14 = 2 · 7 to 12 and 16; from 110 to 108 and 112). From Mathlib's `mul\_add\_one` and `Distrib.leftDistribClass` (examples: verify-witness-move)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot (k + 1) = 2 \cdot k + 2 \land 2 \cdot (k - 1) = 2 \cdot k - 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1685}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_clock\_move\_reflected}}
textbook\_witness\_clock\_move reflected by its shape (examples: verify-witness-move)
\[ \forall (k \sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - (2 \cdot k + 2) = \sigma - 2 \cdot (k + 1) \land \sigma - (2 \cdot k - 2) = \sigma - 2 \cdot (k - 1) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1690}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_gates\_decode}}
the witness gates the decode: for primes p ≤ q, a centre a and gap b with (a − b)(a + b) = p · q and 1 < a − b have a − b = p and a + b = q, so only the true witness decodes and every other candidate decodes nothing. From Witness.witness\_unique (examples: algorithm-impossible-decode-small, algorithm-impossible-decode-wrong-witness)
\[ \forall \{p\ q\ a\ b : \mathbb{N}\}, \mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ p \to \mathrm{Nat.Prime}\ q \to p \le q \to (a - b) \cdot (a + b) = p \cdot q \to 1 < a - b \to a - b = p \land a + b = q \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2117}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_move\_computed}}
the coins of a witness move are computed from the move: for every k ≥ 1 the step down from 2k to 2(k − 1) pays 1, the step up to 2(k + 1) collects 1, and the net is 0. From Mathlib's `mul\_tsub`, `Nat.instCanonicallyOrderedAdd`, `Nat.instOrderedSub`, `LE.total`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftReflectLE\_of\_addLeftReflectLT`, `AddLeftCancelSemigroup.toIsLeftCancelAdd`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `tsub\_tsub\_cancel\_of\_le`, `StarOrderedRing.toExistsAddOfLE`, `Nat.instStarOrderedRing`, `add\_tsub\_cancel\_left` and `mul\_one` (examples: verify-witness-move, verify-witness-move-110)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 1 \le k \to (2 \cdot k - 2 \cdot (k - 1)) / 2 = 1 \land (2 \cdot (k + 1) - 2 \cdot k) / 2 = 1 \land (2 \cdot (k + 1) - 2 \cdot k) / 2 - (2 \cdot k - 2 \cdot (k - 1)) / 2 = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2136}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_move\_computed\_reflected}}
textbook\_witness\_move\_computed reflected by its shape (examples: verify-witness-move, verify-witness-move-110)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}), 1 \le k \to 1 = (2 \cdot k - 2 \cdot (k - 1)) / 2 \land 1 = (2 \cdot (k + 1) - 2 \cdot k) / 2 \land 0 = (2 \cdot (k + 1) - 2 \cdot k) / 2 - (2 \cdot k - 2 \cdot (k - 1)) / 2 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2141}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_states}}
the present, missing and contradicted witnesses make the witnesses, and when none is missing and none contradicted every witness is present: a sum of naturals is zero only when each term is. From Mathlib's `add\_eq\_zero`, `add\_zero`, `Unique.instSubsingleton` (examples: match-witness-p-np, match-witness-planck, match-witness-planck-value, match-witness-negation, match-witness-willow, match-witness-willow-wrong, match-search-witnessed, chat-witness-planck, chat-witness-computed, match-witness-loose)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{present}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{contradicted}\ \mathrm{witnesses} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{present} + \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = \mathrm{witnesses} \to \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = 0 \to \mathrm{present} = \mathrm{witnesses} \land \mathrm{contradicted} = 0 \land \mathrm{missing} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2183}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_states\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_witness\_states (examples: match-witness-p-np, match-witness-planck, match-witness-planck-value, match-witness-negation, match-witness-willow, match-witness-willow-wrong, match-search-witnessed, chat-witness-planck, chat-witness-computed, match-witness-loose)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{present}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{contradicted}\ \mathrm{witnesses} : \mathbb{N}), \neg (\mathrm{present} = \mathrm{witnesses} \land \mathrm{contradicted} = 0 \land \mathrm{missing} = 0) \to \neg (\mathrm{present} + \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = \mathrm{witnesses} \land \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = 0) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2192}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_states\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_witness\_states, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_zero`, `add\_eq\_zero` and `Unique.instSubsingleton` (examples: match-witness-p-np, match-witness-planck, match-witness-planck-value, match-witness-negation, match-witness-willow, match-witness-willow-wrong, match-search-witnessed, chat-witness-planck, chat-witness-computed, match-witness-loose)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{present}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{contradicted}\ \mathrm{witnesses} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{present} = \mathrm{witnesses} \land \mathrm{contradicted} = 0 \land \mathrm{missing} = 0 \to \mathrm{present} + \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = \mathrm{witnesses} \land \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = 0 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2194}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_witness\_states\_reflected}}
textbook\_witness\_states reflected by its shape (examples: match-witness-p-np, match-witness-planck, match-witness-planck-value, match-witness-negation, match-witness-willow, match-witness-willow-wrong, match-search-witnessed, chat-witness-planck, chat-witness-computed, match-witness-loose)
\[ \forall (\mathrm{present}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{contradicted}\ \mathrm{witnesses} : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{present} + \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = \mathrm{witnesses} \to \mathrm{missing} + \mathrm{contradicted} = 0 \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), \sigma - \mathrm{witnesses} = \sigma - \mathrm{present} \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - \mathrm{contradicted} \land \sigma - 0 = \sigma - \mathrm{missing} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2190}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_coverage}}
held plus missing are the kinds the Discovery documents define: with truncated subtraction (ℕ among its models), when h + m is the kinds, the kinds less the missing are the held. From Mathlib's `add\_tsub\_cancel\_right` (examples: google-workspace-coverage)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{held}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{kinds} : \alpha ), \mathrm{held} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{kinds} \to \mathrm{kinds} - \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{held} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L489}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_coverage\_reflected}}
textbook\_workspace\_coverage reflected by its shape (examples: google-workspace-coverage)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} [\mathrm{inst} : \mathrm{PartialOrder} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_1} : \mathrm{AddCommSemigroup} \alpha ] [\mathrm{inst\_2} : \mathrm{Sub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{OrderedSub} \alpha ] [\mathrm{AddLeftReflectLE} \alpha ] (\mathrm{held}\ \mathrm{missing}\ \mathrm{kinds} : \alpha ), \mathrm{held} + \mathrm{missing} = \mathrm{kinds} \to \mathrm{held} = \mathrm{kinds} - \mathrm{missing} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L494}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_docs\_round\_trip}}
a round trip that keeps the shape keeps it through any number of round trips: shape ∘ back = shape gives shape ∘ backⁿ = shape. From Mathlib's `Function.Semiconj.iterate\_right` and `Function.iterate\_id` (examples: google-workspace-docs, google-workspace-docs-lost)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} (\mathrm{shape} : \alpha \to \beta ) (\mathrm{back} : \alpha \to \alpha ), (\forall (x : \alpha ), \mathrm{shape} (\mathrm{back}\ x) = \mathrm{shape}\ x) \to \forall (n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \alpha ), \mathrm{shape} (\mathrm{back}^{}[n] x) = \mathrm{shape}\ x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L482}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_docs\_round\_trip\_reflected}}
textbook\_workspace\_docs\_round\_trip reflected by its shape (examples: google-workspace-docs, google-workspace-docs-lost)
\[ \forall \{\alpha : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_1}\} \{\beta : \mathrm{Type}\ \mathrm{u\_2}\} (\mathrm{shape} : \alpha \to \beta ) (\mathrm{back} : \alpha \to \alpha ), (\forall (x : \alpha ), \mathrm{shape} (\mathrm{back}\ x) = \mathrm{shape}\ x) \to \forall (n : \mathbb{N}) (x : \alpha ), \mathrm{shape}\ x = \mathrm{shape} (\mathrm{back}^{}[n] x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L487}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_drive}}
a field-for-field correspondence both ways is an equivalence, so a document mapped to a Drive File and back is the document. From Mathlib's `Equiv.symm\_apply\_apply` (examples: google-workspace-drive, google-workspace-drive-answers)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (e : \alpha \simeq \beta ) (x : \alpha ), \mathrm{e.symm} (e\ x) = x \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L496}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_drive\_reflected}}
textbook\_workspace\_drive reflected by its shape (examples: google-workspace-drive, google-workspace-drive-answers)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (e : \alpha \simeq \beta ) (x : \alpha ), x = \mathrm{e.symm} (e\ x) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L499}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_round\_trip}}
a read g that the write f undoes (f ∘ g = id) makes every further round trip the identity: (f ∘ g) ∘ (f ∘ g) = id. From Mathlib's `Function.LeftInverse.comp\_eq\_id`, `Function.id\_comp` (examples: google-workspace-form, google-workspace-form-violations, google-workspace-response)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (g : \beta \to \alpha ), \mathrm{Function.LeftInverse}\ f\ g \to (f \circ g) \circ f \circ g = \mathrm{id} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L476}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_round\_trip\_reflected}}
textbook\_workspace\_round\_trip reflected by its shape (examples: google-workspace-form, google-workspace-form-violations, google-workspace-response)
\[ \forall \{\alpha \beta : \mathrm{Type}\} (f : \alpha \to \beta ) (g : \beta \to \alpha ), \mathrm{Function.LeftInverse}\ f\ g \to \mathrm{id} = (f \circ g) \circ f \circ g \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L480}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_sets}}
the family sets are mixed-radix indices, one digit per radix, |Π i, Fin (r i)| = ∏ r i. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_pi`, `Finset.prod\_congr` and `Fintype.card\_fin` (examples: textbook-sets, textbook-set)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}) (r : \mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((i : \mathrm{Fin}\ k) \to \mathrm{Fin} (r\ i)) = \prod i, r\ i \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L93}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_workspace\_sets\_reflected}}
textbook\_workspace\_sets reflected by its shape (examples: textbook-sets, textbook-set)
\[ \forall (k : \mathbb{N}) (r : \mathrm{Fin}\ k \to \mathbb{N}), \prod i, r\ i = \mathrm{Fintype.card} ((i : \mathrm{Fin}\ k) \to \mathrm{Fin} (r\ i)) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L98}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_x25519\_sizes}}
n bytes hold 2\^{}(8n) values: 32 bytes, 256 bits, hold the 2²⁵⁵ − 19 field elements of X25519. From Mathlib's `Fintype.card\_fun`, `Fintype.card\_fin` and `pow\_mul` (examples: seal-x25519)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin} (2 ^{8})) = 2 ^{(8 \cdot n)} \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2388}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_x25519\_sizes\_reflected}}
textbook\_x25519\_sizes reflected by its shape (examples: seal-x25519)
\[ \forall (n : \mathbb{N}), 2 ^{(8 \cdot n)} = \mathrm{Fintype.card} (\mathrm{Fin}\ n \to \mathrm{Fin} (2 ^{8})) \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L2393}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_xeb}}
cross-entropy benchmarking: for D > 1, 2D > D + 1 > D, the inequality by which the random-circuit collision probability 2/(D + 1) exceeds the uniform 1/D (D = 16: 32 > 17 > 16). From Mathlib's `two\_mul`, `add\_lt\_add\_right`, `lt\_add\_one`, `Nat.instAtLeastTwoHAddOfNat`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `Nat.instZeroLEOneClass` (examples: algorithm-xeb)
\[ \forall (D : \mathbb{N}), 1 < D \to D + 1 < 2 \cdot D \land D < D + 1 \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1237}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_xeb\_contrapositive}}
the contrapositive of textbook\_xeb (examples: algorithm-xeb)
\[ \forall (D : \mathbb{N}), \neg (D + 1 < 2 \cdot D \land D < D + 1) \to \neg 1 < D \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1245}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_xeb\_converse}}
the converse of textbook\_xeb, proved. From Mathlib's `add\_lt\_add\_iff\_left`, `IsLeftCancelAdd.addLeftStrictMono\_of\_addLeftMono`, `instIsLeftCancelAddOfAddLeftReflectLE`, `IsOrderedCancelAddMonoid.toAddLeftReflectLE`, `Nat.instIsOrderedCancelAddMonoid`, `IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono`, `Nat.instIsOrderedAddMonoid`, `contravariant\_lt\_of\_covariant\_le`, `Nat.instAtLeastTwoHAddOfNat` and `two\_mul` (examples: algorithm-xeb)
\[ \forall (D : \mathbb{N}), D + 1 < 2 \cdot D \land D < D + 1 \to 1 < D \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1247}

\paragraph{\texttt{textbook\_xeb\_reflected}}
textbook\_xeb reflected by its shape (examples: algorithm-xeb)
\[ \forall (D : \mathbb{N}), 1 < D \to \forall (\sigma : \mathbb{N}), 2 \cdot D \le \sigma \to D + 1 \le \sigma \to \sigma - 2 \cdot D < \sigma - (D + 1) \land \sigma - (D + 1) < \sigma - D \]
\noindent axioms: \texttt{propext}; proof: \url{https://github.com/uuidna/qpu/blob/v1.1.1/lean/Qpu/General/Textbook.lean\#L1243}
